=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/cleanup/bvcg_repack.rs ===
use crate::cleanup::slide_absorb::{
    absorb_redundant_wires_into_resistors, init_cleanup_board, merge_dogleg_wires,
    push_vertical_specials_to_edge, restore_spec_coords, snapshot_spec_coords,
    try_slide_pin_at_hole,
};
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, track_index, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::BoardLayout;
use crate::routing::h_route::FastWire;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
struct VcgWireMeta {
    w_idx: usize,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    c_min: u8,
    c_max: u8,
    span: u8,
    vcg_depth: usize,
    has_groove_anchor: bool,
    has_outer_anchor: bool,
}

fn eval_half_planar_cost(
    wires: &[FastWire],
    h_indices: &[usize],
    h: u8,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> f64 {
    let mut cost = 0.0;
    for i in 0..h_indices.len() {
        let wi = wires[h_indices[i]];
        let ri = wi.r1;
        let (l1, r1) = (wi.c1.min(wi.c2), wi.c1.max(wi.c2));
        let dist_i = if h == 0 { 4 - ri } else { ri - 5 };
        if wi.lane != 0 {
            cost += weights.bvcg_gap_lane_pen;
        }
        if ri == 0 || ri == 9 {
            cost += weights.bvcg_outer_row_pen;
        }
        if ri == 4 || ri == 5 {
            cost += weights.bvcg_groove_row_pen;
        }
        for &wj_idx in &h_indices[(i + 1)..] {
            let wj = wires[wj_idx];
            let rj = wj.r1;
            let (l2, r2) = (wj.c1.min(wj.c2), wj.c1.max(wj.c2));
            let dist_j = if h == 0 { 4 - rj } else { rj - 5 };
            if (l2 <= l1 && r1 <= r2 && (r2 - l2) > (r1 - l1) && dist_j < dist_i)
                || (l1 <= l2 && r2 <= r1 && (r1 - l1) > (r2 - l2) && dist_i < dist_j)
            {
                cost += weights.bvcg_inversion_pen;
            }
            if wi.net == wj.net && (l1 == r2 || r1 == l2 || l1 == l2 || r1 == r2) {
                cost += ((ri - rj).abs() as f64) * weights.bvcg_step_row_weight;
            }
        }
    }
    cost
}

/// PHÉP 4: Tối ưu Đồ thị Ràng buộc Dọc (B-VCG) + Left-Edge Track Repacking trên từng nửa bo
pub fn repack_half_planar_bvcg(
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    wires: &mut Vec<FastWire>,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) {
    for h in [0u8, 1u8] {
        let h_indices: Vec<usize> = wires
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter(|(_, w)| {
                w.r1 == w.r2 && w.c1 != w.c2 && !is_rail_row(w.r1) && half_of(w.r1) == h
            })
            .map(|(idx, _)| idx)
            .collect();
        if h_indices.len() < 2 {
            continue;
        }

        let cost_before = eval_half_planar_cost(wires, &h_indices, h, weights);
        let snap_wires = wires.clone();
        let snap_spec = snapshot_spec_coords(layout);
        let snap_board = *board;

        for &w_idx in &h_indices {
            let w = wires[w_idx];
            if let Some(tid) = track_index(w.r1, w.lane) {
                board.unoccupy_segment(tid, w.c1, w.c2);
            }
            board.unoccupy(w.c1, w.r1);
            board.unoccupy(w.c2, w.r1);
        }

        let groove_r = if h == 0 { 4 } else { 5 };
        let outer_r = if h == 0 { 0 } else { 9 };
        let mut meta: Vec<VcgWireMeta> = h_indices
            .iter()
            .map(|&w_idx| {
                let w = wires[w_idx];
                let (c1, c2) = (w.c1, w.c2);
                let (c_min, c_max) = (c1.min(c2), c1.max(c2));
                let span = c_max - c_min;
                let vcg_depth = h_indices
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|&&o_idx| {
                        if o_idx == w_idx {
                            return false;
                        }
                        let ow = wires[o_idx];
                        let (o_min, o_max) = (ow.c1.min(ow.c2), ow.c1.max(ow.c2));
                        o_min >= c_min && o_max <= c_max && (o_max - o_min) < span
                    })
                    .count();
                VcgWireMeta {
                    w_idx,
                    c1,
                    c2,
                    c_min,
                    c_max,
                    span,
                    vcg_depth,
                    has_groove_anchor: board.is_occupied(c1, groove_r)
                        || board.is_occupied(c2, groove_r),
                    has_outer_anchor: board.is_occupied(c1, outer_r)
                        || board.is_occupied(c2, outer_r),
                }
            })
            .collect();

        meta.sort_by_key(|m| {
            let tier = if m.has_outer_anchor && !m.has_groove_anchor {
                2
            } else {
                m.vcg_depth.min(2)
            };
            (tier, m.span, m.c_min)
        });

        let mut all_assigned = true;
        for m in &meta {
            let row_order = if m.has_outer_anchor && !m.has_groove_anchor {
                if h == 0 { [1i8, 2, 3, 0, 4] } else { [8i8, 7, 6, 9, 5] }
            } else if m.vcg_depth == 0 && m.span <= 8 {
                if h == 0 { [3i8, 2, 1, 0, 4] } else { [6i8, 7, 8, 9, 5] }
            } else if m.vcg_depth <= 1 && m.span <= 16 {
                if h == 0 { [2i8, 3, 1, 0, 4] } else { [7i8, 6, 8, 9, 5] }
            } else if h == 0 {
                [1i8, 2, 3, 0, 4]
            } else {
                [8i8, 7, 6, 9, 5]
            };

            let mut placed = false;
            for allow_gap_lane in [false, true] {
                if placed {
                    break;
                }
                for r_try in row_order {
                    let lanes: &[i8] = if !allow_gap_lane {
                        &[0]
                    } else if r_try == 4 {
                        &[-1, 1]
                    } else {
                        &[1, -1]
                    };
                    let Some((ln, tid)) = lanes.iter().find_map(|&ln| {
                        let tid = track_index(r_try, ln)?;
                        board.is_segment_free(tid, m.c_min, m.c_max).then_some((ln, tid))
                    }) else {
                        continue;
                    };

                    let pin_snap = snapshot_spec_coords(layout);
                    let b_local = *board;
                    let f1 = !board.is_occupied(m.c1, r_try)
                        || try_slide_pin_at_hole(layout, board, m.c1, r_try, h, weights);
                    let f2 = f1
                        && (!board.is_occupied(m.c2, r_try)
                            || try_slide_pin_at_hole(layout, board, m.c2, r_try, h, weights));
                    if f1 && f2 {
                        board.occupy(m.c1, r_try);
                        board.occupy(m.c2, r_try);
                        board.occupy_segment(tid, m.c_min, m.c_max);
                        let net = wires[m.w_idx].net;
                        wires[m.w_idx] = FastWire::new_h(net, m.c1, m.c2, r_try, ln, tid);
                        placed = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    *board = b_local;
                    restore_spec_coords(layout, &pin_snap);
                }
            }
            if !placed {
                all_assigned = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        let cost_after = if all_assigned {
            eval_half_planar_cost(wires, &h_indices, h, weights)
        } else {
            1e9
        };
        if !all_assigned || cost_after > cost_before {
            *wires = snap_wires;
            restore_spec_coords(layout, &snap_spec);
            *board = snap_board;
        }
    }
}

/// Thực thi trọn vẹn 4 Phép Hậu kiểm Chiến thuật (`DiscreteEngine.runQuiescenceCleanup` - chỉ chạy khi đạt 0 lỗi)
pub fn run_quiescence_cleanup(
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    wires: &mut Vec<FastWire>,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) {
    let mut board = init_cleanup_board(layout, wires);
    push_vertical_specials_to_edge(layout, &mut board, weights);
    merge_dogleg_wires(layout, wires, &mut board, weights);
    absorb_redundant_wires_into_resistors(layout, wires, &mut board, weights);
    repack_half_planar_bvcg(layout, wires, &mut board, weights);
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::circuit::PlacedSpecial;
    use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId};

    #[test]
    fn test_full_quiescence_cleanup_all_4_passes() {
        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.leds.push(PlacedSpecial {
            cid: CompId(1),
            id: "L1".into(),
            comp_type: CompType::LED,
            c1: 5,
            r1: -2,
            c2: 5,
            r2: 2,
            locked: false,
            ic_id: None,
        });
        layout.rs.push(PlacedSpecial {
            cid: CompId(2),
            id: "R1".into(),
            comp_type: CompType::R,
            c1: 20,
            r1: 2,
            c2: 25,
            r2: 2,
            locked: false,
            ic_id: None,
        });

        let mut wires = vec![
            // Dogleg (10,3)->(14,3) và (14,2)->(18,2) -> Gộp thành 1 dây (10..18)
            FastWire::new_h(NetId(3), 10, 14, 3, 0, 3),
            FastWire::new_h(NetId(3), 14, 18, 2, 0, 2),
            // Dây nối thừa (25,1)->(28,1) -> Hút vào chân R1 tại hàng cố định 2: (20,2)->(28,2)
            FastWire::new_h(NetId(4), 25, 28, 1, 0, 1),
            // Dây ngắn (12..16) bị đặt ở hàng 1 (ngoài) trong khi dây dài (10..18) chiếm hàng 3 (sát IC) -> Phép 4 B-VCG đảo lại!
            FastWire::new_h(NetId(5), 12, 16, 1, 0, 1),
        ];

        run_quiescence_cleanup(&mut layout, &mut wires, &weights);

        assert_eq!(layout.leds[0].r2, 0); // Phép 1: L1 đẩy sát mép hàng 0
        assert_eq!((layout.rs[0].c1, layout.rs[0].c2), (20, 28)); // Phép 3: R1 hút dây thừa
        assert_eq!((layout.rs[0].r1, layout.rs[0].r2), (2, 2));   // Giữ nguyên hàng cố định 2
        assert_eq!(wires.len(), 2); // Phép 2 + 3: 4 dây ban đầu giảm còn 2 dây

        // Phép 4 (B-VCG): Dây ngắn (12..16) được xếp vào hàng 3 (sát rãnh IC hơn dây dài 10..18 ở hàng 2)
        let w_long = wires.iter().find(|w| w.c1 == 10 && w.c2 == 18).unwrap();
        let w_short = wires.iter().find(|w| w.c1 == 12 && w.c2 == 16).unwrap();
        assert!(w_short.r1 > w_long.r1);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/cleanup/slide_absorb.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::{CostWeights, SpecialSpan, SPECIAL_R};
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, track_index, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, PlacedSpecial};
use crate::model::geometry::{seg_dist_sq, vis_y};
use crate::model::ids::CompId;
use crate::routing::h_route::{FastWire, NO_TRACK};

pub type SpecCoordSnap = (CompId, u8, i8, u8, i8);

/// Khởi tạo `BoardState` sạch trên Stack phục vụ hậu kiểm (`runQuiescenceCleanup` trong discrete-engine.js)
pub fn init_cleanup_board(layout: &BoardLayout, wires: &mut [FastWire]) -> BoardState {
    let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
    let mut fps = Vec::new();
    layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);
    for w in wires.iter_mut() {
        board.occupy(w.c1, w.r1);
        board.occupy(w.c2, w.r2);
        if w.r1 == w.r2 && w.c1 != w.c2 && !is_rail_row(w.r1) {
            if let Some(tid) = track_index(w.r1, w.lane) {
                board.occupy_segment(tid, w.c1, w.c2);
                w.trk = tid as u8;
            }
        }
    }
    board
}

pub fn snapshot_spec_coords(layout: &BoardLayout) -> Vec<SpecCoordSnap> {
    layout.cs.iter().chain(&layout.leds).chain(&layout.rs).map(|s| (s.cid, s.c1, s.r1, s.c2, s.r2)).collect()
}

pub fn restore_spec_coords(layout: &mut BoardLayout, snap: &[SpecCoordSnap]) {
    for (s, &(_, c1, r1, c2, r2)) in layout.cs.iter_mut().chain(&mut layout.leds).chain(&mut layout.rs).zip(snap) {
        s.c1 = c1;
        s.r1 = r1;
        s.c2 = c2;
        s.r2 = r2;
    }
}

/// Kiểm tra dịch chuyển chân linh kiện rời hợp lệ (Hạng mục #1: tính `dy` qua `vis_y` khi 1 chân ở thanh nguồn)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn can_move_spec_pin(
    layout: &BoardLayout,
    target_cid: CompId,
    target_locked: bool,
    sp: SpecialSpan,
    new_c1: u8,
    new_r1: i8,
    new_c2: u8,
    new_r2: i8,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> bool {
    if target_locked {
        return false;
    }
    let one_rail = is_rail_row(new_r1) != is_rail_row(new_r2);
    let dy = if one_rail {
        (vis_y(new_r1) - vis_y(new_r2)).abs()
    } else {
        (new_r1 - new_r2).abs() as f64
    };
    let d = ((new_c1 as f64) - (new_c2 as f64)).hypot(dy);
    if d < sp.min || d > sp.max {
        return false;
    }
    let (p1, p2) = ([new_c1 as f64, vis_y(new_r1)], [new_c2 as f64, vis_y(new_r2)]);
    !layout.cs.iter().chain(&layout.leds).chain(&layout.rs).any(|obj| {
        obj.cid != target_cid
            && !obj.locked
            && seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, [obj.c1 as f64, vis_y(obj.r1)], [obj.c2 as f64, vis_y(obj.r2)])
                < weights.spec_body_clearance_sq
    })
}

fn get_spec_mut(layout: &mut BoardLayout, idx: usize) -> Option<&mut PlacedSpecial> {
    let (cs_len, leds_len) = (layout.cs.len(), layout.leds.len());
    if idx < cs_len {
        Some(&mut layout.cs[idx])
    } else if idx < cs_len + leds_len {
        Some(&mut layout.leds[idx - cs_len])
    } else {
        layout.rs.get_mut(idx - cs_len - leds_len)
    }
}

/// PHÉP 1: Đẩy Tụ lọc nhánh (không khóa), LED và Trở nguồn thẳng đứng về sát mép Hàng 0 / Hàng 9
pub fn push_vertical_specials_to_edge(layout: &mut BoardLayout, board: &mut BoardState, weights: &CostWeights) {
    let total = layout.cs.len() + layout.leds.len() + layout.rs.len();
    for idx in 0..total {
        let obj = get_spec_mut(layout, idx).unwrap();
        let (cid, locked, comp_type, c1, r1, c2, r2) = (obj.cid, obj.locked, obj.comp_type, obj.c1, obj.r1, obj.c2, obj.r2);
        if locked || c1 != c2 {
            continue;
        }
        let sp = SpecialSpan::for_type(comp_type);
        if is_rail_row(r1) && !is_rail_row(r2) {
            let tr = if r2 <= 4 { 0 } else { 9 };
            if r2 != tr && !board.is_occupied(c1, tr) && can_move_spec_pin(layout, cid, locked, sp, c1, r1, c1, tr, weights) {
                board.unoccupy(c1, r2);
                board.occupy(c1, tr);
                get_spec_mut(layout, idx).unwrap().r2 = tr;
            }
        } else if is_rail_row(r2) && !is_rail_row(r1) {
            let tr = if r1 <= 4 { 0 } else { 9 };
            if r1 != tr && !board.is_occupied(c1, tr) && can_move_spec_pin(layout, cid, locked, sp, c1, tr, c1, r2, weights) {
                board.unoccupy(c1, r1);
                board.occupy(c1, tr);
                get_spec_mut(layout, idx).unwrap().r1 = tr;
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Trượt chân R/C/LED tại lỗ `(col, row)` sang hàng liền kề trên cùng nửa `h`
/// Quy tắc Cứng #5: Chỉ cho phép trượt chân linh kiện rời tối đa 1 hàng liền kề (`(r_alt - row).abs() <= 1`)!
pub fn try_slide_pin_at_hole(
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    col: u8,
    row: i8,
    h: u8,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> bool {
    let Some((idx, cid, comp_type, is_pin1, other_c, other_r)) = layout
        .cs.iter().chain(&layout.leds).chain(&layout.rs).enumerate()
        .find(|(_, o)| !o.locked && ((o.c1 == col && o.r1 == row) || (o.c2 == col && o.r2 == row)))
        .map(|(i, o)| {
            let p1 = o.c1 == col && o.r1 == row;
            (i, o.cid, o.comp_type, p1, if p1 { o.c2 } else { o.c1 }, if p1 { o.r2 } else { o.r1 })
        })
    else {
        return false;
    };
    let sp = SpecialSpan::for_type(comp_type);
    let other_is_rail = is_rail_row(other_r);
    let mut cand_rows = if h == 0 { [2i8, 1, 0, 3, 4] } else { [7i8, 8, 9, 6, 5] };
    cand_rows.sort_by(|&ra, &rb| {
        let dya = if other_is_rail { (vis_y(ra) - vis_y(other_r)).abs() } else { (ra - other_r).abs() as f64 };
        let dyb = if other_is_rail { (vis_y(rb) - vis_y(other_r)).abs() } else { (rb - other_r).abs() as f64 };
        let da = ((col as f64) - (other_c as f64)).hypot(dya) + if col == other_c || ra == other_r { -5.0 } else { 0.0 };
        let db = ((col as f64) - (other_c as f64)).hypot(dyb) + if col == other_c || rb == other_r { -5.0 } else { 0.0 };
        da.total_cmp(&db)
    });
    let was_ortho = col == other_c || row == other_r;
    for r_alt in cand_rows {
        if r_alt == row || (r_alt - row).abs() > 1 || board.is_occupied(col, r_alt) {
            continue;
        }
        let (nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2) = if is_pin1 { (col, r_alt, other_c, other_r) } else { (other_c, other_r, col, r_alt) };
        let is_ortho_after = nc1 == nc2 || nr1 == nr2;
        if (!was_ortho || is_ortho_after) && can_move_spec_pin(layout, cid, false, sp, nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2, weights) {
            board.unoccupy(col, row);
            board.occupy(col, r_alt);
            let obj = get_spec_mut(layout, idx).unwrap();
            if is_pin1 { obj.r1 = r_alt; } else { obj.r2 = r_alt; }
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}

/// PHÉP 2: Gộp 2 dây ngang gấp khúc Dogleg cùng mạng thành 1 dây thẳng (`mergeAny`)
pub fn merge_dogleg_wires(layout: &mut BoardLayout, wires: &mut Vec<FastWire>, board: &mut BoardState, weights: &CostWeights) {
    let mut merged_any = true;
    while merged_any {
        merged_any = false;
        let mut i = 0usize;
        while i < wires.len() && !merged_any {
            let w1 = wires[i];
            if w1.r1 != w1.r2 || w1.c1 == w1.c2 || is_rail_row(w1.r1) {
                i += 1;
                continue;
            }
            let h = half_of(w1.r1);
            let mut j = i + 1;
            while j < wires.len() && !merged_any {
                let w2 = wires[j];
                if w2.net != w1.net || w2.r1 != w2.r2 || w2.c1 == w2.c2 || is_rail_row(w2.r1) || half_of(w2.r1) != h {
                    j += 1;
                    continue;
                }
                let shared = if w1.c1 == w2.c1 { Some((w1.c1, w1.c2, w2.c2)) }
                    else if w1.c1 == w2.c2 { Some((w1.c1, w1.c2, w2.c1)) }
                    else if w1.c2 == w2.c1 { Some((w1.c2, w1.c1, w2.c2)) }
                    else if w1.c2 == w2.c2 { Some((w1.c2, w1.c1, w2.c1)) }
                    else { None };
                let Some((cm, c1, c2)) = shared.filter(|&(_, a, b)| a != b) else { j += 1; continue; };
                if 5 - board.free_hole_count(cm, h) != 2 { j += 1; continue; }

                let (r1, r2) = (w1.r1, w2.r1);
                if let Some(t1) = track_index(r1, w1.lane) { board.unoccupy_segment(t1, w1.c1, w1.c2); }
                if let Some(t2) = track_index(r2, w2.lane) { board.unoccupy_segment(t2, w2.c1, w2.c2); }
                board.unoccupy(w1.c1, r1); board.unoccupy(w1.c2, r1);
                board.unoccupy(w2.c1, r2); board.unoccupy(w2.c2, r2);

                let base_rows = if h == 0 { [3i8, 2, 1, 0] } else { [6i8, 7, 8, 9] };
                let (mut try_rows, mut tr_len) = ([0i8; 6], 0usize);
                for &cr in [r1, r2].iter().chain(&base_rows) {
                    if !try_rows[..tr_len].contains(&cr) { try_rows[tr_len] = cr; tr_len += 1; }
                }
                let mut done = false;
                for &r_try in &try_rows[..tr_len] {
                    let Some((lane, tid)) = board.get_available_track_for_row(r_try, c1, c2) else { continue; };
                    let (snap_b, snap_pins) = (*board, snapshot_spec_coords(layout));
                    let f1 = !board.is_occupied(c1, r_try) || try_slide_pin_at_hole(layout, board, c1, r_try, h, weights);
                    let f2 = f1 && (!board.is_occupied(c2, r_try) || try_slide_pin_at_hole(layout, board, c2, r_try, h, weights));
                    if f1 && f2 {
                        board.occupy(c1, r_try); board.occupy(c2, r_try);
                        board.occupy_segment(tid, c1, c2);
                        wires[i] = FastWire::new_h(w1.net, c1, c2, r_try, lane, tid);
                        wires.remove(j);
                        done = true; merged_any = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    *board = snap_b;
                    restore_spec_coords(layout, &snap_pins);
                }
                if !done {
                    board.occupy(w1.c1, r1); board.occupy(w1.c2, r1);
                    board.occupy(w2.c1, r2); board.occupy(w2.c2, r2);
                    if let Some(t1) = track_index(r1, w1.lane) { board.occupy_segment(t1, w1.c1, w1.c2); }
                    if let Some(t2) = track_index(r2, w2.lane) { board.occupy_segment(t2, w2.c1, w2.c2); }
                }
                j += 1;
            }
            i += 1;
        }
    }
}

/// PHÉP 3: Hút dây nối ngang thừa vào chân Điện trở (`L.rs`)
pub fn absorb_redundant_wires_into_resistors(layout: &mut BoardLayout, wires: &mut Vec<FastWire>, board: &mut BoardState, weights: &CostWeights) {
    for i in (0..wires.len()).rev() {
        let w = wires[i];
        if w.net.is_power() || w.r1 != w.r2 || w.c1 == w.c2 || is_rail_row(w.r1) { continue; }
        let (r_w, h) = (w.r1, half_of(w.r1));
        for (c_relay, c_dest) in [(w.c1, w.c2), (w.c2, w.c1)] {
            if 5 - board.free_hole_count(c_relay, h) != 2 { continue; }
            let Some(r_idx) = layout.rs.iter().position(|rx| {
                !rx.locked && ((rx.c1 == c_relay && half_of(rx.r1) == h) || (rx.c2 == c_relay && half_of(rx.r2) == h))
            }) else { continue; };
            let rx = &layout.rs[r_idx];
            let (rx_cid, move_pin1) = (rx.cid, rx.c1 == c_relay && half_of(rx.r1) == h);
            let (fixed_c, fixed_r) = if move_pin1 { (rx.c2, rx.r2) } else { (rx.c1, rx.r1) };
            let old_r = if move_pin1 { rx.r1 } else { rx.r2 };
            let span = (c_dest as i32 - fixed_c as i32).abs();
            if is_rail_row(fixed_r) || half_of(fixed_r) != h || !(3..=12).contains(&span) { continue; }
            let cand_rows: &[i8] = if fixed_r == r_w { &[r_w] } else { &[fixed_r, r_w] };
            let mut absorbed = false;
            for &t_r in cand_rows {
                if (t_r != r_w && board.is_occupied(c_dest, t_r)) || (t_r != fixed_r && board.is_occupied(fixed_c, t_r)) {
                    continue;
                }
                let (nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2) = if move_pin1 { (c_dest, t_r, fixed_c, t_r) } else { (fixed_c, t_r, c_dest, t_r) };
                if can_move_spec_pin(layout, rx_cid, false, SPECIAL_R, nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2, weights) {
                    if let Some(tid) = track_index(r_w, w.lane).or_else(|| (w.trk != NO_TRACK).then_some(w.trk as usize)) {
                        board.unoccupy_segment(tid, w.c1, w.c2);
                    }
                    board.unoccupy(w.c1, r_w); board.unoccupy(w.c2, r_w); board.unoccupy(c_relay, old_r);
                    if t_r != fixed_r { board.unoccupy(fixed_c, fixed_r); board.occupy(fixed_c, t_r); }
                    board.occupy(c_dest, t_r);
                    let r_mut = &mut layout.rs[r_idx];
                    (r_mut.c1, r_mut.r1, r_mut.c2, r_mut.r2) = (nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2);
                    wires.remove(i);
                    absorbed = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if absorbed { break; }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::ids::CompType;

    #[test]
    fn test_vis_y_pullup_resistor_and_hard_1row_slide_rule() {
        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        // Điện trở kéo nguồn R1 cắm dọc từ thanh nguồn VCC (-1) xuống hàng C (row = 2)
        layout.rs.push(PlacedSpecial {
            cid: CompId(1),
            id: "R1".into(),
            comp_type: CompType::R,
            c1: 10,
            r1: -1,
            c2: 10,
            r2: 2,
            locked: false,
            ic_id: None,
        });
        let mut wires = Vec::new();
        let mut board = init_cleanup_board(&layout, &mut wires);

        // Hạng mục #1: Nhờ dy = |vis_y(-1) - vis_y(0)| = 3.0 >= SPECIAL_R.min (3.0), R1 đẩy sát mép hàng A (row = 0)
        push_vertical_specials_to_edge(&mut layout, &mut board, &weights);
        assert_eq!(layout.rs[0].r2, 0);

        // Quy tắc Cứng #5: Khóa hàng 1 (hàng liền kề duy nhất của row = 0), chỉ mở hàng 2, 3, 4 -> Cấm trượt quá 1 hàng!
        board.occupy(10, 1);
        assert!(!try_slide_pin_at_hole(&mut layout, &mut board, 10, 0, 0, &weights));
        assert_eq!(layout.rs[0].r2, 0);

        // Khi mở hàng 1 (|1 - 0| == 1), cho phép trượt chân R1 từ hàng 0 sang hàng 1
        board.unoccupy(10, 1);
        assert!(try_slide_pin_at_hole(&mut layout, &mut board, 10, 0, 0, &weights));
        assert_eq!(layout.rs[0].r2, 1);
    }
}

use crate::model::ids::CompType;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Giới hạn sải chân (đơn vị: ô lỗ cắm) cho R, LED, C, CC
/// Đã giảm ~25% sải chân tối đa của R (18.0 -> 13.5) và LED (12.0 -> 9.0) theo Đề xuất #4
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SpecialSpan {
    pub min: f64,
    pub max: f64,
    pub pref: f64,
}

pub const SPECIAL_R: SpecialSpan = SpecialSpan { min: 3.0, max: 13.5, pref: 4.5 };
pub const SPECIAL_LED: SpecialSpan = SpecialSpan { min: 1.0, max: 9.0, pref: 2.5 };
pub const SPECIAL_C: SpecialSpan = SpecialSpan { min: 1.0, max: 6.0, pref: 2.0 };
pub const SPECIAL_CC: SpecialSpan = SpecialSpan { min: 1.0, max: 6.0, pref: 2.0 };

impl SpecialSpan {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn for_type(t: CompType) -> Self {
        match t {
            CompType::R => SPECIAL_R,
            CompType::LED => SPECIAL_LED,
            CompType::C => SPECIAL_C,
            CompType::CC => SPECIAL_CC,
            _ => SPECIAL_R,
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/discrete_place/decoupling.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::discrete_place::pin_swap::optimize_symmetric_pins;
use crate::macro_place::topology::TopologyCache;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, CompSpec, ParsedNetlist, PlacedSpecial};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn pwr_aux_tag(net: NetId, half: u8) -> u16 {
    0x4000 | (((net.0 == 2) as u16) << 8) | ((half & 1) as u16)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn pwr_tied_tag(net: NetId, cid: CompId, half: u8) -> u16 {
    0x5000 | (((net.0 == 2) as u16) << 9) | (((half & 1) as u16) << 8) | (cid.0 as u16)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn pwr_direct_tag(net: NetId, col: u8, half: u8) -> u16 {
    0x6000 | (((net.0 == 2) as u16) << 9) | (((half & 1) as u16) << 8) | (col as u16)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn pwr_main_tag(net: NetId, cid: CompId, pin: u8) -> u16 {
    0x8000 | (((net.0 == 2) as u16) << 13) | (((cid.0 & 0x7F) as u16) << 6) | ((pin & 0x3F) as u16)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn nc_tag(cid: CompId, pin: u8) -> u16 {
    0xC000 | ((cid.0 as u16) << 6) | ((pin & 0x3F) as u16)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn is_pwr_tag(tag: u16) -> bool {
    tag == 1 || tag == 2 || (tag >= 0x4000 && tag < 0xC000)
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn is_pwr_tied_tag(tag: u16) -> bool {
    tag >= 0x5000 && tag < 0x6000
}

#[inline(always)]
pub const fn base_net_of(tag: u16) -> NetId {
    if tag == 1 || tag == 2 {
        NetId(tag)
    } else if tag >= 0x4000 && tag < 0x5000 {
        if (tag & 0x0100) != 0 { NetId::GND } else { NetId::VCC }
    } else if tag >= 0x5000 && tag < 0x8000 {
        if (tag & 0x0200) != 0 { NetId::GND } else { NetId::VCC }
    } else if tag >= 0x8000 && tag < 0xC000 {
        if (tag & 0x2000) != 0 { NetId::GND } else { NetId::VCC }
    } else if tag >= 3 && tag < 0x4000 {
        NetId(tag)
    } else {
        NetId::NONE
    }
}

#[inline]
pub fn pwr_tag(
    net: NetId,
    cid: CompId,
    pin: u8,
    row: i8,
    comp_type: CompType,
    main_ic_pwr_pins: &FxHashSet<PinRef>,
) -> u16 {
    if !net.is_power() {
        return net.0;
    }
    if comp_type == CompType::IC {
        if main_ic_pwr_pins.contains(&PinRef { comp: cid, pin }) {
            return pwr_main_tag(net, cid, pin);
        }
        return pwr_tied_tag(net, cid, half_of(row));
    }
    pwr_aux_tag(net, half_of(row))
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct HolePin {
    pub col: u8,
    pub row: i8,
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub pin: u8,
    pub tag: u16,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct DiscreteContext {
    pub net_of: FxHashMap<PinRef, NetId>,
    pub col_own: [[Option<CompId>; 64]; 2],
    pub pres: FxHashMap<NetId, [u64; 2]>,
    pub holes: FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<HolePin>>,
    pub main_ic_pwr_pins: FxHashSet<PinRef>,
    pub invalid_comp_ids: FxHashSet<CompId>,
    pub btn_bridges: Vec<((u8, u8), (u8, u8))>,
    pub locked_ic_caps: Vec<PlacedSpecial>,
    pub locked_decoupling_ids: FxHashSet<CompId>,
}

impl Default for DiscreteContext {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            net_of: FxHashMap::default(),
            col_own: [[None; 64]; 2],
            pres: FxHashMap::default(),
            holes: FxHashMap::default(),
            main_ic_pwr_pins: FxHashSet::default(),
            invalid_comp_ids: FxHashSet::default(),
            btn_bridges: Vec::new(),
            locked_ic_caps: Vec::new(),
            locked_decoupling_ids: FxHashSet::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl DiscreteContext {
    pub fn add_pin(
        &mut self,
        board: &mut BoardState,
        cid: CompId,
        pin: u8,
        col: u8,
        row: i8,
        comps: &[CompSpec],
        parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
        errors: &mut Vec<String>,
    ) {
        let Some(&net) = self.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: cid, pin }) else { return };
        if is_rail_row(row) {
            return;
        }
        let h = half_of(row) as usize;
        let cu = col as usize;
        let c_obj = comps.iter().find(|c| c.cid == cid);
        let c_type = c_obj.map_or(CompType::R, |c| c.comp_type);
        let tag = pwr_tag(net, cid, pin, row, c_type, &self.main_ic_pwr_pins);

        let cur_tag = board.col_net[h][cu];
        if cur_tag != 0 && cur_tag != tag {
            let prev_base = base_net_of(cur_tag);
            // Cho phép Tụ điện, Điện trở nguồn hoặc chân nguồn của SW/BTN2 dùng chung cột nguồn cùng mạng (VCC chung với VCC, GND chung với GND) với chân IC
            if !(net.is_power() && prev_base == net) {
                let comp_name = c_obj.map_or("?", |c| c.id.as_str());
                let net_name = parsed.nets.iter().find(|n| n.id == net).map_or("?", |n| n.name.as_str());
                let owner_name = self.col_own[h][cu]
                    .and_then(|oc| comps.iter().find(|x| x.cid == oc))
                    .map_or("nguồn", |x| x.id.as_str());
                errors.push(format!(
                    "{comp_name}:{pin} (net {net_name}) chung cột {col} với {owner_name} -> ngắn mạch hoặc vi phạm trung chuyển nguồn"
                ));
            }
        }
        if board.col_net[h][cu] == 0 {
            board.col_net[h][cu] = tag;
        }
        if self.col_own[h][cu].is_none() {
            self.col_own[h][cu] = Some(cid);
        }
        if !net.is_power() {
            self.pres.entry(net).or_insert([0u64; 2])[h] |= 1u64 << col;
        }
        let final_tag = board.col_net[h][cu];
        self.holes.entry(net).or_default().push(HolePin { col, row, cid, pin, tag: final_tag });
    }
}

fn split_id_num(s: &str) -> (&str, u32) {
    let idx = s.find(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(s.len());
    (&s[..idx], s[idx..].parse::<u32>().unwrap_or(0))
}

fn natural_id_cmp(a: &str, b: &str) -> core::cmp::Ordering {
    split_id_num(a).cmp(&split_id_num(b))
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn lock_cap_across_ic(
    cap: &CompSpec,
    ic_cid: CompId,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    ctx: &mut DiscreteContext,
    decoupled_ic_set: &mut FxHashSet<CompId>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
) {
    let Some(ic_comp) = comps.iter().find(|x| x.cid == ic_cid) else { return };
    let (Some(p_v), Some(p_g)) = ic_comp.get_ic_pwr_pins() else { return };
    let (Some(h_v), Some(h_g)) = (layout.pin_hole(ic_cid, p_v), layout.pin_hole(ic_cid, p_g)) else { return };
    let r_adj_v = if h_v.1 == 4 { 3 } else { 6 };
    let r_adj_g = if h_g.1 == 4 { 3 } else { 6 };
    if board.is_occupied(h_v.0, r_adj_v) || board.is_occupied(h_g.0, r_adj_g) {
        return;
    }
    let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: cap.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
    let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: cap.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
    let rev_pins = n1 == Some(NetId::GND) && n2 == Some(NetId::VCC);
    let (c1, r1) = if rev_pins { (h_g.0, r_adj_g) } else { (h_v.0, r_adj_v) };
    let (c2, r2) = if rev_pins { (h_v.0, r_adj_v) } else { (h_g.0, r_adj_g) };

    board.occupy(c1, r1);
    board.occupy(c2, r2);
    ctx.net_of.entry(PinRef { comp: cap.cid, pin: 1 }).or_insert(if rev_pins { NetId::GND } else { NetId::VCC });
    ctx.net_of.entry(PinRef { comp: cap.cid, pin: 2 }).or_insert(if rev_pins { NetId::VCC } else { NetId::GND });

    let pr_v = PinRef { comp: ic_cid, pin: p_v };
    if let std::collections::hash_map::Entry::Vacant(e) = ctx.net_of.entry(pr_v) {
        e.insert(NetId::VCC);
        board.col_net[half_of(h_v.1) as usize][h_v.0 as usize] = 0;
        ctx.add_pin(board, ic_cid, p_v, h_v.0, h_v.1, comps, parsed, errors);
    }
    let pr_g = PinRef { comp: ic_cid, pin: p_g };
    if let std::collections::hash_map::Entry::Vacant(e) = ctx.net_of.entry(pr_g) {
        e.insert(NetId::GND);
        board.col_net[half_of(h_g.1) as usize][h_g.0 as usize] = 0;
        ctx.add_pin(board, ic_cid, p_g, h_g.0, h_g.1, comps, parsed, errors);
    }

    let locked_obj = PlacedSpecial {
        cid: cap.cid,
        id: cap.id.clone(),
        comp_type: cap.comp_type,
        c1,
        r1,
        c2,
        r2,
        locked: true,
        ic_id: Some(ic_cid),
    };
    ctx.locked_ic_caps.push(locked_obj.clone());
    layout.cs.push(locked_obj);
    ctx.locked_decoupling_ids.insert(cap.cid);
    decoupled_ic_set.insert(ic_cid);
}

/// Thiết lập chân linh kiện Đại cục, đảo chân đối xứng BTN2/SW và Khóa cứng Tụ lọc nguồn IC (Hàng 3 & 6)
pub fn setup_macro_and_decoupling(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    weights: &CostWeights,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
) -> DiscreteContext {
    let mut ctx = DiscreteContext {
        net_of: parsed.net_of.clone(),
        ..Default::default()
    };

    for err_msg in &parsed.errors {
        for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special()) {
            if err_msg.starts_with(&format!("{}:", c.id))
                || err_msg.starts_with(&format!("LED {} ", c.id))
                || err_msg.starts_with(&format!("Tụ phân cực {} ", c.id))
            {
                ctx.invalid_comp_ids.insert(c.cid);
            }
        }
    }

    let placed_ids: FxHashSet<CompId> = layout
        .ics.iter().map(|x| x.cid)
        .chain(layout.sws.iter().map(|x| x.cid))
        .chain(layout.btns.iter().map(|x| x.cid))
        .collect();

    for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC) {
        let (p_v, p_g) = c.get_ic_pwr_pins();
        if c.custom_pwr {
            if p_v.is_none() || p_g.is_none() {
                errors.push(format!("{}: chân VCC/GND tùy chỉnh phải nằm trong khoảng 1..{}", c.id, c.pins));
            } else if p_v == p_g {
                errors.push(format!("{}: chân VCC và GND không được trùng nhau (chân {})", c.id, p_v.unwrap()));
            }
        }
        if let Some(pv) = p_v {
            let pr = PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: pv };
            ctx.main_ic_pwr_pins.insert(pr);
            if let Some(&nv) = ctx.net_of.get(&pr) {
                if nv != NetId::VCC {
                    errors.push(format!("{}:{pv} được cấu hình là chân VCC nhưng trong Netlist lại nối vào mạng khác", c.id));
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(pg) = p_g {
            let pr = PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: pg };
            ctx.main_ic_pwr_pins.insert(pr);
            if let Some(&ng) = ctx.net_of.get(&pr) {
                if ng != NetId::GND {
                    errors.push(format!("{}:{pg} được cấu hình là chân GND nhưng trong Netlist lại nối vào mạng khác", c.id));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    optimize_symmetric_pins(layout, &ctx.net_of, parsed, topo, weights);

    for net in &parsed.nets {
        for &pr in &net.pins {
            if comps.iter().any(|c| c.cid == pr.comp && !c.comp_type.is_special()) {
                if let Some((col, row)) = layout.pin_hole(pr.comp, pr.pin) {
                    ctx.add_pin(board, pr.comp, pr.pin, col, row, comps, parsed, errors);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| !c.comp_type.is_special() && placed_ids.contains(&c.cid)) {
        for p in 1..=c.pins {
            if ctx.net_of.contains_key(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: p }) {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some((col, row)) = layout.pin_hole(c.cid, p) {
                let (h, cu) = (half_of(row) as usize, col as usize);
                if board.col_net[h][cu] == 0 {
                    board.col_net[h][cu] = nc_tag(c.cid, p);
                    ctx.col_own[h][cu] = Some(c.cid);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if !layout.ics.is_empty() {
        let mut ic_by_natural = layout.ics.clone();
        ic_by_natural.sort_by(|a, b| natural_id_cmp(&a.id, &b.id));
        let mut decoupled_ic_set: FxHashSet<CompId> = FxHashSet::default();

        let mut cand_caps: Vec<&CompSpec> = comps
            .iter()
            .filter(|c| {
                if !matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::CC | CompType::C) || ctx.invalid_comp_ids.contains(&c.cid) {
                    return false;
                }
                let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
                let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
                let is_vcc_gnd = matches!((n1, n2), (Some(NetId::VCC), Some(NetId::GND)) | (Some(NetId::GND), Some(NetId::VCC)));
                let is_unassigned_cc = c.comp_type == CompType::CC && n1.is_none() && n2.is_none();
                is_vcc_gnd || is_unassigned_cc
            })
            .collect();
        cand_caps.sort_by_key(|c| u8::from(c.comp_type != CompType::CC));

        for &cap in &cand_caps {
            if let Some(&target_ic) = parsed.cap_ic_hint.get(&cap.cid) {
                if !decoupled_ic_set.contains(&target_ic) && ic_by_natural.iter().any(|x| x.cid == target_ic) {
                    lock_cap_across_ic(cap, target_ic, board, layout, &mut ctx, &mut decoupled_ic_set, comps, parsed, errors);
                }
            }
        }
        for &cap in &cand_caps {
            if ctx.locked_decoupling_ids.contains(&cap.cid) {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some(ic_obj) = ic_by_natural.iter().find(|x| !decoupled_ic_set.contains(&x.cid)) {
                lock_cap_across_ic(cap, ic_obj.cid, board, layout, &mut ctx, &mut decoupled_ic_set, comps, parsed, errors);
            }
        }
    }
    ctx
}
