=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/discrete_place/pin_swap.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::macro_place::topology::TopologyCache;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, rail_ok, DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, ParsedNetlist};
use crate::model::ids::{NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

/// Đánh giá chi phí đặt chân nguồn `VCC`/`GND` của SW/BTN2 tại cột `col` thuộc nửa bo `half` (#2, #3):
/// - Phạt nặng nếu `col` rơi trúng cột nghỉ của thanh nguồn Rail (`!rail_ok(col)`, tức `(c - 3) % 6 == 5`)
/// - Phạt nếu lỗ cắm thẳng trên thanh nguồn bị chiếm bởi 12 lỗ dây nguồn mặc định (`DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES`)
/// - Thưởng điểm nếu khớp/gần với cột nguồn cùng mạng của IC hoặc linh kiện lân cận trên cùng nửa bo
#[inline]
fn eval_pwr_pin_col_cost(
    net: NetId,
    col: u8,
    half: u8,
    pwr_anchors: &[(u8, u8, NetId)],
) -> f64 {
    let is_vcc = net == NetId::VCC;
    let rr = if half == 0 {
        if is_vcc { -1i8 } else { -2i8 }
    } else if is_vcc {
        11i8
    } else {
        10i8
    };
    let is_outer_rail = matches!(rr, -2 | 11);

    let mut cost = 0.0;
    if !rail_ok(col) {
        cost += if is_outer_rail { 28.0 } else { 22.0 };
    } else {
        let pass_r = if half == 0 { -1i8 } else { 10i8 };
        let hits_default = DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES
            .iter()
            .any(|&(dc, dr)| dc == col && (dr == rr || (is_outer_rail && dr == pass_r)));
        if hits_default {
            cost += 15.0;
        }
    }

    let mut min_same_dist = 999i32;
    for &(ac, ah, anet) in pwr_anchors {
        if ah == half && anet == net && ac != col {
            let d = (ac as i32 - col as i32).abs();
            if d < min_same_dist {
                min_same_dist = d;
            }
        }
    }
    if min_same_dist <= 3 {
        cost -= 3.5 - (min_same_dist as f64) * 0.5;
    } else if min_same_dist < 999 {
        cost += (min_same_dist as f64) * 0.25;
    }
    cost
}

/// Tự động hoán đổi chân đối xứng cho BTN2 (1<->2) và SW (1<->3):
/// - Quay chân tín hiệu về phía IC mục tiêu
/// - Tự động lật SW/BTN2 khi chân nguồn VCC/GND rơi trúng cột nghỉ Rail (`(c - 3) % 6 == 5`) hoặc khớp cột nguồn lân cận (#2, #3)
pub fn optimize_symmetric_pins(
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    net_of: &FxHashMap<PinRef, NetId>,
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) {
    let mut ic_pin_col: FxHashMap<PinRef, f64> = FxHashMap::default();
    let mut pwr_anchors: Vec<(u8, u8, NetId)> = Vec::new();

    for ic in &layout.ics {
        let h = ic.pins / 2;
        for p in 1..=ic.pins {
            let (c, r) = if ic.rot == 0 {
                if p <= h { (ic.c_min + p - 1, 5i8) } else { (ic.c_min + (ic.pins - p), 4i8) }
            } else if p <= h {
                (ic.c_max - p + 1, 4i8)
            } else {
                (ic.c_min + p - h - 1, 5i8)
            };
            let pr = PinRef { comp: ic.cid, pin: p };
            ic_pin_col.insert(pr, c as f64);
            if let Some(&net) = net_of.get(&pr) {
                if net.is_power() {
                    pwr_anchors.push((c, half_of(r), net));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let mut net_target_col: FxHashMap<NetId, f64> = FxHashMap::default();
    for net in &parsed.nets {
        if net.id.is_power() {
            continue;
        }
        let mut sum = 0.0;
        let mut cnt = 0usize;
        for pr in &net.pins {
            if let Some(&col) = ic_pin_col.get(pr) {
                sum += col;
                cnt += 1;
            }
        }
        if cnt > 0 {
            net_target_col.insert(net.id, sum / (cnt as f64));
        }
    }

    for &n1 in &topo.r_adj_keys {
        if net_target_col.contains_key(&n1) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(neighbors) = topo.r_adj.get(&n1) {
            for &n2 in neighbors {
                if let Some(&target_c) = net_target_col.get(&n2) {
                    net_target_col.insert(n1, target_c);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let pin_col_cost = |net_opt: Option<NetId>, col: u8, half: u8, anchors: &[(u8, u8, NetId)]| -> f64 {
        let Some(net) = net_opt else { return 0.0 };
        if net.is_power() {
            eval_pwr_pin_col_cost(net, col, half, anchors)
        } else if let Some(&t) = net_target_col.get(&net) {
            (col as f64 - t).abs()
        } else {
            0.0
        }
    };

    // Hoán đổi chân 1 <-> 2 của BTN2 nếu giúp chân tín hiệu về gần IC mục tiêu hoặc né cột nghỉ nguồn
    for btn in &mut layout.btns {
        let half = half_of(btn.pin_row());
        let n1 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: btn.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: btn.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        if btn.flip == 0 {
            let (c_left, c_right) = (btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
            let cost_normal = pin_col_cost(n1, c_left, half, &pwr_anchors)
                + pin_col_cost(n2, c_right, half, &pwr_anchors);
            let cost_flip = pin_col_cost(n1, c_right, half, &pwr_anchors)
                + pin_col_cost(n2, c_left, half, &pwr_anchors);
            if cost_flip + weights.pin_swap_threshold < cost_normal {
                btn.flip = 1;
            }
        }
        let (c1, c2) = if btn.flip != 0 { (btn.c_max, btn.c_min) } else { (btn.c_min, btn.c_max) };
        if let Some(net) = n1.filter(|n| n.is_power()) {
            pwr_anchors.push((c1, half, net));
        }
        if let Some(net) = n2.filter(|n| n.is_power()) {
            pwr_anchors.push((c2, half, net));
        }
    }

    // Hoán đổi chân 1 <-> 3 của SW nếu giảm khoảng cách dây về IC mục tiêu hoặc giúp chân nguồn VCC/GND né cột nghỉ Rail (#2, #3)
    for sw in &mut layout.sws {
        let half = half_of(sw.row);
        let n1 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: sw.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n3 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: sw.cid, pin: 3 }).copied();
        let overlaps_ic = layout.ics.iter().any(|ic| sw.c_min <= ic.c_max && sw.c_max >= ic.c_min);
        if sw.flip == 0 && !overlaps_ic {
            let (c_left, c_right) = (sw.c_min, sw.c_max);
            let cost_normal = pin_col_cost(n1, c_left, half, &pwr_anchors)
                + pin_col_cost(n3, c_right, half, &pwr_anchors);
            let cost_flip = pin_col_cost(n1, c_right, half, &pwr_anchors)
                + pin_col_cost(n3, c_left, half, &pwr_anchors);
            if cost_flip + weights.pin_swap_threshold < cost_normal {
                sw.flip = 1;
            }
        }
        let (c1, c3) = if sw.flip != 0 { (sw.c_max, sw.c_min) } else { (sw.c_min, sw.c_max) };
        if let Some(net) = n1.filter(|n| n.is_power()) {
            pwr_anchors.push((c1, half, net));
        }
        if let Some(net) = n3.filter(|n| n.is_power()) {
            pwr_anchors.push((c3, half, net));
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::macro_place::topology::build_topology_cache;
    use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, PlacedIc, PlacedSw};
    use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType};
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_sw_flip_avoids_rail_gap_and_matches_neighbor_pwr() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "S1", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "S2", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "R2", CompType::R),
        ];
        // S1: chân 3 nối GND, đặt ở cột 6..=8 (chân 3 rơi vào cột 8 là cột nghỉ nguồn `(8-3)%6 == 5`)
        // S2: chân 1 nối VCC, chân 3 nối GND, đặt ở cột 16..=18 cạnh U1 (chân 7 GND ở cột 15)
        let txt = "
            VCC U1:14; GND U1:7
            S1:2 -> R1 -> U1:1; S1:3 -> GND
            S2:1 -> VCC; S2:2 -> R2 -> U1:2; S2:3 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc {
            cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 9, c_max: 15, w: 7,
        });
        layout.sws.push(PlacedSw {
            cid: CompId(1), id: "S1".into(), c_min: 6, c_max: 8, row: 9, flip: 0,
        });
        layout.sws.push(PlacedSw {
            cid: CompId(2), id: "S2".into(), c_min: 16, c_max: 18, row: 9, flip: 0,
        });

        optimize_symmetric_pins(&mut layout, &parsed.net_of, &parsed, &topo, &weights);

        // S1 tự động lật (flip = 1) để chân 3 (GND) chuyển từ cột nghỉ 8 sang cột 6 (rail_ok(6) == true)
        assert_eq!(layout.sws[0].flip, 1);
        assert_eq!(layout.pin_hole(CompId(1), 3), Some((6, 9)));

        // S2 tự động lật (flip = 1) để chân 3 (GND) chuyển về cột 16 sát chân GND U1:7 (cột 15)
        assert_eq!(layout.sws[1].flip, 1);
        assert_eq!(layout.pin_hole(CompId(2), 3), Some((16, 9)));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/discrete_place/special.rs ===
use crate::config::personas::get_persona;
use crate::config::weights::{CostWeights, SpecialSpan};
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::{
    base_net_of, is_pwr_tag, nc_tag, pwr_aux_tag, pwr_tag, DiscreteContext,
};
use crate::discrete_place::special_order::{
    apply_frozen_specials, build_special_order, can_direct_plug_rail, can_straight_rail,
    compute_net_stats, find_series_partners, update_net_centers, IcSnapshot, SpecialOptions,
};
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, rail_ok, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, CompSpec, ParsedNetlist, PlacedSpecial};
use crate::model::geometry::{seg_dist_sq, v_seg_free, vis_y, Footprint};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, Mulberry32, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
struct Lookahead {
    net: Option<NetId>,
    sp: SpecialSpan,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
struct PrepHole {
    col: u8,
    row: i8,
    half: u8,
    is_rail: bool,
    is_shared: bool,
    is_pwr: bool,
    vy: f64,
    base: f64,
}

fn valid_holes(
    board: &BoardState,
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
    net_opt: Option<NetId>,
    cid: CompId,
    pin: u8,
    comp_type: CompType,
) -> Vec<(u8, i8)> {
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(64);
    if let Some(net) = net_opt.filter(|n| n.is_power()) {
        let r_rows = if net == NetId::VCC { [-1i8, 11i8] } else { [-2i8, 10i8] };
        for c in 3u8..=61 {
            if !rail_ok(c) {
                continue;
            }
            for rr in r_rows {
                if !board.is_occupied(c, rr) {
                    out.push((c, rr));
                }
            }
        }
        if out.len() >= 8 {
            return out;
        }
    }
    for c in 1u8..=63 {
        for h in [0u8, 1u8] {
            let cur_tag = board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize];
            match net_opt {
                Some(net) => {
                    let tag = pwr_tag(net, cid, pin, if h != 0 { 5 } else { 0 }, comp_type, &ctx.main_ic_pwr_pins);
                    if cur_tag != 0 && cur_tag != tag {
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                None if cur_tag != 0 => continue,
                None => {}
            }
            let r_range = if h != 0 { 5i8..=9 } else { 0i8..=4 };
            let mut has_non_groove = false;
            for r in r_range.clone() {
                if !board.is_occupied(c, r) && r != 4 && r != 5 {
                    has_non_groove = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            for r in r_range {
                if !board.is_occupied(c, r) && (!has_non_groove || (r != 4 && r != 5)) {
                    out.push((c, r));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    out
}

fn lookahead_info(
    cid: CompId,
    pin_net: Option<NetId>,
    r_comps: &[CompSpec],
    placed_ids: &FxHashSet<CompId>,
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
) -> Option<Lookahead> {
    let net = pin_net.filter(|n| !n.is_power())?;
    for other in r_comps {
        if other.cid == cid || placed_ids.contains(&other.cid) {
            continue;
        }
        let on1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: other.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let on2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: other.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        if on1 == Some(net) {
            return Some(Lookahead { net: on2, sp: SpecialSpan::for_type(other.comp_type) });
        }
        if on2 == Some(net) {
            return Some(Lookahead { net: on1, sp: SpecialSpan::for_type(other.comp_type) });
        }
    }
    None
}

fn can_partner_ortho_share(
    col: u8,
    h: u8,
    la: Lookahead,
    board: &BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
) -> bool {
    let Some(net) = la.net else { return false };
    let (min_d, max_d) = (la.sp.min.ceil() as i32, la.sp.max.floor() as i32);
    if !net.is_power() {
        if let Some(masks) = ctx.pres.get(&net) {
            let m = masks[h as usize];
            for tc in 1u8..=63 {
                if (m & (1u64 << tc)) != 0 {
                    let d = (tc as i32 - col as i32).abs();
                    if d >= min_d && d <= max_d {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    if can_direct_plug_rail(board, fps, col, h, net) {
        return true;
    }
    let lo = (col as i32 - max_d).max(1) as u8;
    let hi = (col as i32 + max_d).min(63) as u8;
    let aux_tag = pwr_aux_tag(net, h);
    for tc in lo..=hi {
        if (tc as i32 - col as i32).abs() < min_d {
            continue;
        }
        let cur = board.col_net[h as usize][tc as usize];
        if (cur == 0 || cur == aux_tag) && can_straight_rail(board, fps, tc, h, net) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    false
}

/// Tính điểm thưởng LED Đối Xứng (#10) khi đặt LED `c` so với các LED cùng nhóm đã đặt trước đó
#[inline]
fn eval_sym_led_bonus(
    a_col: u8,
    a_row: i8,
    b_col: u8,
    b_row: i8,
    sym_partners: &[PlacedSpecial],
    pair_bonus: f64,
) -> f64 {
    if sym_partners.is_empty() || pair_bonus <= 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let (ha, hb) = (half_of(a_row), half_of(b_row));
    let mut best_bonus = 0.0_f64;
    for pl in sym_partners {
        let (pha, phb) = (half_of(pl.r1), half_of(pl.r2));
        let same_cols = (a_col == pl.c1 && b_col == pl.c2) || (a_col == pl.c2 && b_col == pl.c1);
        // 1. Đối xứng gương qua rãnh E-F: Cùng cặp cột (c1, c2), nằm ở nửa bo đối diện
        if same_cols && ha != pha && hb != phb {
            let mut b = pair_bonus;
            let mirror_r1 = (!is_rail_row(a_row) && !is_rail_row(pl.r1) && a_row + pl.r1 == 9)
                || (is_rail_row(a_row) && is_rail_row(pl.r1));
            let mirror_r2 = (!is_rail_row(b_row) && !is_rail_row(pl.r2) && b_row + pl.r2 == 9)
                || (is_rail_row(b_row) && is_rail_row(pl.r2));
            if mirror_r1 && mirror_r2 {
                b += pair_bonus * 0.25;
            }
            if b > best_bonus {
                best_bonus = b;
            }
        } else if ha == pha && hb == phb {
            // 2. Thẳng hàng trên cùng nửa bo: Cùng hàng lỗ cắm và song song hướng chân
            let same_rows = a_row == pl.r1 && b_row == pl.r2;
            let same_vec = (a_col as i32 - b_col as i32) == (pl.c1 as i32 - pl.c2 as i32);
            if same_rows && same_vec {
                let b = pair_bonus * 0.85;
                if b > best_bonus {
                    best_bonus = b;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    best_bonus
}

/// Đặt toàn bộ linh kiện rời 2 chân (R, LED, C, CC) lên mặt bo (`placeSpecialComponents` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn place_special_components(
    use_feedback: bool,
    seed_offset: u32,
    opts: &SpecialOptions,
    snap_ic: &IcSnapshot,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    ctx: &mut DiscreteContext,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    ordered_comps: &[CompSpec],
    all_comps: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    col_hist: &[f32; 65],
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
    unplaced: &mut usize,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
) -> FxHashSet<NetId> {
    if use_feedback || seed_offset > 0 {
        snap_ic.restore(board, ctx, layout, unplaced, errors);
    }
    let p_cfg = get_persona(opts.persona);
    let ortho_w = opts.ortho_weight.unwrap_or(p_cfg.ortho_w);
    let dyn_br_pen = opts.dyn_br_pen.unwrap_or(p_cfg.br_pen);
    let base_seed = opts.r_seed.unwrap_or(if opts.sr != 0 { opts.sr } else { 1 });
    let mut r_rng = Mulberry32::new(base_seed.wrapping_add(997).wrapping_add(seed_offset));

    let (total_net_pins, mut cross_half_nets) = compute_net_stats(parsed, ctx);
    let series_partner = find_series_partners(ordered_comps, ctx, &total_net_pins);
    let r_comps = build_special_order(ordered_comps, ctx, &series_partner, &opts.r_order, &p_cfg);

    let mut placed_special_ids = ctx.locked_decoupling_ids.clone();
    let mut net_center: FxHashMap<NetId, f64> = FxHashMap::default();
    update_net_centers(ctx, &mut net_center);

    apply_frozen_specials(
        &r_comps, opts, &series_partner, board, layout, ctx,
        &mut placed_special_ids, all_comps, parsed, errors,
    );
    update_net_centers(ctx, &mut net_center);

    let sig_share_bonus = (ortho_w + 18.0).max(30.0);

    for c in &r_comps {
        if placed_special_ids.contains(&c.cid) {
            continue;
        }
        let sp = SpecialSpan::for_type(c.comp_type);
        let (min_sq, max_sq) = (sp.min * sp.min, sp.max * sp.max);
        let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();

        if matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::CC | CompType::C)
            && matches!((n1, n2), (Some(NetId::VCC), Some(NetId::GND)) | (Some(NetId::GND), Some(NetId::VCC)))
            && ctx.locked_ic_caps.len() >= layout.ics.len()
            && !layout.ics.is_empty()
        {
            placed_special_ids.insert(c.cid);
            continue;
        }
        if (n1.is_some() && n1 == n2) || ctx.invalid_comp_ids.contains(&c.cid) {
            *unplaced += 1;
            continue;
        }

        let holes_a = valid_holes(board, ctx, n1, c.cid, 1, c.comp_type);
        let holes_b = valid_holes(board, ctx, n2, c.cid, 2, c.comp_type);
        let la1 = lookahead_info(c.cid, n1, &r_comps, &placed_special_ids, ctx);
        let la2 = lookahead_info(c.cid, n2, &r_comps, &placed_special_ids, ctx);

        let raw_t1 = n1.and_then(|n| net_center.get(&n).copied())
            .or_else(|| la1.and_then(|l| l.net).and_then(|n| net_center.get(&n).copied()));
        let raw_t2 = n2.and_then(|n| net_center.get(&n).copied())
            .or_else(|| la2.and_then(|l| l.net).and_then(|n| net_center.get(&n).copied()));
        let c_target1 = raw_t1.or(raw_t2);
        let c_target2 = raw_t2.or(raw_t1);
        let is_series_chain = series_partner.contains_key(&c.cid);

        let sym_partners: Vec<PlacedSpecial> = if c.comp_type == CompType::LED && !parsed.sym_led_groups.is_empty() {
            let mut sp_list = Vec::new();
            for grp in &parsed.sym_led_groups {
                if grp.contains(&c.cid) {
                    for &pid in grp {
                        if pid != c.cid {
                            if let Some(pl) = layout.leds.iter().find(|l| l.cid == pid) {
                                sp_list.push(pl.clone());
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            sp_list
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };

        let (prep_a, prep_b) = {
            let check_bridge_cost = |col: u8, h: u8, net_opt: Option<NetId>, b_ref: &BoardState| -> f64 {
                let is_pwr = net_opt.is_some_and(NetId::is_power);
                if !is_pwr && net_opt.is_some_and(|n| b_ref.col_net[h as usize][col as usize] == n.0) {
                    return 0.0;
                }
                let groove_free = !b_ref.is_occupied(col, 4) && !b_ref.is_occupied(col, 5) && v_seg_free(fps, col, 4, 5);
                if !groove_free {
                    return -4.5;
                }
                let other_tag = b_ref.col_net[(1 - h) as usize][col as usize];
                let is_series_node = !is_pwr && net_opt.and_then(|n| total_net_pins.get(&n)).copied().unwrap_or(0) <= 2;
                if other_tag == 0 {
                    return if is_pwr { dyn_br_pen - 2.0 } else if is_series_node { dyn_br_pen } else { (dyn_br_pen * 0.62).round() };
                }
                if !is_pwr && net_opt.is_some_and(|n| other_tag == n.0) {
                    return -6.0;
                }
                if cross_half_nets.contains(&NetId(other_tag)) { 8.0 } else { -11.0 }
            };

            let prep_list = |h_list: &[(u8, i8)], net_opt: Option<NetId>, c_target: Option<f64>, la: Option<Lookahead>| -> Vec<PrepHole> {
                let is_pwr = net_opt.is_some_and(NetId::is_power);
                let net_masks = net_opt.filter(|n| !n.is_power()).and_then(|n| ctx.pres.get(&n)).copied();
                let net_set_size = net_masks.map_or(0, |m| (m[0].count_ones() + m[1].count_ones()) as usize);
                let tot_pins = net_opt.and_then(|n| total_net_pins.get(&n)).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                let placed_pins = net_opt.filter(|n| !n.is_power()).and_then(|n| ctx.holes.get(&n)).map_or(0, Vec::len);
                let is_two_pin_series = !is_pwr && tot_pins == 2 && placed_pins == 1;
                let dist_weight = if is_series_chain { 0.68 } else { 0.26 };
                let mut col_cache = [f64::NAN; 128];

                h_list.iter().map(|&(col, row)| {
                    let is_rail = is_rail_row(row);
                    if is_rail {
                        let dist = c_target.map_or(0.0, |t| (col as f64 - t).abs() * 0.26);
                        return PrepHole {
                            col, row, half: 2, is_rail: true, is_shared: false, is_pwr: true,
                            vy: vis_y(row), base: weights.spec_rail_base_bonus + dist + 0.003 * (col as f64),
                        };
                    }
                    let h = half_of(row);
                    let cache_idx = (h as usize) * 64 + (col as usize);
                    let is_shared = net_masks.is_some_and(|m| (m[h as usize] & (1u64 << col)) != 0);
                    let mut cc = col_cache[cache_idx];
                    if cc.is_nan() {
                        let free_in_strip = board.free_hole_count(col, h);
                        let cur_tag = board.col_net[h as usize][col as usize];
                        let is_ic_pwr_shared = is_pwr && Some(base_net_of(cur_tag)) == net_opt;
                        let is_aux_shared = is_pwr && net_opt.is_some_and(|n| cur_tag == pwr_aux_tag(n, h));
                        let all_in_strip = is_shared && net_set_size == 1 && (placed_pins + 1 == tot_pins) && !is_pwr && la.is_none();
                        let must_wire_later = !all_in_strip && (is_pwr || tot_pins > 1 || la.is_some());
                        let starve_pen = if free_in_strip <= 1 && must_wire_later { weights.spec_starve_pen } else { 0.0 };
                        let split_pen = if is_two_pin_series && !is_shared { weights.spec_split_series_pen } else { 0.0 };
                        let share = if is_shared {
                            sig_share_bonus
                        } else if is_ic_pwr_shared {
                            18.0
                        } else if is_aux_shared {
                            6.0
                        } else if is_pwr && net_opt.is_some_and(|n| can_straight_rail(board, fps, col, h, n)) {
                            p_cfg.rail_share
                        } else {
                            0.0
                        };
                        let dist = if !is_shared { c_target.map_or(0.0, |t| (col as f64 - t).abs() * dist_weight) } else { 0.0 };
                        let look = if !is_shared && la.is_some_and(|l| can_partner_ortho_share(col, h, l, board, fps, ctx)) {
                            let l_net = la.and_then(|l| l.net);
                            if l_net.is_some_and(|n| n.is_power() && can_direct_plug_rail(board, fps, col, h, n)) {
                                16.0
                            } else if p_cfg.led_first && c.comp_type == CompType::LED {
                                18.0
                            } else {
                                7.0
                            }
                        } else {
                            0.0
                        };
                        // Hạng mục #4: Khi led_first = true, phạt +150.0 nếu LED đặt cùng nửa bo nhưng cách chân neo IC của R dưới 3 ô (< 3)
                        let r_led_lock_pen = if p_cfg.led_first && c.comp_type == CompType::LED {
                            if let Some(l_net) = la.and_then(|l| l.net).filter(|n| !n.is_power()) {
                                if let Some(m) = ctx.pres.get(&l_net).map(|ms| ms[h as usize]) {
                                    let max_d = la.map_or(13, |l| l.sp.max.floor() as i32);
                                    let too_close = (1u8..=63).any(|tc| (m & (1u64 << tc)) != 0 && (tc as i32 - col as i32).abs() < 3);
                                    let has_valid = (1u8..=63).any(|tc| {
                                        let d = (tc as i32 - col as i32).abs();
                                        (m & (1u64 << tc)) != 0 && d >= 3 && d <= max_d
                                    });
                                    if too_close && !has_valid { 150.0 } else { 0.0 }
                                } else {
                                    0.0
                                }
                            } else {
                                0.0
                            }
                        } else {
                            0.0
                        };
                        let br_pen = if is_shared || is_aux_shared || is_ic_pwr_shared { 0.0 } else { check_bridge_cost(col, h, net_opt, board) };
                        let is_evicted = (opts.evict_cols_mask & (1u64 << col)) != 0;
                        let fb_col_pen = if !is_shared {
                            (if use_feedback { (col_hist[col as usize] as f64) * 2.5 } else { 0.0 })
                                + (if is_evicted { weights.spec_evict_col_pen } else { 0.0 })
                        } else {
                            0.0
                        };
                        cc = -share - look + dist + br_pen + fb_col_pen + split_pen + starve_pen + r_led_lock_pen + 0.003 * (col as f64);
                        col_cache[cache_idx] = cc;
                    }
                    let groove_pen = if row == 4 || row == 5 { weights.spec_groove_row_pen } else { 0.0 };
                    let strip_tag = board.col_net[h as usize][col as usize];
                    let is_pwr_strip = is_pwr || is_pwr_tag(strip_tag);
                    let rail_hole_pen = if row == 0 || row == 9 { if is_pwr_strip { 4.5 } else { 1.5 } } else { 0.0 };
                    let fb_row_pen = if use_feedback { (row_hist[row as usize] as f64) * 3.0 } else { 0.0 };
                    PrepHole {
                        col, row, half: h, is_rail: false, is_shared, is_pwr,
                        vy: vis_y(row), base: cc + groove_pen + rail_hole_pen + fb_row_pen,
                    }
                }).collect()
            };
            (prep_list(&holes_a, n1, c_target1, la1), prep_list(&holes_b, n2, c_target2, la2))
        };

        let mut b_by_col: [Vec<PrepHole>; 65] = core::array::from_fn(|_| Vec::new());
        for b in prep_b {
            b_by_col[b.col as usize].push(b);
        }

        let placed_specials: Vec<([f64; 2], [f64; 2], u8, u8)> = layout.rs.iter()
            .chain(layout.leds.iter())
            .chain(layout.cs.iter().filter(|x| !x.locked))
            .map(|pl| (
                [pl.c1 as f64, vis_y(pl.r1)],
                [pl.c2 as f64, vis_y(pl.r2)],
                pl.c1.min(pl.c2).saturating_sub(1),
                pl.c1.max(pl.c2).saturating_add(1),
            ))
            .collect();

        let (mut best_pair, mut bs) = (None, 1e9_f64);
        let max_span_i = sp.max.floor() as i32;

        for a in &prep_a {
            let c_min_b = (a.col as i32 - max_span_i).max(1) as u8;
            let c_max_b = (a.col as i32 + max_span_i).min(63) as u8;
            for c_b in c_min_b..=c_max_b {
                for b in &b_by_col[c_b as usize] {
                    if (a.is_rail && b.is_rail) || (!a.is_rail && !b.is_rail && a.col == b.col && a.half == b.half) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let one_rail = a.is_rail != b.is_rail;
                    // Hạng mục #1: Khi 1 chân ở thanh nguồn (one_rail), tính khoảng cách dọc qua vis_y
                    let dx = (a.col as f64) - (b.col as f64);
                    let dy = if one_rail {
                        (a.vy - b.vy).abs()
                    } else {
                        (a.row - b.row).abs() as f64
                    };
                    let d_sq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
                    if d_sq < min_sq || d_sq > max_sq {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let mut outer_rail_pen = 0.0;
                    if a.col == b.col {
                        if (a.row == -2 || b.row == -2) && board.is_occupied(a.col, -1) { continue; }
                        if (a.row == 11 || b.row == 11) && board.is_occupied(a.col, 10) { continue; }
                        if (one_rail && matches!(a.row, -2 | 11)) || (one_rail && matches!(b.row, -2 | 11)) {
                            outer_rail_pen = 2.0;
                        }
                    }
                    let d = d_sq.sqrt();
                    let mut edge_snap_adj = 0.0;
                    if one_rail {
                        let bd = if a.is_rail { b } else { a };
                        if !is_pwr_tag(board.col_net[bd.half as usize][bd.col as usize]) {
                            edge_snap_adj = if bd.row == 0 || bd.row == 9 { -5.5 } else if bd.row == 1 || bd.row == 8 { -1.5 } else { 3.5 };
                        }
                    } else if c.comp_type == CompType::R && (a.is_pwr != b.is_pwr) {
                        // Hạng mục #4: Chỉ phạt +120.0 vượt rãnh E-F đối với điện trở nối nguồn; cho phép trở tín hiệu nối tiếp bước qua rãnh
                        if a.col != b.col { edge_snap_adj += 35.0; }
                        if (a.row <= 4) != (b.row <= 4) { edge_snap_adj += 120.0; }
                    }

                    // Hạng mục #4: Mở ngưỡng thưởng both_anchored lên toàn dải d <= sp.max (13.5, thưởng -38.0)
                    let both_anchored = a.is_shared && b.is_shared && !one_rail;
                    let span_pen = if both_anchored && d <= sp.max {
                        (d - 3.0).max(0.0) * 0.12 - 38.0
                    } else {
                        (d - sp.pref).abs() * if is_series_chain && d > 8.0 { 1.15 } else { 0.38 }
                    };
                    let is_ortho = a.col == b.col || a.row == b.row;
                    let horiz_edge_pen = if !one_rail && a.row == b.row && (a.row == 0 || a.row == 9) { 10.0 } else { 0.0 };
                    let sym_bonus = eval_sym_led_bonus(a.col, a.row, b.col, b.row, &sym_partners, weights.sym_led_pair_bonus);
                    let jit = r_rng.next_f64() * if use_feedback { 1.4 } else { 0.75 };
                    let sc = span_pen + a.base + b.base + outer_rail_pen + edge_snap_adj + horiz_edge_pen
                        - (if is_ortho { ortho_w } else { 0.0 }) - sym_bonus + jit;
                    if sc >= bs {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let (p1, p2) = ([a.col as f64, a.vy], [b.col as f64, b.vy]);
                    let (seg_min_c, seg_max_c) = (a.col.min(b.col), a.col.max(b.col));
                    let colliding = placed_specials.iter().any(|&(pl1, pl2, min_c, max_c)| {
                        seg_max_c >= min_c && seg_min_c <= max_c && seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, pl1, pl2) < weights.spec_body_clearance_sq
                    });
                    if !colliding {
                        bs = sc;
                        best_pair = Some(((a.col, a.row), (b.col, b.row)));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        let Some((a_pt, b_pt)) = best_pair else {
            errors.push(format!("{}: không có 2 lỗ trống thỏa khoảng cách {}-{} hoặc bị đè lên linh kiện khác", c.id, sp.min, sp.max));
            *unplaced += 1;
            continue;
        };
        board.occupy(a_pt.0, a_pt.1);
        board.occupy(b_pt.0, b_pt.1);
        if a_pt.0 == b_pt.0 {
            if a_pt.1 == -2 || b_pt.1 == -2 { board.occupy(a_pt.0, -1); }
            if a_pt.1 == 11 || b_pt.1 == 11 { board.occupy(a_pt.0, 10); }
        }
        if n1.is_none() && !is_rail_row(a_pt.1) {
            board.col_net[half_of(a_pt.1) as usize][a_pt.0 as usize] = nc_tag(c.cid, 1);
        }
        if n2.is_none() && !is_rail_row(b_pt.1) {
            board.col_net[half_of(b_pt.1) as usize][b_pt.0 as usize] = nc_tag(c.cid, 2);
        }
        let placed = PlacedSpecial {
            cid: c.cid, id: c.id.clone(), comp_type: c.comp_type,
            c1: a_pt.0, r1: a_pt.1, c2: b_pt.0, r2: b_pt.1, locked: false, ic_id: None,
        };
        match c.comp_type {
            CompType::R => layout.rs.push(placed),
            CompType::LED => layout.leds.push(placed),
            _ => layout.cs.push(placed),
        }
        placed_special_ids.insert(c.cid);
        ctx.add_pin(board, c.cid, 1, a_pt.0, a_pt.1, all_comps, parsed, errors);
        ctx.add_pin(board, c.cid, 2, b_pt.0, b_pt.1, all_comps, parsed, errors);
        update_net_centers(ctx, &mut net_center);
    }

    for n in &parsed.nets {
        if !n.id.is_power() {
            if let Some(h_list) = ctx.holes.get(&n.id) {
                if h_list.iter().any(|h| h.row <= 4) && h_list.iter().any(|h| h.row >= 5) {
                    cross_half_nets.insert(n.id);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    cross_half_nets
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::setup_macro_and_decoupling;
    use crate::macro_place::topology::build_topology_cache;
    use crate::model::circuit::PlacedIc;
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_pullup_ortho_row0_and_symmetric_leds() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "R2", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "R3", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "L1", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(5), "L2", CompType::LED),
        ];
        // R1 là điện trở kéo nguồn nối từ U1:13 (cột 11, nửa trên) lên VCC
        // L1 (nửa trên, qua R2 nối U1:12 ở cột 12) và L2 (nửa dưới, qua R3 nối U1:3 ở cột 12) đối xứng gương #SYM_LED: L1=L2
        let txt = "
            #SYM_LED: L1=L2
            VCC U1:14; GND U1:7
            VCC -> R1 -> U1:13
            U1:12 -> R2 -> L1 -> GND
            U1:3 -> R3 -> L2 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc {
            cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 10, c_max: 16, w: 7,
        });
        let (mut board, mut fps) = (BoardState::new(false), Vec::new());
        layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);

        let (mut errors, mut unplaced) = (Vec::new(), 0usize);
        let mut ctx = setup_macro_and_decoupling(&mut board, &mut layout, &parsed, &topo, &comps, &weights, &mut errors);
        let snap_ic = IcSnapshot::capture(&board, &ctx, unplaced, errors.len());
        let (col_hist, row_hist) = ([0.0f32; 65], [0.0f32; 10]);
        let opts = SpecialOptions { persona: 2, sr: 1, ..Default::default() };

        place_special_components(
            false, 0, &opts, &snap_ic, &mut board, &mut layout, &mut ctx,
            &fps, &comps, &comps, &parsed, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights, &mut unplaced, &mut errors,
        );

        assert_eq!(unplaced, 0);
        assert!(errors.is_empty(), "Errors: {:?}", errors);

        // Hạng mục #1: Trở kéo nguồn R1 đứng thẳng góc 90° từ lỗ Hàng A (row = 0) lên thẳng Thanh Nguồn (row = -1)
        let r1 = layout.rs.iter().find(|r| r.id == "R1").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r1.c1, r1.c2);
        assert_eq!((r1.r1.min(r1.r2), r1.r1.max(r1.r2)), (-1, 0));

        // Hạng mục #10: L1 và L2 đặt đối xứng gương qua rãnh E-F (cùng cột, 1 con ở nửa trên, 1 con ở nửa dưới)
        let l1 = layout.leds.iter().find(|l| l.id == "L1").unwrap();
        let l2 = layout.leds.iter().find(|l| l.id == "L2").unwrap();
        assert_eq!((l1.c1, l1.c2), (l2.c1, l2.c2));
        assert_ne!(half_of(l1.r1), half_of(l2.r1));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/discrete_place/special_order.rs ===
use crate::config::personas::PersonaConfig;
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::{nc_tag, pwr_tag, DiscreteContext, HolePin};
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, rail_ok, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, CompSpec, ParsedNetlist, PlacedSpecial};
use crate::model::geometry::{v_seg_free, Footprint};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};

/// Cấu hình tùy chọn cho thuật toán đặt linh kiện rời (`placeSpecialComponents`)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct SpecialOptions {
    pub persona: usize,
    pub sr: u32,
    pub r_seed: Option<u32>,
    pub ortho_weight: Option<f64>,
    pub dyn_br_pen: Option<f64>,
    pub dyn_aux_share: Option<f64>,
    pub r_order: Vec<usize>,
    /// Mặt nạ bit các cột bị trục xuất trong chế độ Vi phẫu (cột 1..=63 ứng với bit 1..=63)
    pub evict_cols_mask: u64,
    pub boost_nets: FxHashSet<NetId>,
    pub frozen_specials: FxHashMap<CompId, PlacedSpecial>,
}

/// Bản chụp trạng thái mặt bo ngay sau khi khóa cứng Tụ lọc IC (`snapIC` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct IcSnapshot {
    pub board: BoardState,
    pub col_own: [[Option<CompId>; 64]; 2],
    pub pres: FxHashMap<NetId, [u64; 2]>,
    pub holes: FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<HolePin>>,
    pub locked_cs: Vec<PlacedSpecial>,
    pub unplaced_base: usize,
    pub err_len_base: usize,
}

impl IcSnapshot {
    pub fn capture(
        board: &BoardState,
        ctx: &DiscreteContext,
        unplaced: usize,
        err_len: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            board: *board,
            col_own: ctx.col_own,
            pres: ctx.pres.clone(),
            holes: ctx.holes.clone(),
            locked_cs: ctx.locked_ic_caps.clone(),
            unplaced_base: unplaced,
            err_len_base: err_len,
        }
    }

    pub fn restore(
        &self,
        board: &mut BoardState,
        ctx: &mut DiscreteContext,
        layout: &mut BoardLayout,
        unplaced: &mut usize,
        errors: &mut Vec<String>,
    ) {
        *board = self.board;
        ctx.col_own = self.col_own;
        ctx.pres = self.pres.clone();
        ctx.holes = self.holes.clone();
        layout.rs.clear();
        layout.leds.clear();
        layout.cs = self.locked_cs.clone();
        *unplaced = self.unplaced_base;
        errors.truncate(self.err_len_base);
    }
}

/// Kiểm tra cột `col` thuộc nửa `half` có thể nối dây thẳng đứng trực tiếp lên thanh nguồn `net` (`canStraightRail`)
#[inline]
pub fn can_straight_rail(
    board: &BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    col: u8,
    half: u8,
    net: NetId,
) -> bool {
    if !rail_ok(col) {
        return false;
    }
    let row_d = if half == 0 { 0 } else { 9 };
    let is_vcc = net == NetId::VCC;
    let rr = if half == 0 {
        if is_vcc { -1 } else { -2 }
    } else if is_vcc {
        11
    } else {
        10
    };
    !board.is_occupied(col, row_d) && !board.is_occupied(col, rr) && v_seg_free(fps, col, row_d, rr)
}

/// Kiểm tra linh kiện rời tại cột `col` thuộc nửa `half` có thể cắm thẳng chân vào lỗ thanh nguồn `net` (`canDirectPlugRail`)
#[inline]
pub fn can_direct_plug_rail(
    board: &BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    col: u8,
    half: u8,
    net: NetId,
) -> bool {
    if !rail_ok(col) {
        return false;
    }
    let is_vcc = net == NetId::VCC;
    let rr = if half == 0 {
        if is_vcc { -1 } else { -2 }
    } else if is_vcc {
        11
    } else {
        10
    };
    let pass_ok = match rr {
        -2 => !board.is_occupied(col, -1),
        11 => !board.is_occupied(col, 10),
        _ => true,
    };
    !board.is_occupied(col, rr)
        && pass_ok
        && v_seg_free(fps, col, if half == 0 { 1 } else { 8 }, rr)
}

/// Tính tổng số chân của từng mạng và tập các mạng vắt qua cả 2 nửa mặt bo (`totalNetPins` & `crossHalfNets`)
pub fn compute_net_stats(
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
) -> (FxHashMap<NetId, usize>, FxHashSet<NetId>) {
    let mut total_net_pins = FxHashMap::default();
    let mut cross_half_nets = FxHashSet::default();
    for n in &parsed.nets {
        total_net_pins.insert(n.id, n.pins.len());
        if n.id.is_power() {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(h_list) = ctx.holes.get(&n.id) {
            let has_top = h_list.iter().any(|h| h.row <= 4);
            let has_bot = h_list.iter().any(|h| h.row >= 5);
            if has_top && has_bot {
                cross_half_nets.insert(n.id);
            }
        }
    }
    (total_net_pins, cross_half_nets)
}

/// Cập nhật tọa độ cột trung tâm của các mạng tín hiệu (`updateCenters` trong main.html)
pub fn update_net_centers(ctx: &DiscreteContext, net_center: &mut FxHashMap<NetId, f64>) {
    net_center.clear();
    for (&net, h_list) in &ctx.holes {
        if net.is_power() || h_list.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let sum: f64 = h_list.iter().map(|h| h.col as f64).sum();
        net_center.insert(net, sum / (h_list.len() as f64));
    }
}

/// Tìm các cặp linh kiện rời mắc nối tiếp trực tiếp qua mạng 2 cực (`seriesPartner` trong main.html)
pub fn find_series_partners(
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
    total_net_pins: &FxHashMap<NetId, usize>,
) -> FxHashMap<CompId, (CompId, NetId)> {
    let mut partners = FxHashMap::default();
    let all_spec: Vec<&CompSpec> = comps
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special() && !ctx.locked_decoupling_ids.contains(&c.cid))
        .collect();

    for i in 0..all_spec.len() {
        for j in (i + 1)..all_spec.len() {
            let (a, b) = (all_spec[i], all_spec[j]);
            let na1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: a.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
            let na2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: a.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
            let nb1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: b.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
            let nb2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: b.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
            let shared = [na1, na2].into_iter().flatten().find(|&n| {
                !n.is_power()
                    && total_net_pins.get(&n).copied().unwrap_or(0) == 2
                    && (Some(n) == nb1 || Some(n) == nb2)
            });
            if let Some(net) = shared {
                partners.insert(a.cid, (b.cid, net));
                partners.insert(b.cid, (a.cid, net));
            }
        }
    }
    partners
}

/// Phân nhóm và sắp xếp thứ tự ưu tiên đặt linh kiện rời (`rComps` trong `placeSpecialComponents`)
pub fn build_special_order(
    ordered_comps: &[CompSpec],
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
    series_partner: &FxHashMap<CompId, (CompId, NetId)>,
    r_order: &[usize],
    p_cfg: &PersonaConfig,
) -> Vec<CompSpec> {
    let all_sp: Vec<&CompSpec> = ordered_comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special()).collect();
    let mut r_order_rank: FxHashMap<CompId, usize> = FxHashMap::default();
    for (pos, &orig_idx) in r_order.iter().enumerate() {
        if let Some(c) = all_sp.get(orig_idx) {
            r_order_rank.insert(c.cid, pos);
        }
    }

    let has_pres = |n_opt: Option<NetId>| -> bool {
        n_opt.is_some_and(|n| ctx.pres.get(&n).is_some_and(|m| m[0] != 0 || m[1] != 0))
    };

    let r_priority = |c: &CompSpec| -> u8 {
        if matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::C | CompType::CC) {
            return 0;
        }
        let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        let p1 = n1.is_some_and(NetId::is_power);
        let p2 = n2.is_some_and(NetId::is_power);
        if !p1 && !p2 && has_pres(n1) && has_pres(n2) {
            return 1;
        }
        let no_partner = !series_partner.contains_key(&c.cid);
        if (p1 && has_pres(n2) && no_partner) || (p2 && has_pres(n1) && no_partner) {
            return 2;
        }
        3
    };

    let mut base_r: Vec<CompSpec> = ordered_comps
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special() && !ctx.locked_decoupling_ids.contains(&c.cid))
        .cloned()
        .collect();

    base_r.sort_by(|a, b| {
        let (pa, pb) = (r_priority(a), r_priority(b));
        if pa != pb {
            return pa.cmp(&pb);
        }
        let ra = r_order_rank.get(&a.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
        let rb = r_order_rank.get(&b.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
        ra.cmp(&rb)
    });

    let mut r_comps = Vec::with_capacity(base_r.len());
    let mut used_r: FxHashSet<CompId> = FxHashSet::default();

    for c in &base_r {
        if !used_r.insert(c.cid) {
            continue;
        }
        let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        let mut group = vec![c.clone()];

        for other in &base_r {
            if used_r.contains(&other.cid) {
                continue;
            }
            let on1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: other.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
            let on2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: other.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
            let share_n1 = n1.is_some_and(|n| !n.is_power() && (on1 == Some(n) || on2 == Some(n)));
            let share_n2 = n2.is_some_and(|n| !n.is_power() && (on1 == Some(n) || on2 == Some(n)));
            if share_n1 || share_n2 {
                used_r.insert(other.cid);
                group.push(other.clone());
            }
        }

        let grp_len = group.len();
        group.sort_by(|a, b| {
            let cap_a = matches!(a.comp_type, CompType::C | CompType::CC);
            let cap_b = matches!(b.comp_type, CompType::C | CompType::CC);
            if cap_a != cap_b {
                return if cap_a { core::cmp::Ordering::Less } else { core::cmp::Ordering::Greater };
            }
            if p_cfg.led_first && grp_len > 1 && (a.comp_type == CompType::LED) != (b.comp_type == CompType::LED) {
                return if a.comp_type == CompType::LED {
                    core::cmp::Ordering::Less
                } else {
                    core::cmp::Ordering::Greater
                };
            }
            let (pa, pb) = (r_priority(a), r_priority(b));
            if pa != pb {
                return pa.cmp(&pb);
            }
            let a_anc = u8::from(!(has_pres(ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: a.cid, pin: 1 }).copied())
                || has_pres(ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: a.cid, pin: 2 }).copied())));
            let b_anc = u8::from(!(has_pres(ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: b.cid, pin: 1 }).copied())
                || has_pres(ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: b.cid, pin: 2 }).copied())));
            a_anc.cmp(&b_anc)
        });
        r_comps.extend(group);
    }
    r_comps
}

/// Khôi phục các linh kiện rời đóng băng hợp lệ trong chế độ Bác sĩ Vi phẫu (`activeFrozen`)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn apply_frozen_specials(
    r_comps: &[CompSpec],
    opts: &SpecialOptions,
    series_partner: &FxHashMap<CompId, (CompId, NetId)>,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &mut BoardLayout,
    ctx: &mut DiscreteContext,
    placed_special_ids: &mut FxHashSet<CompId>,
    all_comps: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
) {
    if opts.frozen_specials.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let is_evicted_col = |c: u8| (opts.evict_cols_mask & (1u64 << c)) != 0;
    let in_pres = |ctx_ref: &DiscreteContext, n: NetId, col: u8, row: i8| -> bool {
        ctx_ref.pres.get(&n).is_some_and(|m| (m[half_of(row) as usize] & (1u64 << col)) != 0)
    };

    let should_evict = |cid: CompId, ctx_ref: &DiscreteContext| -> bool {
        let Some(fz) = opts.frozen_specials.get(&cid) else { return true };
        if fz.locked || ctx_ref.locked_decoupling_ids.contains(&cid) {
            return false;
        }
        if is_evicted_col(fz.c1) || is_evicted_col(fz.c2) {
            return true;
        }
        let n1 = ctx_ref.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = ctx_ref.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        if n1.is_some_and(|n| opts.boost_nets.contains(&n))
            || n2.is_some_and(|n| opts.boost_nets.contains(&n))
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(n) = n1.filter(|n| !n.is_power() && ctx_ref.pres.contains_key(n)) {
            if !is_rail_row(fz.r1) && !in_pres(ctx_ref, n, fz.c1, fz.r1) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        if let Some(n) = n2.filter(|n| !n.is_power() && ctx_ref.pres.contains_key(n)) {
            if !is_rail_row(fz.r2) && !in_pres(ctx_ref, n, fz.c2, fz.r2) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        if let Some(&(pt_id, pt_net)) = series_partner.get(&cid) {
            let Some(pfz) = opts.frozen_specials.get(&pt_id) else { return true };
            if is_evicted_col(pfz.c1) || is_evicted_col(pfz.c2) || opts.boost_nets.contains(&pt_net) {
                return true;
            }
            let a_spots = [
                (!is_rail_row(fz.r1)).then(|| (fz.c1, half_of(fz.r1))),
                (!is_rail_row(fz.r2)).then(|| (fz.c2, half_of(fz.r2))),
            ];
            let b_spots = [
                (!is_rail_row(pfz.r1)).then(|| (pfz.c1, half_of(pfz.r1))),
                (!is_rail_row(pfz.r2)).then(|| (pfz.c2, half_of(pfz.r2))),
            ];
            if !a_spots.iter().flatten().any(|a| b_spots.iter().flatten().any(|b| a == b)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    };

    for c in r_comps {
        if placed_special_ids.contains(&c.cid)
            || ctx.invalid_comp_ids.contains(&c.cid)
            || should_evict(c.cid, ctx)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let fz = &opts.frozen_specials[&c.cid];
        if board.is_occupied(fz.c1, fz.r1) || board.is_occupied(fz.c2, fz.r2) {
            continue;
        }
        let n1 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = ctx.net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        if !is_rail_row(fz.r1) {
            let t1 = n1.map_or(0, |n| pwr_tag(n, c.cid, 1, fz.r1, c.comp_type, &ctx.main_ic_pwr_pins));
            let cur = board.col_net[half_of(fz.r1) as usize][fz.c1 as usize];
            if cur != 0 && cur != t1 {
                continue;
            }
        }
        if !is_rail_row(fz.r2) {
            let t2 = n2.map_or(0, |n| pwr_tag(n, c.cid, 2, fz.r2, c.comp_type, &ctx.main_ic_pwr_pins));
            let cur = board.col_net[half_of(fz.r2) as usize][fz.c2 as usize];
            if cur != 0 && cur != t2 {
                continue;
            }
        }

        board.occupy(fz.c1, fz.r1);
        board.occupy(fz.c2, fz.r2);
        if fz.c1 == fz.c2 {
            if fz.r1 == -2 || fz.r2 == -2 {
                board.occupy(fz.c1, -1);
            }
            if fz.r1 == 11 || fz.r2 == 11 {
                board.occupy(fz.c1, 10);
            }
        }
        if n1.is_none() && !is_rail_row(fz.r1) {
            board.col_net[half_of(fz.r1) as usize][fz.c1 as usize] = nc_tag(c.cid, 1);
        }
        if n2.is_none() && !is_rail_row(fz.r2) {
            board.col_net[half_of(fz.r2) as usize][fz.c2 as usize] = nc_tag(c.cid, 2);
        }
        let placed = PlacedSpecial {
            cid: c.cid,
            id: c.id.clone(),
            comp_type: c.comp_type,
            c1: fz.c1,
            r1: fz.r1,
            c2: fz.c2,
            r2: fz.r2,
            locked: false,
            ic_id: None,
        };
        match c.comp_type {
            CompType::R => layout.rs.push(placed),
            CompType::LED => layout.leds.push(placed),
            _ => layout.cs.push(placed),
        }
        placed_special_ids.insert(c.cid);
        ctx.add_pin(board, c.cid, 1, fz.c1, fz.r1, all_comps, parsed, errors);
        ctx.add_pin(board, c.cid, 2, fz.c2, fz.r2, all_comps, parsed, errors);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::config::personas::get_persona;
    use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
    use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::setup_macro_and_decoupling;
    use crate::macro_place::topology::build_topology_cache;
    use crate::model::circuit::PlacedIc;
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_series_partner_and_special_order() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "L1", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "C1", CompType::C),
        ];
        let txt = "VCC U1:14; GND U1:7; U1:1 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND; U1:2 -> C1 -> GND";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 10, c_max: 16, w: 7 });
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut fps = Vec::new();
        layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);
        let mut errors = Vec::new();
        let ctx = setup_macro_and_decoupling(&mut board, &mut layout, &parsed, &topo, &comps, &CostWeights::default(), &mut errors);

        let (tot_pins, _) = compute_net_stats(&parsed, &ctx);
        let partners = find_series_partners(&comps, &ctx, &tot_pins);
        assert_eq!(partners.get(&CompId(1)).map(|x| x.0), Some(CompId(2)));

        // Với Persona 2 (I3:BridgeLED, led_first = true): C1 đặt trước, sau đó trong nhóm (R1, L1) thì L1 đứng trước R1!
        let ordered = build_special_order(&comps, &ctx, &partners, &[], &get_persona(2));
        let ids: Vec<&str> = ordered.iter().map(|c| c.id.as_str()).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, vec!["C1", "L1", "R1"]);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/macro_place/cost.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::macro_place::topology::{compute_exact_1d_positions, TopologyCache};
use crate::model::bitboard::BoardState;
use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, ParsedNetlist, PlacedIc};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

/// Pha 1 Toán học: Hàm mục tiêu Cầu rãnh thuần túy Z = sum(U_k * L_k) (`pureOrientationBridgeCost`)
pub fn pure_orientation_bridge_cost(
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> f64 {
    let mut by_cid: [Option<&CompSpec>; 256] = [None; 256];
    for c in comps {
        by_cid[c.cid.0 as usize] = Some(c);
    }

    let get_pin_half = |pr: PinRef| -> Option<u8> {
        let c = by_cid[pr.comp.0 as usize]?;
        match c.comp_type {
            CompType::IC => {
                let w = c.pins / 2;
                let r = rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0;
                Some(if (pr.pin <= w) == r { 0 } else { 1 })
            }
            CompType::SW | CompType::BTN2 => {
                let h = half
                    .get(&c.cid)
                    .copied()
                    .map(|v| if v != 0 { 1 } else { 0 })
                    .unwrap_or(if c.comp_type == CompType::SW { 1 } else { 0 });
                Some(h)
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    };

    let (mut bridges, mut top_load, mut bot_load) = (0i32, 0i32, 0i32);
    let mut net_halves: FxHashMap<NetId, (i32, i32)> = FxHashMap::default();

    for net in &parsed.nets {
        if net.id.is_power() {
            continue;
        }
        let (mut t, mut b) = (0i32, 0i32);
        for &pr in &net.pins {
            match get_pin_half(pr) {
                Some(0) => t += 1,
                Some(1) => b += 1,
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        net_halves.insert(net.id, (t, b));
        if t > 0 && b > 0 { bridges += 1; }
        if t >= 2 { top_load += t - 1; }
        if b >= 2 { bot_load += b - 1; }
    }

    let mut r_cross_pen = 0.0;
    for &(n1, n2) in &topo.r_edges {
        let (Some(&(t1, b1)), Some(&(t2, b2))) = (net_halves.get(&n1), net_halves.get(&n2)) else {
            continue;
        };
        let (only_top1, only_bot1) = (t1 > 0 && b1 == 0, b1 > 0 && t1 == 0);
        let (only_top2, only_bot2) = (t2 > 0 && b2 == 0, b2 > 0 && t2 == 0);
        if (only_top1 && only_bot2) || (only_bot1 && only_top2) {
            r_cross_pen += weights.p1_rcross_pen;
        }
    }

    (bridges as f64) * weights.p1_bridge_weight
        + r_cross_pen
        + ((top_load - bot_load).abs() as f64) * weights.p1_load_diff_weight
}

/// Hàm đánh giá tổng hợp: Cầu rãnh + Độ căng dây ngang + Bán kính vàng (`crossCost` trong ic-core-engine.js)
pub fn cross_cost(
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    order_indices: Option<&[usize]>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> f64 {
    let pos_id = compute_exact_1d_positions(order_indices, Some(half), comps);
    let mut by_cid: [Option<&CompSpec>; 256] = [None; 256];
    for c in comps {
        if pos_id[c.cid.0 as usize] > 0 {
            by_cid[c.cid.0 as usize] = Some(c);
        }
    }

    let get_pin_loc = |pr: PinRef| -> Option<(i32, u8)> {
        let c = by_cid[pr.comp.0 as usize]?;
        let base_c = pos_id[c.cid.0 as usize].max(1);
        let p = pr.pin as i32;
        match c.comp_type {
            CompType::IC => {
                let w = (c.pins / 2) as i32;
                let r = rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0;
                let h = if (p <= w) == r { 0 } else { 1 };
                let pin_c = if !r {
                    if p <= w { base_c + p - 1 } else { base_c + (c.pins as i32 - p) }
                } else if p <= w {
                    base_c + w - p
                } else {
                    base_c + p - w - 1
                };
                Some((pin_c, h))
            }
            CompType::SW => {
                let h = half.get(&c.cid).copied().map_or(1, |v| u8::from(v != 0));
                Some((base_c + p - 1, h))
            }
            CompType::BTN2 => {
                let h = half.get(&c.cid).copied().map_or(0, |v| u8::from(v != 0));
                Some((base_c + if p == 1 { 0 } else { 2 }, h))
            }
            _ => None,
        }
    };

    let (mut cost, mut top_load, mut bot_load) = (0.0, 0i32, 0i32);
    let mut net_locs: FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<(i32, u8)>> = FxHashMap::default();

    for net in &parsed.nets {
        if net.id.is_power() {
            continue;
        }
        let (mut t, mut b, mut min_c, mut max_c) = (0i32, 0i32, 999i32, -999i32);
        let mut locs = Vec::with_capacity(net.pins.len());
        for &pr in &net.pins {
            if let Some(loc) = get_pin_loc(pr) {
                locs.push(loc);
                if loc.1 != 0 { b += 1; } else { t += 1; }
                min_c = min_c.min(loc.0);
                max_c = max_c.max(loc.0);
            }
        }
        net_locs.insert(net.id, locs);
        if t > 0 && b > 0 { cost += weights.cross_bridge_pen; }
        if max_c >= min_c { cost += ((max_c - min_c) as f64) * weights.cross_span_weight; }
        if t >= 2 { top_load += t - 1; }
        if b >= 2 { bot_load += b - 1; }
    }

    for &(n1, n2) in &topo.r_edges {
        let (Some(l1), Some(l2)) = (net_locs.get(&n1), net_locs.get(&n2)) else { continue };
        for p1 in l1 {
            for p2 in l2 {
                if p1.1 != p2.1 { cost += weights.cross_rcross_pen; }
                let d_col = (p1.0 - p2.0).abs() as f64;
                if p1.1 == p2.1 && d_col < 3.0 {
                    cost += (3.0 - d_col) * weights.cross_rclose_pen;
                } else {
                    cost += d_col * weights.cross_rdist_weight;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    cost += ((top_load - bot_load).abs() as f64) * weights.p1_load_diff_weight;

    let (mut top_io, mut bot_io) = (0i32, 0i32);
    for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_io()) {
        let h = half.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(if c.comp_type == CompType::SW { 1 } else { 0 });
        if h != 0 { bot_io += 1; } else { top_io += 1; }
    }
    let io_diff = (top_io - bot_io).abs();
    if io_diff > 1 {
        cost += ((io_diff - 1) as f64) * weights.cross_io_imbalance_pen;
    }
    cost
}

/// Tính nhu cầu Khe Cầu Rãnh thực tế (`computeBridgeDemand` trong ic-core-engine.js)
pub fn compute_bridge_demand(
    items: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> FxHashMap<CompId, f64> {
    let order_indices: Vec<usize> = items
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|it| comps.iter().position(|x| x.cid == it.cid))
        .collect();
    let pos_id = compute_exact_1d_positions(Some(&order_indices), Some(half), comps);

    let mut by_cid: [Option<&CompSpec>; 256] = [None; 256];
    let mut demand: FxHashMap<CompId, f64> = FxHashMap::default();
    for c in items {
        if pos_id[c.cid.0 as usize] > 0 {
            by_cid[c.cid.0 as usize] = Some(c);
        }
        let load = topo.ic_periph_load.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) as f64;
        demand.insert(c.cid, load * weights.bridge_demand_periph_scale);
    }

    let ic_list: Vec<&CompSpec> = items.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC).collect();
    if ic_list.is_empty() {
        return demand;
    }

    for net in &parsed.nets {
        if net.id.is_power() {
            continue;
        }
        let (mut has_top, mut has_bot, mut min_c, mut max_c) = (false, false, 999i32, -999i32);
        for &pr in &net.pins {
            let Some(c) = by_cid[pr.comp.0 as usize] else { continue };
            let base_c = pos_id[c.cid.0 as usize].max(1);
            let p = pr.pin as i32;
            if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
                let w = (c.pins / 2) as i32;
                let r = rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0;
                if (p <= w) == r { has_top = true; } else { has_bot = true; }
                let pin_c = if !r {
                    if p <= w { base_c + p - 1 } else { base_c + (c.pins as i32 - p) }
                } else if p <= w {
                    base_c + w - p
                } else {
                    base_c + p - w - 1
                };
                min_c = min_c.min(pin_c);
                max_c = max_c.max(pin_c);
            } else if c.comp_type.is_io() {
                let h = half
                    .get(&c.cid)
                    .copied()
                    .map_or(if c.comp_type == CompType::SW { 1 } else { 0 }, |v| u8::from(v != 0));
                if h == 0 { has_top = true; } else { has_bot = true; }
                min_c = min_c.min(base_c);
                max_c = max_c.max(base_c);
            }
        }
        if has_top && has_bot && max_c >= min_c {
            for ic in &ic_list {
                let gap_col = pos_id[ic.cid.0 as usize].max(1) + (ic.pins as i32) / 2;
                if gap_col >= min_c - 2 && gap_col <= max_c + 2 {
                    *demand.entry(ic.cid).or_insert(0.0) += weights.bridge_demand_cross_net_add;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    demand
}

/// Chọn cặp chân đại diện (bestT, bestB) gần Khe Cầu Rãnh Trống nhất (`pickBestCrossPair`)
pub fn pick_best_cross_pair(
    top_r: &[[i8; 2]],
    bot_r: &[[i8; 2]],
    placed_ics: &[PlacedIc],
    board: &BoardState,
    net_tag: u16,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> ([i8; 2], [i8; 2], i32) {
    let mut ic_occupied_col = [false; 65];
    for ic in placed_ics {
        for c in ic.c_min..=ic.c_max {
            if (c as usize) < ic_occupied_col.len() {
                ic_occupied_col[c as usize] = true;
            }
        }
    }

    let mut open_bridge_cols = [0i32; 64];
    let mut open_len = 0usize;
    for c in 1u8..=63 {
        let cu = c as usize;
        if ic_occupied_col[cu] {
            continue;
        }
        let (n0, n1) = (board.col_net[0][cu], board.col_net[1][cu]);
        if !board.is_occupied(c, 4)
            && !board.is_occupied(c, 5)
            && (n0 == 0 || n0 == net_tag)
            && (n1 == 0 || n1 == net_tag)
        {
            open_bridge_cols[open_len] = c as i32;
            open_len += 1;
        }
    }

    let (mut best_t, mut best_b, mut min_d) = (top_r[0], bot_r[0], 1e9_f64);
    for &t in top_r {
        for &b in bot_r {
            let (tc, bc) = (t[0] as i32, b[0] as i32);
            let span = (tc - bc).abs() as f64;
            let mut gap_dist = 0.0;
            if open_len > 0 {
                let mut best_g = 999i32;
                for &g in &open_bridge_cols[..open_len] {
                    best_g = best_g.min((tc - g).abs() + (bc - g).abs());
                }
                gap_dist = (best_g as f64) * weights.pick_cross_gap_dist_scale;
            }
            let d = span + gap_dist;
            if d < min_d {
                min_d = d;
                best_t = t;
                best_b = b;
            }
        }
    }
    (best_t, best_b, ((best_t[0] as i32) - (best_b[0] as i32)).abs())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::macro_place::topology::{build_topology_cache, infer_optimal_btn_halves};
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_topology_fm_and_cost_functions() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(1), "U2", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "B1", CompType::BTN2),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "S1", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(5), "L1", CompType::LED),
        ];
        let txt = "
            VCC U1:14 U2:14; GND U1:7 U2:7
            U1:1 -> U2:13
            U1:2 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND
            B1:1 -> R1; B1:2 -> GND
            S1:2 -> U2:1; S1:1 -> VCC; S1:3 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        assert_eq!(topo.r_edges.len(), 1);
        assert!(topo.ic_periph_load.get(&CompId(0)).copied().unwrap_or(0) > 0);

        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let mut rot = FxHashMap::default();
        rot.insert(CompId(0), 0);
        rot.insert(CompId(1), 0);

        let half = infer_optimal_btn_halves(&parsed, &topo, &rot, &comps, &weights);
        assert_eq!(half.len(), 2);

        let pos = compute_exact_1d_positions(None, Some(&half), &comps);
        assert_eq!(pos[0], 1);
        assert_eq!(pos[1], 10);

        let p1_cost = pure_orientation_bridge_cost(&parsed, &topo, &rot, &half, &comps, &weights);
        let full_cost = cross_cost(&parsed, &topo, &rot, &half, None, &comps, &weights);
        assert!(p1_cost > 0.0);
        assert!(full_cost > p1_cost);

        let demand = compute_bridge_demand(&comps, &parsed, &topo, &rot, &half, &comps, &weights);
        assert!(demand.get(&CompId(0)).copied().unwrap_or(0.0) > 0.0);
    }
}
