/// Chọn `k` phương án tốt nhất và đa dạng nhất từ `pool` (`pickTop` trong main.html)
pub fn pick_top_solutions(pool: &[BoardSolution], k: usize) -> Vec<BoardSolution> {
    let mut sorted = pool.to_vec();
    sorted.sort_by(cmp_solutions);
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(k);
    let mut used_layout = FxHashSet::default();
    let mut used_persona = FxHashSet::default();
    let mut picked_sigs = FxHashSet::default();

    for r in &sorted {
        if !used_layout.contains(&r.layout_hash) && !used_persona.contains(r.persona.as_str()) {
            used_layout.insert(r.layout_hash);
            used_persona.insert(r.persona.as_str());
            picked_sigs.insert(r.sig);
            out.push(r.clone());
            if out.len() == k {
                return out;
            }
        }
    }
    for r in &sorted {
        if used_layout.insert(r.layout_hash) && picked_sigs.insert(r.sig) {
            out.push(r.clone());
            if out.len() == k {
                return out;
            }
        }
    }
    for r in sorted {
        if out.len() >= k {
            break;
        }
        if picked_sigs.insert(r.sig) {
            out.push(r);
        }
    }
    out
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct BatchStepResult {
    pub improved: bool,
    pub iterations: u32,
    pub best_score: f64,
    pub best_err_count: usize,
    pub best_unplaced: usize,
    pub best_ortho: usize,
    pub focus: String,
    pub tabu_bans: u32,
    pub island_status: String,
}

/// Bộ điều phối Tiến hóa Đa Quần Đảo & Vi Phẫu (`solveAsync` trong main.html)
pub struct MetaSolver {
    pub comps: Vec<CompSpec>,
    pub parsed: ParsedNetlist,
    pub topo: TopologyCache,
    pub weights: CostWeights,
    pub use_star_hub: bool,
    pub pool: Vec<BoardSolution>,
    pub pool_seen: FxHashSet<u64>,
    pub island_pools: [Vec<BoardSolution>; NUM_ISLANDS],
    pub island_stall: [u32; NUM_ISLANDS],
    pub todo: VecDeque<CandidateOpts>,
    pub handover_queue: VecDeque<CandidateOpts>,
    pub handover_seen: FxHashSet<u64>,
    pub tabu: TabuBasin,
    pub agent: MetaAgent,
    pub rl: RlBandit,
    pub kb: KnowledgeBank,
    pub logger: SearchLogger,
    pub rng: Mulberry32,
    pub it: u32,
    pub stall: u32,
    pub rr_tick: usize,
    place_comp_count: usize,
    r_comp_count: usize,
    go: Vec<usize>,
    go_interleaved: Vec<usize>,
    asg: Vec<MacroAssignment>,
    asg_inter: Vec<MacroAssignment>,
}

impl MetaSolver {
    pub fn new(
        comps: Vec<CompSpec>,
        netlist_text: &str,
        use_star_hub: bool,
        seed: u32,
        mut kb: KnowledgeBank,
    ) -> Self {
        let parsed = parse_netlist(netlist_text, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let r_comp_count = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special()).count();
        let place_comp_count = comps.len() - r_comp_count;
        let mut rl = RlBandit::new(comps.len());
        let mut rng = Mulberry32::new(seed);

        let go = conn_order(&parsed, &topo, false, None, None, &comps, &weights);
        let go_interleaved = conn_order(&parsed, &topo, true, None, None, &comps, &weights);
        let asg = best_assignments(&parsed, &topo, 16, &go, &comps, &weights);
        let asg_inter = best_assignments(&parsed, &topo, 10, &go_interleaved, &comps, &weights);

        // Hạng mục #3: Đồng bộ default_half chia đều đối xứng 2 nửa bo cho cả SW và BTN2 (is_io())
        let (mut user_rot, mut default_half, mut io_idx) =
            (FxHashMap::default(), FxHashMap::default(), 0usize);
        for c in &comps {
            if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
                user_rot.insert(c.cid, c.rot);
            } else if c.comp_type.is_io() {
                default_half.insert(c.cid, (io_idx % 2) as u8);
                io_idx += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut todo = VecDeque::new();
        for (i, mut s) in kb
            .build_warm_start_queue(&mut rl, &go, place_comp_count, r_comp_count, &mut rng, &comps)
            .into_iter()
            .enumerate()
        {
            s.island = i % NUM_ISLANDS;
            s.persona = i % NUM_ISLANDS;
            s.use_star_hub = use_star_hub;
            todo.push_back(s);
        }

        let idx_all: Vec<usize> = (0..comps.len()).collect();
        for (isl, ord, sr) in [
            (0, go.clone(), 1),
            (1, go_interleaved.clone(), 2),
            (2, go.clone(), 3),
            (3, idx_all, 4),
        ] {
            todo.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                order: ord,
                rot: user_rot.clone(),
                half: default_half.clone(),
                gap: Some(1),
                island: isl,
                persona: isl,
                sr: Some(sr),
                use_star_hub,
                phase: SearchPhase::Init,
                ..Default::default()
            });
        }
        for (i, a) in asg.iter().enumerate() {
            let isl = match i % 3 {
                0 => 0,
                1 => 2,
                _ => 3,
            };
            let use_ord = if a.order.is_empty() { go.clone() } else { a.order.clone() };
            let isl2 = (isl + 2) % NUM_ISLANDS;
            todo.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                order: use_ord.clone(),
                rot: a.rot.clone(),
                half: a.half.clone(),
                gap: Some(1),
                island: isl,
                persona: isl,
                sr: Some(10 + i as u32),
                use_star_hub,
                phase: SearchPhase::Init,
                ..Default::default()
            });
            todo.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                order: use_ord,
                rot: a.rot.clone(),
                half: a.half.clone(),
                gap: Some(2),
                island: isl2,
                persona: isl2,
                sr: Some(40 + i as u32),
                net_ord: 1,
                use_star_hub,
                phase: SearchPhase::Init,
                ..Default::default()
            });
        }
        for (i, a) in asg_inter.iter().enumerate() {
            let use_ord = if a.order.is_empty() {
                go_interleaved.clone()
            } else {
                a.order.clone()
            };
            for (gap, sr_base, net_ord) in [(1, 80u32, 0usize), (2, 100u32, 2usize)] {
                todo.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                    order: use_ord.clone(),
                    rot: a.rot.clone(),
                    half: a.half.clone(),
                    gap: Some(gap),
                    island: 1,
                    persona: 1,
                    sr: Some(sr_base + i as u32),
                    net_ord,
                    use_star_hub,
                    phase: SearchPhase::Init,
                    ..Default::default()
                });
            }
        }

        let logger = SearchLogger::new(parsed.nets.len());
        kb.rebuild_flat_seeds();
        Self {
            comps,
            parsed,
            topo,
            weights,
            use_star_hub,
            pool: Vec::new(),
            pool_seen: FxHashSet::default(),
            island_pools: Default::default(),
            island_stall: [0; NUM_ISLANDS],
            todo,
            handover_queue: VecDeque::new(),
            handover_seen: FxHashSet::default(),
            tabu: TabuBasin::new(),
            agent: MetaAgent::default(),
            rl,
            kb,
            logger,
            rng,
            it: 0,
            stall: 0,
            rr_tick: 0,
            place_comp_count,
            r_comp_count,
            go,
            go_interleaved,
            asg,
            asg_inter,
        }
    }

    pub fn next_candidate(&mut self) -> CandidateOpts {
        if let Some(mut ho) = self.handover_queue.pop_front() {
            ho.use_star_hub = self.use_star_hub;
            return ho;
        }
        if let Some(mut o) = self.todo.pop_front() {
            o.use_star_hub = self.use_star_hub;
            return o;
        }

        let isl = self.rl.pick_island(self.rr_tick, self.agent.focus, &mut self.rng);
        self.rr_tick += 1;
        let i_stall = self.island_stall[isl];
        let sub_len = self.island_pools[isl].len();
        let top_window = sub_len.min(if i_stall > 30 { 16 } else { 6 });

        if top_window > 0 && self.rng.next_f64() < 0.80 {
            let pick_non_tabu = |rng: &mut Mulberry32,
                                 pool_slice: &[BoardSolution],
                                 win: usize,
                                 tabu: &TabuBasin,
                                 comps: &[CompSpec],
                                 cur_it: u32|
             -> usize {
                let mut idx = rng.next_usize(win);
                for _ in 0..4 {
                    let o = &pool_slice[idx].opts;
                    if !tabu.is_tabu(tabu_key(&o.order, &o.rot, &o.half, comps), cur_it) {
                        return idx;
                    }
                    idx = rng.next_usize(pool_slice.len());
                }
                idx
            };
            let p1_idx = pick_non_tabu(
                &mut self.rng, &self.island_pools[isl], top_window, &self.tabu, &self.comps, self.it,
            );
            let p2_idx = pick_non_tabu(
                &mut self.rng, &self.island_pools[isl], top_window, &self.tabu, &self.comps, self.it,
            );
            let p1 = self.island_pools[isl][p1_idx].clone();
            let p2 = self.island_pools[isl][p2_idx].clone();
            let (parent, fallback_mate) = if cmp_solutions(&p1, &p2).is_le() { (p1, p2) } else { (p2, p1) };
            let other_isl = (isl + 1 + self.rng.next_usize(NUM_ISLANDS - 1)) % NUM_ISLANDS;
            let mate = if !self.island_pools[other_isl].is_empty() && self.rng.next_f64() < 0.25 {
                self.island_pools[other_isl][0].clone()
            } else {
                fallback_mate
            };
            let mut o = mutate_candidate(
                &parent.opts, Some(&parent), i_stall, Some(&mate), isl, self.it,
                self.place_comp_count, self.r_comp_count, &self.agent, &self.tabu, &self.rl, &self.comps, &self.parsed, &mut self.rng,
            );
            if o.phase == SearchPhase::P1Unblock {
                polish_one_ic(
                    &mut o, self.agent.focus, &self.comps, &self.parsed, &self.topo, &self.weights, &mut self.rng,
                );
            }
            o.use_star_hub = self.use_star_hub;
            return o;
        }

        if !self.kb.seeds.is_empty() && self.rng.next_f64() < 0.30 && self.agent.focus != AgentFocus::Macro {
            let seed_dna = self.kb.seeds[self.rng.next_usize(self.kb.seeds.len().min(10))].clone();
            let fb_ord = if isl == 1 { &self.go_interleaved } else { &self.go };
            let mapped = self.kb.to_opts(
                &seed_dna, fb_ord, self.place_comp_count, self.r_comp_count, &mut self.rng, false, &self.comps,
            );
            if !self.tabu.is_tabu(tabu_key(&mapped.order, &mapped.rot, &mapped.half, &self.comps), self.it) {
                let mut o = mutate_candidate(
                    &mapped, None, 0, None, isl, self.it,
                    self.place_comp_count, self.r_comp_count, &self.agent, &self.tabu, &self.rl, &self.comps, &self.parsed, &mut self.rng,
                );
                o.use_star_hub = self.use_star_hub;
                return o;
            }
        }

        let base_ord = if isl == 1 { &self.go_interleaved } else { &self.go };
        let a_list = if isl == 1 && !self.asg_inter.is_empty() { &self.asg_inter } else { &self.asg };
        let picked_a = (!a_list.is_empty() && self.rng.next_f64() < 0.55)
            .then(|| a_list[self.rng.next_usize(a_list.len())].clone());
        let order = if self.rng.next_f64() < 0.48 {
            base_ord.clone()
        } else if self.rng.next_f64() < 0.70 {
            self.rl.build_guided_order(&self.comps, &mut self.rng)
        } else {
            let mut v: Vec<usize> = (0..self.comps.len()).collect();
            self.rng.shuffle(&mut v);
            v
        };

        let (rot, half) = if let Some(a) = picked_a {
            (a.rot, a.half)
        } else {
            let (mut r_map, mut h_map) = (FxHashMap::default(), FxHashMap::default());
            for c in &self.comps {
                if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
                    r_map.insert(
                        c.cid,
                        self.rl.sample_binary(self.rl.q_rot[c.cid.0 as usize], 0.30, &mut self.rng),
                    );
                } else if c.comp_type.is_io() {
                    h_map.insert(
                        c.cid,
                        self.rl.sample_binary(self.rl.q_half[c.cid.0 as usize], 0.30, &mut self.rng),
                    );
                }
            }
            (r_map, h_map)
        };
        let mut r_order: Vec<usize> = (0..self.r_comp_count).collect();
        self.rng.shuffle(&mut r_order);

        CandidateOpts {
            island: isl,
            persona: isl,
            order,
            rot,
            half,
            r_order,
            dyn_br_pen: self.agent.dyn_br_pen,
            dyn_aux_share: self.agent.dyn_aux_share,
            rev: self.rng.next_f64() < 0.5,
            net_ord: self.rng.next_usize(18),
            sr: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
            r_seed: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
            use_star_hub: self.use_star_hub,
            phase: SearchPhase::Diversify,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    fn check_and_break_stall(&mut self, isl: usize) {
        if self.island_stall[isl] > 55 {
            if let Some(isl_top) = self.island_pools[isl].first().filter(|s| !s.errors.is_empty()) {
                let tk = tabu_key(&isl_top.opts.order, &isl_top.opts.rot, &isl_top.opts.half, &self.comps);
                self.tabu.force_ban(tk, self.it);
            }
            self.island_stall[isl] = 10;
        }
        if self.stall > 110 {
            if let Some(top) = self.pool.first().filter(|s| !s.errors.is_empty()).cloned() {
                let tk = tabu_key(&top.opts.order, &top.opts.rot, &top.opts.half, &self.comps);
                self.tabu.force_ban(tk, self.it);
                self.rl.penalize_force_ban(&top.opts.order, &top.opts.rot, &self.comps);
                for k in 0..4 {
                    let mut jumped = top.opts.clone();
                    jumped.island = k % NUM_ISLANDS;
                    jumped.persona = k % NUM_ISLANDS;
                    jumped.sr = Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
                    jumped.r_seed = Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
                    apply_macro_jump(&mut jumped, self.agent.focus, &self.comps, &mut self.rng);
                    self.handover_queue.push_back(jumped);
                }
            }
            self.stall = 15;
        }
    }

    pub fn ingest_result(&mut self, sol: BoardSolution, elapsed_ms: u64) -> bool {
        let o = sol.opts.clone();
        self.it += 1;
        if self.it.is_multiple_of(40) {
            let br_cnt = self.logger.best_snap.as_ref().map_or(9, |s| s.bridges.len());
            let err_opt = self.logger.best_snap.as_ref().map(|s| s.err_count);
            self.agent.reflect(self.stall, br_cnt, err_opt);
        }

        let isl = o.island % NUM_ISLANDS;
        let isl_improved = self.island_pools[isl].first().is_none_or(|best| cmp_solutions(&sol, best).is_lt());
        let t_key = tabu_key(&o.order, &o.rot, &o.half, &self.comps);
        let newly_tabu = sol.unplaced == 0
            && !sol.errors.is_empty()
            && sol.errors.len() <= 3
            && self.tabu.record(t_key, sol.score, self.it);

        self.rl.update(
            sol.unplaced, sol.errors.len(), sol.score, o.parent_score, isl_improved,
            newly_tabu, isl, o.arm, &o.order, &o.rot, &o.half, &self.comps,
        );

        let is_dup = self.pool_seen.contains(&sol.sig);
        self.logger.record(&sol, &o, self.it, elapsed_ms, is_dup);
        if is_dup {
            self.stall += 1;
            self.island_stall[isl] += 1;
            self.check_and_break_stall(isl);
            return false;
        }

        if sol.unplaced == 0
            && !sol.errors.is_empty()
            && sol.errors.len() <= 3
            && !o.handed_over
            && !self.tabu.is_tabu(t_key, self.it)
            && self.handover_queue.len() < 12
        {
            let m_sig = macro_handover_sig(
                &o.order, &o.rot, &o.half, o.gaps.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[]), sol.errors.len(), &self.comps,
            );
            if self.handover_seen.insert(m_sig) {
                if self.handover_seen.len() > 2000 {
                    self.handover_seen.clear();
                }
                let frozen_map = extract_frozen_map(&sol);
                let (boost_nets, evict_mask, _) = parse_err_info(&sol.errors, &self.parsed);

                self.handover_queue.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                    island: isl, persona: 4, passes: Some(32), lock_gaps: o.gaps.is_some(),
                    frozen_specials: frozen_map.clone(), evict_cols_mask: 0, boost_nets: boost_nets.clone(),
                    dyn_br_pen: self.agent.dyn_br_pen, dyn_aux_share: self.agent.dyn_aux_share,
                    net_ord: self.rng.next_usize(25), sr: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
                    r_seed: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32), phase: SearchPhase::P1Bridge,
                    handed_over: true, parent_score: Some(sol.score), ..o.clone()
                });
                if evict_mask != 0 {
                    self.handover_queue.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                        island: isl, persona: 4, passes: Some(32), lock_gaps: o.gaps.is_some(),
                        frozen_specials: frozen_map, evict_cols_mask: evict_mask, boost_nets: boost_nets.clone(),
                        dyn_br_pen: Some(40.0), dyn_aux_share: Some(25.0), net_ord: self.rng.next_usize(25),
                        rev: !o.rev, sr: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
                        r_seed: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32), phase: SearchPhase::P1Bridge,
                        handed_over: true, parent_score: Some(sol.score), ..o.clone()
                    });
                }
                let alt_isl = if isl == 2 { 3 } else { 2 };
                self.handover_queue.push_back(CandidateOpts {
                    island: alt_isl, persona: alt_isl, passes: Some(26), lock_gaps: o.gaps.is_some(),
                    frozen_specials: Default::default(), evict_cols_mask: 0, boost_nets,
                    dyn_br_pen: Some(38.0), dyn_aux_share: Some(24.0), net_ord: self.rng.next_usize(25),
                    sr: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32), r_seed: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
                    phase: SearchPhase::P1Bridge, handed_over: true, parent_score: Some(sol.score), ..o
                });
            }
        }

        if self.pool_seen.len() > 12000 {
            self.pool_seen.clear();
        }
        self.pool_seen.insert(sol.sig);

        let sub = &mut self.island_pools[isl];
        sub.push(sol.clone());
        sub.sort_by(cmp_solutions);
        sub.truncate(25);
        if isl_improved {
            self.island_stall[isl] = 0;
        } else {
            self.island_stall[isl] += 1;
        }

        let imp = self.pool.first().is_none_or(|best| cmp_solutions(&sol, best).is_lt());
        self.pool.push(sol.clone());
        self.pool.sort_by(cmp_solutions);
        self.pool.truncate(80);

        if imp {
            self.stall = 0;
            let br_cnt = self.logger.best_snap.as_ref().map_or(9, |s| s.bridges.len());
            self.agent.reflect(0, br_cnt, Some(sol.errors.len()));
            if sol.unplaced == 0 && sol.errors.len() <= 3 {
                if let Some(dna) = KnowledgeBank::extract_from_solution(&sol) {
                    self.kb.add_seed(dna);
                }
            }
        } else {
            self.stall += 1;
            self.check_and_break_stall(isl);
        }
        imp
    }

    pub fn format_island_status(&self) -> String {
        self.island_pools
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, sp)| match sp.first() {
                None => format!("I{}:-", i + 1),
                Some(s) if s.unplaced > 0 => format!("I{}:thiếu", i + 1),
                Some(s) => format!("I{}:{}L", i + 1, s.errors.len()),
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" · ")
    }

    pub fn step_batch(&mut self, batch_size: usize, elapsed_ms: u64) -> BatchStepResult {
        let mut any_improved = false;
        for _ in 0..batch_size.max(1) {
            let mut cand = self.next_candidate();
            let sol = build_board(&mut cand, &self.comps, &self.parsed, &self.topo, &self.weights);
            if self.ingest_result(sol, elapsed_ms) {
                any_improved = true;
            }
        }
        let best = self.pool.first();
        BatchStepResult {
            improved: any_improved,
            iterations: self.it,
            best_score: best.map_or(f64::INFINITY, |s| s.score),
            best_err_count: best.map_or(0, |s| s.errors.len()),
            best_unplaced: best.map_or(0, |s| s.unplaced),
            best_ortho: best.map_or(0, |s| s.ortho_count),
            focus: self.agent.focus.as_str().to_string(),
            tabu_bans: self.tabu.ban_count,
            island_status: self.format_island_status(),
        }
    }

    pub fn set_steer_mode(&mut self, mode: SteerMode) {
        self.agent.mode = mode;
        let br_cnt = self.logger.best_snap.as_ref().map_or(9, |s| s.bridges.len());
        let err_opt = self.logger.best_snap.as_ref().map(|s| s.err_count);
        self.agent.reflect(self.stall, br_cnt, err_opt);
    }

    pub fn trigger_tabu_jump(&mut self, tab_idx: usize) {
        let top = pick_top_solutions(&self.pool, 5);
        let Some(cur_sol) = top.get(tab_idx).or_else(|| self.pool.first()) else { return };
        let tk = tabu_key(&cur_sol.opts.order, &cur_sol.opts.rot, &cur_sol.opts.half, &self.comps);
        self.tabu.force_ban(tk, self.it);
        self.rl.penalize_force_ban(&cur_sol.opts.order, &cur_sol.opts.rot, &self.comps);
        self.handover_queue.clear();
        for k in 0..8 {
            let mut jumped = cur_sol.opts.clone();
            jumped.island = k % NUM_ISLANDS;
            jumped.persona = k % NUM_ISLANDS;
            jumped.sr = Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
            jumped.r_seed = Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
            apply_macro_jump(&mut jumped, self.agent.focus, &self.comps, &mut self.rng);
            self.handover_queue.push_back(jumped);
        }
        self.stall = 0;
    }

    pub fn trigger_deep_surgery(&mut self, tab_idx: usize) {
        let top = pick_top_solutions(&self.pool, 5);
        let Some(cur_sol) = top.get(tab_idx).or_else(|| self.pool.first()) else { return };
        let frozen_map = extract_frozen_map(cur_sol);
        let (boost_nets, evict_mask, _) = parse_err_info(&cur_sol.errors, &self.parsed);
        for k in (0..6).rev() {
            self.handover_queue.push_front(CandidateOpts {
                persona: if k < 4 { 4 } else { 2 },
                passes: Some(36),
                lock_gaps: true,
                frozen_specials: if k % 2 == 0 { frozen_map.clone() } else { Default::default() },
                evict_cols_mask: evict_mask,
                boost_nets: boost_nets.clone(),
                dyn_br_pen: Some(42.0),
                dyn_aux_share: Some(26.0),
                net_ord: self.rng.next_usize(30),
                rev: k % 2 == 1,
                sr: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
                r_seed: Some(self.rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
                phase: SearchPhase::P1Bridge,
                handed_over: true,
                parent_score: Some(cur_sol.score),
                ..cur_sol.opts.clone()
            });
        }
    }

    pub fn pick_top(&self, k: usize) -> Vec<BoardSolution> {
        pick_top_solutions(&self.pool, k)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::ids::CompId;

    #[test]
    fn test_meta_solver_default_half_balances_sw_and_btn2() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "S1", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "S2", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "S3", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "S4", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(5), "B1", CompType::BTN2),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(6), "B2", CompType::BTN2),
        ];
        let txt = "VCC U1:14; GND U1:7; S1:2 -> U1:1; S2:2 -> U1:2; S3:2 -> U1:3; S4:2 -> U1:4; B1:1 -> U1:5; B2:1 -> U1:6";
        let solver = MetaSolver::new(comps, txt, true, 42, KnowledgeBank::new());

        let first_init = solver.todo.front().expect("todo queue must have initial candidates");
        assert_eq!(first_init.half.len(), 6);
        let top_cnt = (1..=6).filter(|&id| first_init.half.get(&CompId(id)) == Some(&0)).count();
        let bot_cnt = (1..=6).filter(|&id| first_init.half.get(&CompId(id)) == Some(&1)).count();
        assert_eq!((top_cnt, bot_cnt), (3, 3));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/knowledge.rs ===
use crate::meta_search::builder::{BoardSolution, CandidateOpts, SearchPhase};
use crate::meta_search::rl_bandit::RlBandit;
use crate::model::circuit::CompSpec;
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, Mulberry32};
use core::cmp::Ordering;
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[inline]
fn is_ic_id(id: &str) -> bool {
    id.starts_with('U') || id.starts_with("IC")
}

fn hash_dna_variant(
    macro_ids: &[String],
    rot: &FxHashMap<String, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<String, u8>,
    gaps: Option<&Vec<i32>>,
    ic_gaps: &[i32],
) -> u64 {
    let mut h = 0xcbf29ce484222325_u64;
    let mut mix = |b: u8| {
        h ^= b as u64;
        h = h.wrapping_mul(0x00000100000001b3);
    };
    for id in macro_ids {
        for &b in id.as_bytes() {
            mix(b);
        }
        mix(b'|');
        if is_ic_id(id) {
            mix(rot.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(0));
        } else {
            mix(half.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(0));
        }
    }
    if let Some(gs) = gaps {
        for &g in gs {
            mix((g.clamp(0, 15) as u8) | 0x80);
        }
    }
    for &ig in ic_gaps {
        mix((ig.clamp(0, 15) as u8) | 0x40);
    }
    h
}

/// Cấu trúc DNA 3 tầng của một bố cục (`dna` trong `KnowledgeBank` - main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BackboneDna {
    pub macro_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub ic_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub io_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub ic_key: String,
    pub var_sig: u64,
    pub ic_gaps: Vec<i32>,
    pub gaps: Option<Vec<i32>>,
    pub rot: FxHashMap<String, u8>,
    pub half: FxHashMap<String, u8>,
    pub r_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub err_count: usize,
    pub ortho_count: usize,
    pub score: f64,
    pub arch_code: String,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ArchetypeGroup {
    pub ic_key: String,
    pub ic_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub rot: FxHashMap<String, u8>,
    pub arch_code: String,
    pub variants: Vec<BackboneDna>,
}

/// Kho Tri Thức Học Phân Tầng 3 Cấp (`KnowledgeBank` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct KnowledgeBank {
    pub archetypes: FxHashMap<String, ArchetypeGroup>,
    pub seeds: Vec<BackboneDna>,
}

impl KnowledgeBank {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn cmp_dna(a: &BackboneDna, b: &BackboneDna) -> Ordering {
        if a.err_count != b.err_count {
            return a.err_count.cmp(&b.err_count);
        }
        if b.ortho_count != a.ortho_count {
            return b.ortho_count.cmp(&a.ortho_count);
        }
        a.score.total_cmp(&b.score)
    }

    pub fn normalize_dna(mut dna: BackboneDna) -> Option<BackboneDna> {
        if dna.macro_ids.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        dna.ic_ids = dna.macro_ids.iter().filter(|id| is_ic_id(id)).cloned().collect();
        if dna.ic_ids.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        dna.io_ids = dna.macro_ids.iter().filter(|id| !is_ic_id(id)).cloned().collect();
        dna.ic_key = dna
            .ic_ids
            .iter()
            .map(|id| format!("{id}:{}", if dna.rot.get(id).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0 { "R" } else { "0" }))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(" -> ");
        dna.var_sig = hash_dna_variant(&dna.macro_ids, &dna.rot, &dna.half, dna.gaps.as_ref(), &dna.ic_gaps);
        if dna.score == 0.0 && dna.err_count > 0 {
            dna.score = (dna.err_count as f64) * 1e8;
        }
        Some(dna)
    }

    pub fn rebuild_flat_seeds(&mut self) {
        let mut groups: Vec<&mut ArchetypeGroup> = self.archetypes.values_mut().collect();
        groups.sort_by(|a, b| Self::cmp_dna(&a.variants[0], &b.variants[0]));
        for (idx, g) in groups.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            g.arch_code = format!("IC-DNA #{}", idx + 1);
        }
        let mut flat = Vec::with_capacity(80);
        for tier in 0..2 {
            for g in &mut groups {
                if let Some(v) = g.variants.get_mut(tier) {
                    v.arch_code = g.arch_code.clone();
                    flat.push(v.clone());
                }
            }
        }
        flat.truncate(80);
        self.seeds = flat;
    }

    pub fn add_seed(&mut self, raw_dna: BackboneDna) -> bool {
        let Some(dna) = Self::normalize_dna(raw_dna) else { return false };
        let grp = self.archetypes.entry(dna.ic_key.clone()).or_insert_with(|| ArchetypeGroup {
            ic_key: dna.ic_key.clone(),
            ic_ids: dna.ic_ids.clone(),
            rot: dna.rot.clone(),
            arch_code: String::new(),
            variants: Vec::new(),
        });
        if grp.variants.iter().any(|v| v.var_sig == dna.var_sig) {
            return false;
        }
        grp.variants.push(dna);
        grp.variants.sort_by(Self::cmp_dna);
        grp.variants.truncate(2);

        if self.archetypes.len() > 36 {
            let worst_key = self
                .archetypes
                .iter()
                .max_by(|a, b| Self::cmp_dna(&a.1.variants[0], &b.1.variants[0]))
                .map(|(k, _)| k.clone());
            if let Some(k) = worst_key {
                self.archetypes.remove(&k);
            }
        }
        self.rebuild_flat_seeds();
        true
    }

    pub fn extract_from_solution(sol: &BoardSolution) -> Option<BackboneDna> {
        let mut ics_sorted = sol.layout.ics.clone();
        ics_sorted.sort_by_key(|x| x.c_min);
        let mut ic_gaps = Vec::with_capacity(ics_sorted.len().saturating_sub(1));
        for w in ics_sorted.windows(2) {
            ic_gaps.push((w[1].c_min as i32 - w[0].c_max as i32 - 1).max(0));
        }
        let mut placed: Vec<(u8, String, u8, u8)> = Vec::new();
        for x in &ics_sorted {
            placed.push((x.c_min, x.id.clone(), 0, u8::from(x.rot != 0)));
        }
        for x in &sol.layout.btns {
            placed.push((x.c_min, x.id.clone(), 1, u8::from(x.row_top > 4)));
        }
        for x in &sol.layout.sws {
            placed.push((x.c_min, x.id.clone(), 2, 0));
        }
        if placed.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        placed.sort_by_key(|p| p.0);

        let mut rot = FxHashMap::default();
        let mut half = FxHashMap::default();
        let mut macro_ids = Vec::with_capacity(placed.len());
        for (_, id, kind, val) in placed {
            if kind == 0 {
                rot.insert(id.clone(), val);
            } else if kind == 1 {
                half.insert(id.clone(), val);
            }
            macro_ids.push(id);
        }
        let r_ids: Vec<String> = sol.layout.cs.iter().chain(&sol.layout.rs).chain(&sol.layout.leds).map(|x| x.id.clone()).collect();

        Self::normalize_dna(BackboneDna {
            macro_ids,
            ic_gaps,
            rot,
            half,
            gaps: sol.opts.gaps.clone(),
            r_ids,
            err_count: sol.errors.len(),
            ortho_count: sol.ortho_count,
            score: sol.score,
            ..Default::default()
        })
    }

    pub fn harvest_pool(&mut self, pool: &[BoardSolution]) -> usize {
        let mut cnt = 0usize;
        for s in pool.iter().filter(|s| s.unplaced == 0 && s.errors.len() <= 4) {
            if let Some(dna) = Self::extract_from_solution(s) {
                if self.add_seed(dna) {
                    cnt += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        cnt
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn to_opts(
        &self,
        dna: &BackboneDna,
        fallback_order: &[usize],
        place_comp_count: usize,
        r_comp_count: usize,
        rng: &mut Mulberry32,
        lock_ic_only: bool,
        comps: &[CompSpec],
    ) -> CandidateOpts {
        let id_to_idx: FxHashMap<&str, usize> = comps.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, c)| (c.id.as_str(), i)).collect();
        let mut used = FxHashSet::default();
        let mut order = Vec::with_capacity(comps.len());

        let src_ids = if lock_ic_only && !dna.ic_ids.is_empty() { &dna.ic_ids } else { &dna.macro_ids };
        for id in src_ids {
            if let Some(&idx) = id_to_idx.get(id.as_str()) {
                let valid = if lock_ic_only { comps[idx].comp_type == CompType::IC } else { !comps[idx].comp_type.is_special() };
                if valid && used.insert(idx) {
                    order.push(idx);
                }
            }
        }
        for &idx in fallback_order {
            if used.insert(idx) {
                order.push(idx);
            }
        }
        for idx in 0..comps.len() {
            if used.insert(idx) {
                order.push(idx);
            }
        }

        let mut rot: FxHashMap<CompId, u8> = FxHashMap::default();
        let mut half: FxHashMap<CompId, u8> = FxHashMap::default();
        for c in comps {
            match c.comp_type {
                CompType::IC => {
                    rot.insert(c.cid, dna.rot.get(&c.id).copied().unwrap_or(c.rot));
                }
                CompType::BTN2 => {
                    half.insert(c.cid, dna.half.get(&c.id).copied().unwrap_or(0));
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        let mut gaps = if !lock_ic_only {
            dna.gaps.as_ref().filter(|g| g.len() == place_comp_count).cloned()
        } else {
            None
        };
        if gaps.is_none() && !dna.ic_gaps.is_empty() {
            let place_items: Vec<&CompSpec> = order.iter().filter_map(|&ci| comps.get(ci)).filter(|c| !c.comp_type.is_special()).collect();
            let mut g_vec: Vec<i32> = place_items.iter().map(|c| if c.comp_type == CompType::IC { 2 } else { 1 }).collect();
            let mut ic_cursor = 0usize;
            for (i, item) in place_items.iter().enumerate() {
                if item.comp_type == CompType::IC && ic_cursor < dna.ic_gaps.len() {
                    g_vec[i] = dna.ic_gaps[ic_cursor].clamp(0, 4);
                    ic_cursor += 1;
                }
            }
            gaps = Some(g_vec);
        }

        let spec_comps: Vec<&CompSpec> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_special()).collect();
        let spec_id_to_pos: FxHashMap<&str, usize> = spec_comps.iter().enumerate().map(|(pos, c)| (c.id.as_str(), pos)).collect();
        let mut r_order = Vec::with_capacity(r_comp_count);
        let mut used_r = FxHashSet::default();
        for id in &dna.r_ids {
            if let Some(&pos) = spec_id_to_pos.get(id.as_str()) {
                if used_r.insert(pos) {
                    r_order.push(pos);
                }
            }
        }
        for k in 0..r_comp_count {
            if used_r.insert(k) {
                r_order.push(k);
            }
        }

        let lock_gaps = gaps.is_some() && !lock_ic_only;
        CandidateOpts {
            order,
            rot,
            half,
            gaps,
            lock_gaps,
            r_order,
            ortho_weight: Some(if dna.err_count == 0 { 18.0 } else { 7.5 }),
            passes: Some(26),
            sr: Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
            r_seed: Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
            phase: if dna.err_count == 0 { SearchPhase::P2Ortho } else { SearchPhase::P1Bridge },
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    pub fn build_warm_start_queue(
        &self,
        rl: &mut RlBandit,
        fallback_order: &[usize],
        place_comp_count: usize,
        r_comp_count: usize,
        rng: &mut Mulberry32,
        comps: &[CompSpec],
    ) -> Vec<CandidateOpts> {
        if self.seeds.is_empty() {
            return Vec::new();
        }
        let id_to_idx: FxHashMap<&str, usize> = comps.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, c)| (c.id.as_str(), i)).collect();
        let mut unique_groups: Vec<&ArchetypeGroup> = self.archetypes.values().collect();
        unique_groups.sort_by(|a, b| Self::cmp_dna(&a.variants[0], &b.variants[0]));

        for (rank_idx, grp) in unique_groups.iter().take(24).enumerate() {
            let dna = &grp.variants[0];
            let rank_boost = (1.8 - (rank_idx as f32) * 0.06).max(0.4);
            let err_bonus = match dna.err_count { 0 => 6.5, 1 => 3.5, 2 => 1.8, _ => 0.0 };
            let w = (((10 - dna.err_count.min(9)) as f32 * 0.85).max(0.6) + err_bonus) * rank_boost;

            for (id, &v) in &dna.rot {
                if let Some(&idx) = id_to_idx.get(id.as_str()) {
                    rl.q_rot[comps[idx].cid.0 as usize][usize::from(v != 0)] += w * 0.90;
                }
            }
            let ic_indices: Vec<usize> = dna.ic_ids.iter().filter_map(|id| id_to_idx.get(id.as_str()).copied()).collect();
            for i in 0..ic_indices.len() {
                for j in (i + 1)..ic_indices.len() {
                    let (u, v) = (ic_indices[i], ic_indices[j]);
                    let step_w = if j == i + 1 { 1.75 } else if j == i + 2 { 0.75 } else { 0.35 };
                    rl.q_pair[u][v] += w * step_w;
                    rl.q_pair[v][u] = (rl.q_pair[v][u] * 0.90).max(0.2);
                }
            }
            for (id, &v) in &dna.half {
                if let Some(&idx) = id_to_idx.get(id.as_str()) {
                    rl.q_half[comps[idx].cid.0 as usize][usize::from(v != 0)] += w * 0.28;
                }
            }
            let all_macro: Vec<usize> = dna.macro_ids.iter().filter_map(|id| id_to_idx.get(id.as_str()).copied()).collect();
            for i in 0..all_macro.len() {
                for j in (i + 1)..all_macro.len() {
                    let (u, v) = (all_macro[i], all_macro[j]);
                    if comps[u].comp_type == CompType::IC && comps[v].comp_type == CompType::IC {
                        continue;
                    }
                    rl.q_pair[u][v] += w * if j == i + 1 { 0.32 } else { 0.12 };
                }
            }
        }

        let mut queue = Vec::new();
        let ic_cids: Vec<CompId> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC).map(|c| c.cid).collect();

        for (idx_g, grp) in unique_groups.iter().take(12).enumerate() {
            let top_dna = &grp.variants[0];
            let exact_opt = self.to_opts(top_dna, fallback_order, place_comp_count, r_comp_count, rng, false, comps);
            queue.push(exact_opt.clone());

            let mut backbone_opt = self.to_opts(top_dna, fallback_order, place_comp_count, r_comp_count, rng, true, comps);
            backbone_opt.net_ord = rng.next_usize(20);
            backbone_opt.rev = idx_g % 2 == 1;
            queue.push(backbone_opt);

            if idx_g < 5 && top_dna.ic_ids.len() >= 2 {
                let mut rev_ics = top_dna.ic_ids.clone();
                rev_ics.reverse();
                let mut rev_macro = rev_ics.clone();
                rev_macro.extend(top_dna.io_ids.iter().cloned());
                let mut rev_ic_gaps = top_dna.ic_gaps.clone();
                rev_ic_gaps.reverse();
                let rev_rot: FxHashMap<String, u8> = top_dna.rot.iter().map(|(k, &v)| (k.clone(), u8::from(v == 0))).collect();
                let mirror_dna = BackboneDna {
                    ic_ids: rev_ics,
                    macro_ids: rev_macro,
                    ic_gaps: rev_ic_gaps,
                    rot: rev_rot,
                    gaps: None,
                    ..top_dna.clone()
                };
                queue.push(self.to_opts(&mirror_dna, fallback_order, place_comp_count, r_comp_count, rng, true, comps));
            }

            if idx_g < 4 && !ic_cids.is_empty() {
                let mut tweaked = exact_opt;
                let pick_ic = ic_cids[rng.next_usize(ic_cids.len())];
                let cur_r = tweaked.rot.get(&pick_ic).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                tweaked.rot.insert(pick_ic, u8::from(cur_r == 0));
                tweaked.gaps = None;
                tweaked.lock_gaps = false;
                tweaked.sr = Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
                tweaked.r_seed = Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32);
                tweaked.net_ord = rng.next_usize(25);
                queue.push(tweaked);
            }
        }
        queue
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/logger.rs ===
use crate::meta_search::builder::{BoardSolution, CandidateOpts, SearchPhase};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ErrorTypeCounts {
    pub rled_unplaced: u32,
    pub rail_blocked: u32,
    pub sig_congested: u32,
    pub short_circuit: u32,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MilestoneSnap {
    pub it: u32,
    pub t_ms: u64,
    pub phase: String,
    pub score: i64,
    pub unplaced: usize,
    pub err_count: usize,
    pub warn_count: usize,
    pub ortho_count: usize,
    pub wire_count: usize,
    pub wire_len: i32,
    pub max_c: u8,
    pub ic_layout: String,
    pub sw_layout: String,
    pub btn_layout: String,
    pub bridges: Vec<u8>,
    pub gaps: Vec<i32>,
    pub errors: Vec<String>,
}

/// Hệ thống lưu Nhật ký & Chẩn đoán nguyên nhân thất bại (`SearchLogger` trong main.html)[cite: 4]
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SearchLogger {
    pub total_nets: usize,
    pub iterations: u32,
    pub unique_configs: u32,
    pub duplicates: u32,
    pub elapsed_ms: u64,
    pub phase_counts: [u32; 6],
    pub err_types: ErrorTypeCounts,
    pub net_fails: FxHashMap<String, u32>,
    pub comp_fails: FxHashMap<String, u32>,
    pub col_hotspots: FxHashMap<u8, u32>,
    pub milestones: Vec<MilestoneSnap>,
    pub best_snap: Option<MilestoneSnap>,
}

impl SearchLogger {
    pub fn new(total_nets: usize) -> Self {
        Self { total_nets, ..Default::default() }
    }

    pub fn reset(&mut self, fresh: bool, total_nets: usize) {
        if fresh || self.iterations == 0 {
            *self = Self::new(total_nets);
        }
    }

    pub fn record(
        &mut self,
        sol: &BoardSolution,
        opts: &CandidateOpts,
        it: u32,
        elapsed_ms: u64,
        is_dup: bool,
    ) {
        self.iterations = it;
        self.elapsed_ms = elapsed_ms;
        if is_dup {
            self.duplicates += 1;
        } else {
            self.unique_configs += 1;
        }
        let p_idx = match opts.phase {
            SearchPhase::Init => 0,
            SearchPhase::P1Unblock => 1,
            SearchPhase::P1Bridge => 2,
            SearchPhase::P2Ortho => 3,
            SearchPhase::P3Compact => 4,
            SearchPhase::Diversify => 5,
        };
        self.phase_counts[p_idx] += 1;

        for e in &sol.errors {
            if e.contains("không có 2 lỗ trống") {
                self.err_types.rled_unplaced += 1;
                if let Some((cid, _)) = e.split_once(':') {
                    *self.comp_fails.entry(cid.trim().to_string()).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            } else if e.contains("dây nguồn") || e.contains("dây rail") {
                self.err_types.rail_blocked += 1;
                if let Some((n, _)) = e.split_once(':') {
                    *self.net_fails.entry(n.trim().to_string()).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            } else if e.contains("ngắn mạch") || e.contains("Ngắn mạch") {
                self.err_types.short_circuit += 1;
            } else {
                self.err_types.sig_congested += 1;
                if let Some(rest) = e.strip_prefix("Net ") {
                    if let Some((n, _)) = rest.split_once(':') {
                        let net_name = n.trim().to_string();
                        *self.net_fails.entry(net_name.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
                        for (cid, nets) in &sol.comp_nets {
                            if nets.contains(&net_name) {
                                *self.comp_fails.entry(cid.clone()).or_insert(0) += 1;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            if let Some(pos) = e.find("cột ") {
                let after = &e[pos + "cột ".len()..];
                let num_s: String = after.chars().take_while(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
                if let Ok(col) = num_s.parse::<u8>() {
                    *self.col_hotspots.entry(col).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if self.best_snap.as_ref().is_none_or(|b| (sol.score.round() as i64) < b.score) {
            let ic_layout = sol.layout.ics.iter()
                .map(|x| format!("{}{}[{}-{}]", x.id, if x.rot != 0 { "(R)" } else { "" }, x.c_min, x.c_max))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
            let sw_layout = sol.layout.sws.iter()
                .map(|x| format!("{}[{}-{}]", x.id, x.c_min, x.c_max))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
            let btn_layout = sol.layout.btns.iter()
                .map(|x| format!("{}({})[{}-{}]", x.id, if x.row_top <= 4 { "U" } else { "L" }, x.c_min, x.c_max))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ");
            let bridges: Vec<u8> = sol.fast_wires.iter()
                .filter(|w| w.c1 == w.c2 && w.r1.min(w.r2) == 4 && w.r1.max(w.r2) == 5)
                .map(|w| w.c1)
                .collect();

            let snap = MilestoneSnap {
                it,
                t_ms: elapsed_ms,
                phase: opts.phase.as_str().to_string(),
                score: sol.score.round() as i64,
                unplaced: sol.unplaced,
                err_count: sol.errors.len(),
                warn_count: sol.warns.len(),
                ortho_count: sol.ortho_count,
                wire_count: sol.wires.len(),
                wire_len: sol.len,
                max_c: sol.max_c,
                ic_layout,
                sw_layout,
                btn_layout,
                bridges,
                gaps: opts.gaps.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                errors: sol.errors.clone(),
            };
            self.best_snap = Some(snap.clone());
            self.milestones.push(snap);
            if self.milestones.len() > 120 {
                let remove_cnt = self.milestones.len() - 120;
                self.milestones.drain(1..=remove_cnt);
            }
        }
    }

    fn top_entries_str(map: &FxHashMap<String, u32>, k: usize) -> String {
        if map.is_empty() {
            return "Không có".into();
        }
        let mut v: Vec<(&String, &u32)> = map.iter().collect();
        v.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(a.1));
        v.into_iter().take(k).map(|(key, val)| format!("{key} ({val})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
    }

    pub fn build_text_report(&self) -> String {
        let b = self.best_snap.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        let rate = if self.elapsed_ms > 0 {
            (self.iterations as f64) * 1000.0 / (self.elapsed_ms as f64)
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let pc = &self.phase_counts;
        let et = &self.err_types;
        let mut hot_v: Vec<(&u8, &u32)> = self.col_hotspots.iter().collect();
        hot_v.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.cmp(a.1));
        let hot_str = if hot_v.is_empty() {
            "Không có".into()
        } else {
            hot_v.into_iter().take(10).map(|(c, v)| format!("Cột {c} ({v})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
        };
        let gaps_s = b.gaps.iter().map(ToString::to_string).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let br_s = b.bridges.iter().map(ToString::to_string).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");

        let mut lines = vec![
            "=== BÁO CÁO PHÂN TÍCH TÌM KIẾM BREADBOARD AUTO-ROUTER ===".into(),
            format!("Tổng cấu hình thử : {} ({} duy nhất, {} trùng lặp)", self.iterations, self.unique_configs, self.duplicates),
            format!("Thời gian chạy    : {:.2}s (Tốc độ: {:.1} cấu hình/giây)", (self.elapsed_ms as f64) / 1000.0, rate),
            format!("Phân bổ pha tìm   : Khởi tạo={}, Gỡ lỗi={}, Mở khe cầu={}, Nắn thẳng R/LED={}, Thu gọn={}, Đa dạng hóa={}", pc[0], pc[1], pc[2], pc[3], pc[4], pc[5]),
            String::new(),
            format!("--- 1. KẾT QUẢ TỐT NHẤT HIỆN TẠI (Vòng #{} - {}ms - Pha: {}) ---", b.it, b.t_ms, b.phase),
            format!("- Điểm mục tiêu   : {} (Thiếu: {}, Lỗi: {}, Cảnh báo: {})", b.score, b.unplaced, b.err_count, b.warn_count),
            format!("- Thẩm mỹ & Dây   : {} R/LED thẳng hàng, {} đoạn dây, Dài {} ô, Rộng {}/63 cột", b.ortho_count, b.wire_count, b.wire_len, b.max_c),
            format!("- Bố cục IC       : {}", if b.ic_layout.is_empty() { "Trống" } else { &b.ic_layout }),
            format!("- Bố cục BTN      : {}", if b.btn_layout.is_empty() { "Trống" } else { &b.btn_layout }),
            format!("- Bố cục SW       : {}", if b.sw_layout.is_empty() { "Trống" } else { &b.sw_layout }),
            format!("- Mảng khe hở     : [{gaps_s}]"),
            format!("- Cột cầu rãnh E-F: Đã dùng {} cột [{br_s}]", b.bridges.len()),
            format!("- Lỗi còn tồn đọng ({}):", b.errors.len()),
        ];
        if b.errors.is_empty() {
            lines.push("   ✓ 0 lỗi (Hoàn hảo)".into());
        } else {
            for e in &b.errors {
                lines.push(format!("   ✕ {e}"));
            }
        }
        lines.extend([
            String::new(),
            "--- 2. THỐNG KÊ CHẨN ĐOÁN NGUYÊN NHÂN THẤT BẠI TRÊN TOÀN QUÁ TRÌNH ---".into(),
            format!("- Phân loại lỗi   : Nghẽn tín hiệu/Cầu rãnh={}, Kẹt dây nguồn Rail={}, Hết chỗ cắm R/LED={}, Ngắn mạch={}", et.sig_congested, et.rail_blocked, et.rled_unplaced, et.short_circuit),
            format!("- Top Net kẹt nhất: {}", Self::top_entries_str(&self.net_fails, 10)),
            format!("- Top Linh kiện   : {}", Self::top_entries_str(&self.comp_fails, 10)),
            format!("- Điểm nóng (Cột) : {hot_str}"),
            String::new(),
            "--- 3. LỊCH SỬ CỘT MỐC TIẾN HÓA (MILESTONES) ---".into(),
        ]);
        for (i, m) in self.milestones.iter().enumerate() {
            lines.push(format!(
                "  #{} [Vòng {} - {}ms - {}] -> Lỗi: {}, Thiếu: {}, R/LED thẳng: {}, Dài: {}, Rộng: {}c, IC: {}",
                i + 1, m.it, m.t_ms, m.phase, m.err_count, m.unplaced, m.ortho_count, m.wire_len, m.max_c, m.ic_layout
            ));
        }
        lines.join("\n")
    }
}

    SearchPhase,
