=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/mutator.rs ===
use crate::config::personas::PERSONAS;
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::macro_place::cost::cross_cost;
use crate::macro_place::topology::TopologyCache;
use crate::meta_search::builder::{
    extract_frozen_map, parse_err_info, BoardSolution, CandidateOpts, SearchPhase,
};
use crate::meta_search::rl_bandit::{AgentFocus, MetaAgent, RlBandit, NUM_ISLANDS};
use crate::meta_search::tabu_basin::TabuBasin;
use crate::meta_search::zobrist::{decode_btn_bend, encode_btn_bend, tabu_key};
use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, ParsedNetlist};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, Mulberry32};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

/// Cân bằng phân bố nửa bo `half` cho toàn bộ linh kiện IO (`SW` + `BTN2`) về tỷ lệ đối xứng (VD: 3 Trên – 3 Dưới) (#3)
fn rebalance_io_halves(
    half: &mut FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    rng: &mut Mulberry32,
) {
    let io_cids: Vec<(CompId, u8)> = comps
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_io())
        .map(|c| {
            let def_h = u8::from(c.comp_type == CompType::SW);
            let h = u8::from(half.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_h) != 0);
            (c.cid, h)
        })
        .collect();
    if io_cids.len() < 2 {
        return;
    }
    let target_top = io_cids.len() / 2;
    let mut top_list: Vec<CompId> = io_cids.iter().filter(|&&(_, h)| h == 0).map(|&(cid, _)| cid).collect();
    let mut bot_list: Vec<CompId> = io_cids.iter().filter(|&&(_, h)| h != 0).map(|&(cid, _)| cid).collect();

    while top_list.len() > target_top + (io_cids.len() % 2) {
        let idx = rng.next_usize(top_list.len());
        let cid = top_list.swap_remove(idx);
        half.insert(cid, 1);
        bot_list.push(cid);
    }
    while bot_list.len() > io_cids.len() - target_top {
        let idx = rng.next_usize(bot_list.len());
        let cid = bot_list.swap_remove(idx);
        half.insert(cid, 0);
        top_list.push(cid);
    }
}

/// Đột biến góc bẻ chân `btn_bend` (`-1, 0, +1`) cho 1 nút bấm `BTN2` (lưu mã hóa trong `rot`) (#2, #8)
fn mutate_one_btn_bend(
    rot: &mut FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    rng: &mut Mulberry32,
) -> bool {
    let btns: Vec<CompId> = comps
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::BTN2)
        .map(|c| c.cid)
        .collect();
    if btns.is_empty() {
        return false;
    }
    let cid = btns[rng.next_usize(btns.len())];
    let h = half.get(&cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
    let def_code = if h == 0 { encode_btn_bend(-1) } else { encode_btn_bend(1) };
    let cur_bend = decode_btn_bend(rot.get(&cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_code));
    let choices: [i8; 2] = match cur_bend {
        -1 => [0, 1],
        0 => [-1, 1],
        _ => [-1, 0],
    };
    let next_bend = choices[rng.next_usize(2)];
    rot.insert(cid, encode_btn_bend(next_bend));
    true
}

/// Đột biến Nhảy bậc Vĩ mô (`applyMacroJump` trong main.html) khi bố cục cũ bị khóa Tabu
pub fn apply_macro_jump(
    q: &mut CandidateOpts,
    focus: AgentFocus,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    rng: &mut Mulberry32,
) {
    let lock_right = focus == AgentFocus::LockIo;
    let swappable: Vec<usize> = q
        .order
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .filter_map(|(pos, &ci)| {
            let c = comps.get(ci)?;
            if c.comp_type != CompType::IC || (lock_right && c.id == "U1") {
                return None;
            }
            Some(pos)
        })
        .collect();
    if swappable.len() >= 2 {
        let idx_a = rng.next_usize(swappable.len());
        let mut idx_b = rng.next_usize(swappable.len());
        if idx_a == idx_b {
            idx_b = (idx_a + 1) % swappable.len();
        }
        q.order.swap(swappable[idx_a], swappable[idx_b]);
    }
    let rot_cands: Vec<CompId> = comps
        .iter()
        .filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC && !(lock_right && c.id == "U1"))
        .map(|c| c.cid)
        .collect();
    if !rot_cands.is_empty() {
        let p1 = rot_cands[rng.next_usize(rot_cands.len())];
        let cur1 = q.rot.get(&p1).copied().unwrap_or(0);
        q.rot.insert(p1, u8::from(cur1 == 0));
        if rng.next_f64() < 0.5 && rot_cands.len() > 1 {
            let p2 = rot_cands[rng.next_usize(rot_cands.len())];
            let cur2 = q.rot.get(&p2).copied().unwrap_or(0);
            q.rot.insert(p2, u8::from(cur2 == 0));
        }
    }
    if !lock_right {
        let io_cids: Vec<(CompId, CompType)> = comps
            .iter()
            .filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_io())
            .map(|c| (c.cid, c.comp_type))
            .collect();
        if !io_cids.is_empty() {
            let (pick_io, ct) = io_cids[rng.next_usize(io_cids.len())];
            let def_h = u8::from(ct == CompType::SW);
            let cur_h = q.half.get(&pick_io).copied().unwrap_or(def_h);
            q.half.insert(pick_io, u8::from(cur_h == 0));
            rebalance_io_halves(&mut q.half, comps, rng);
        }
        mutate_one_btn_bend(&mut q.rot, &q.half, comps, rng);
    }
    q.persona = q.island % NUM_ISLANDS;
    q.gaps = None;
    q.lock_gaps = false;
    q.frozen_specials.clear();
    q.evict_cols_mask = 0;
    q.handed_over = false;
    q.rot_mutated = true;
    q.phase = SearchPhase::Diversify;
}

/// Tinh chỉnh chiều xoay 1 IC hoặc nửa bo 1 IO (`SW`/`BTN2`) bằng hàm mục tiêu nhanh `cross_cost` (`polishOneIC`)
pub fn polish_one_ic(
    q: &mut CandidateOpts,
    focus: AgentFocus,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    topo: &TopologyCache,
    weights: &CostWeights,
    rng: &mut Mulberry32,
) {
    if q.rot_mutated || rng.next_f64() > 0.45 {
        return;
    }
    let ics: Vec<&CompSpec> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC).collect();
    if !ics.is_empty() {
        let pick = ics[rng.next_usize(ics.len())];
        if !(focus == AgentFocus::LockIo && pick.id == "U1") {
            let c0 = cross_cost(parsed, topo, &q.rot, &q.half, Some(&q.order), comps, weights);
            let old_r = q.rot.get(&pick.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
            q.rot.insert(pick.cid, u8::from(old_r == 0));
            if cross_cost(parsed, topo, &q.rot, &q.half, Some(&q.order), comps, weights) > c0 {
                q.rot.insert(pick.cid, old_r);
            }
        }
    }
    if focus != AgentFocus::LockIo && rng.next_f64() < 0.35 {
        let ios: Vec<&CompSpec> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_io()).collect();
        if !ios.is_empty() {
            let pick_io = ios[rng.next_usize(ios.len())];
            let c0 = cross_cost(parsed, topo, &q.rot, &q.half, Some(&q.order), comps, weights);
            let def_h = u8::from(pick_io.comp_type == CompType::SW);
            let old_h = q.half.get(&pick_io.cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_h);
            q.half.insert(pick_io.cid, u8::from(old_h == 0));
            if cross_cost(parsed, topo, &q.rot, &q.half, Some(&q.order), comps, weights) > c0 {
                q.half.insert(pick_io.cid, old_h);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Hàm đột biến & lai ghép đa chiến thuật (`mutate` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn mutate_candidate(
    base: &CandidateOpts,
    parent: Option<&BoardSolution>,
    stall_cnt: u32,
    mate: Option<&BoardSolution>,
    isl: usize,
    cur_it: u32,
    place_comp_count: usize,
    r_comp_count: usize,
    agent: &MetaAgent,
    tabu: &TabuBasin,
    rl: &RlBandit,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    rng: &mut Mulberry32,
) -> CandidateOpts {
    let default_gap = base.gap.unwrap_or(1);
    let mut q = CandidateOpts {
        island: isl % NUM_ISLANDS,
        persona: isl % NUM_ISLANDS,
        order: base.order.clone(),
        rot: base.rot.clone(),
        half: base.half.clone(),
        io_flip: base.io_flip.clone(),
        gap: base.gap,
        gaps: Some(base.gaps.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| vec![default_gap; place_comp_count])),
        compact: false,
        lock_gaps: false,
        r_order: if !base.r_order.is_empty() { base.r_order.clone() } else { (0..r_comp_count).collect() },
        ortho_weight: base.ortho_weight,
        dyn_br_pen: agent.dyn_br_pen,
        dyn_aux_share: agent.dyn_aux_share,
        evict_cols_mask: 0,
        boost_nets: Default::default(),
        frozen_specials: Default::default(),
        rev: base.rev,
        net_ord: base.net_ord,
        passes: Some(PERSONAS[isl % NUM_ISLANDS].passes),
        sr: Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
        r_seed: Some(rng.next_usize(1_000_000_000) as u32),
        use_star_hub: base.use_star_hub,
        phase: base.phase,
        rot_mutated: false,
        handed_over: false,
        parent_score: parent.map(|p| p.score),
        arm: None,
    };

    let has_err = parent.is_none_or(|p| p.unplaced > 0 || !p.errors.is_empty());
    let is_near_solved = parent.is_some_and(|p| p.unplaced == 0 && (1..=3).contains(&p.errors.len()));
    let t_key = tabu_key(&q.order, &q.rot, &q.half, comps);

    // 1. CHỐNG KẸT TUYỆT ĐỐI: Kiểm tra Tabu và stall_cnt TRƯỚC khi xét nhánh P5:Surgeon
    if has_err
        && (agent.focus == AgentFocus::Macro
            || tabu.is_tabu(t_key, cur_it)
            || (stall_cnt > 24 && rng.next_f64() < 0.65))
    {
        apply_macro_jump(&mut q, agent.focus, comps, rng);
        return q;
    }

    if let Some(m) = mate.filter(|_| rng.next_f64() < 0.28) {
        for c in comps {
            if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
                if agent.focus == AgentFocus::LockIo && c.id == "U1" {
                    continue;
                }
                if rng.next_f64() < 0.5 {
                    if let Some(&rv) = m.opts.rot.get(&c.cid) {
                        q.rot.insert(c.cid, rv);
                    }
                }
            } else if c.comp_type.is_io() && agent.focus != AgentFocus::LockIo && rng.next_f64() < 0.5 {
                if let Some(&hv) = m.opts.half.get(&c.cid) {
                    q.half.insert(c.cid, hv);
                }
                if c.comp_type == CompType::BTN2 {
                    if let Some(&bv) = m.opts.rot.get(&c.cid) {
                        q.rot.insert(c.cid, bv);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // 2. KHI ĐÃ ĐẠT 0 LỖI (!has_err): Kích hoạt P5:Surgeon + Nắn thẳng Ortho + Thu gọn Compact
    if !has_err && rng.next_f64() < 0.88 {
        let p = parent.unwrap();
        if agent.focus == AgentFocus::Surgery && rng.next_f64() < 0.55 {
            q.persona = 4; // P5:Surgeon trực tiếp tinh chỉnh trên phôi 0 lỗi
            q.passes = Some(28);
        }
        q.ortho_weight = Some(14.0 + (rng.next_usize(16) as f64));
        if r_comp_count > 1 && rng.next_f64() < 0.75 {
            let i = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
            let j = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
            q.r_order.swap(i, j);
        }
        if p.ortho_count >= r_comp_count && q.gaps.as_ref().is_some_and(|g| !g.is_empty()) && rng.next_f64() < 0.58 {
            q.compact = true;
            q.lock_gaps = true;
            if let Some(gs) = q.gaps.as_mut() {
                let gi = rng.next_usize(gs.len());
                if gs[gi] > 0 {
                    gs[gi] -= 1;
                }
            }
            q.phase = SearchPhase::P3Compact;
        } else {
            q.phase = SearchPhase::P2Ortho;
        }
        q.net_ord = rng.next_usize(18);
        if rng.next_f64() < 0.35 {
            q.rev = !q.rev;
        }
        return q;
    }

    // 3. KHI CÒN 1..=3 LỖI (#8): Hạ nhẹ ortho_weight (2.5..6.0) + Đột biến btn_bend (-1, 0, +1) + Vi phẫu P5:Surgeon
    let parent_already_surgeon = parent.is_some_and(|p| p.opts.persona == 4 || p.opts.handed_over);
    let surg_prob = if parent_already_surgeon && stall_cnt > 8 {
        0.15
    } else {
        match agent.focus {
            AgentFocus::Surgery => 0.80,
            AgentFocus::Bridge => 0.65,
            _ => 0.48,
        }
    };

    if is_near_solved && stall_cnt <= 18 && rng.next_f64() < surg_prob {
        let p = parent.unwrap();
        q.phase = SearchPhase::P1Bridge;
        // Hạ nhẹ ortho_weight xuống 2.5..6.0 để R/LED/C linh hoạt xoay chéo cứu lỗi trước (#8)
        q.ortho_weight = Some(2.5 + rng.next_f64() * 3.5);
        if rng.next_f64() < 0.65 {
            mutate_one_btn_bend(&mut q.rot, &q.half, comps, rng);
        }
        let (boost_nets, evict_mask, raw_cols) = parse_err_info(&p.errors, parsed);
        q.boost_nets = boost_nets;
        if !parent_already_surgeon && (agent.focus == AgentFocus::Surgery || rng.next_f64() < 0.50) {
            q.persona = 4;
            q.passes = Some(32);
            q.frozen_specials = extract_frozen_map(p);
            q.evict_cols_mask = if agent.focus == AgentFocus::Surgery || rng.next_f64() < 0.75 { evict_mask } else { 0 };
            q.lock_gaps = true;
            q.handed_over = true;
        } else {
            q.persona = isl % NUM_ISLANDS;
            q.passes = Some(24);
        }
        q.net_ord = rng.next_usize(30);
        if r_comp_count > 1 {
            let i = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
            let j = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
            q.r_order.swap(i, j);
        }
        let place_items: Vec<CompId> = q.order.iter().filter_map(|&ci| comps.get(ci)).filter(|c| !c.comp_type.is_special()).map(|c| c.cid).collect();
        if (q.frozen_specials.is_empty() || agent.focus == AgentFocus::Bridge)
            && !raw_cols.is_empty()
            && q.gaps.as_ref().is_some_and(|g| g.len() == place_items.len())
        {
            let target_col = raw_cols[rng.next_usize(raw_cols.len())];
            let near_cands: Vec<CompId> = p.layout.ics.iter().map(|x| (x.cid, x.c_min, x.c_max))
                .chain(p.layout.sws.iter().map(|x| (x.cid, x.c_min, x.c_max)))
                .chain(p.layout.btns.iter().map(|x| (x.cid, x.c_min, x.c_max)))
                .filter(|&(_, c_min, c_max)| c_max >= target_col.saturating_sub(8) && c_min <= target_col.saturating_add(8))
                .map(|(cid, _, _)| cid)
                .collect();
            if !near_cands.is_empty() {
                let picked = near_cands[rng.next_usize(near_cands.len())];
                if let Some(near_idx) = place_items.iter().position(|&cid| cid == picked) {
                    q.lock_gaps = true;
                    let gs = q.gaps.as_mut().unwrap();
                    let boost = if rng.next_f64() < 0.45 && near_idx > 0 { near_idx - 1 } else { near_idx };
                    gs[boost] = (gs[boost] + 1).min(4);
                    let shrink_cands: Vec<usize> = gs.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(idx, &g)| (idx != boost && g > 0).then_some(idx)).collect();
                    if !shrink_cands.is_empty() {
                        let s_idx = shrink_cands[rng.next_usize(shrink_cands.len())];
                        gs[s_idx] -= 1;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if rng.next_f64() < 0.35 {
            q.rev = !q.rev;
        }
        return q;
    }

    // 4. ĐỘT BIẾN ĐẠI CỤC TỰ NHIÊN TRÊN 4 QUẦN ĐẢO (I1..I4)
    q.phase = SearchPhase::P1Unblock;
    q.persona = isl % NUM_ISLANDS;
    q.passes = Some(PERSONAS[isl % NUM_ISLANDS].passes);
    // Nếu phôi cha còn 1..=3 lỗi, duy trì ortho_weight thấp (2.5..6.0) và thử bẻ chân BTN2 (#8)
    if is_near_solved {
        q.ortho_weight = Some(2.5 + rng.next_f64() * 3.5);
        if rng.next_f64() < 0.55 {
            mutate_one_btn_bend(&mut q.rot, &q.half, comps, rng);
        }
    } else {
        q.ortho_weight = None;
    }

    let lock_right = agent.focus == AgentFocus::LockIo;
    let arm = rl.pick_arm(agent.focus, rng);
    q.arm = Some(arm);

    let place_pos: Vec<usize> = q.order.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(pos, &ci)| {
        let c = comps.get(ci)?;
        if c.comp_type.is_special() || (lock_right && (c.id == "U1" || c.comp_type != CompType::IC)) {
            return None;
        }
        if isl != 1 && arm <= 1 && c.comp_type != CompType::IC && rng.next_f64() < 0.75 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(pos)
    }).collect();

    let ic_cids: Vec<CompId> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC && !(lock_right && c.id == "U1")).map(|c| c.cid).collect();
    let io_comps: Vec<(CompId, CompType)> = comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type.is_io()).map(|c| (c.cid, c.comp_type)).collect();

    match arm {
        0 if place_pos.len() > 1 => {
            let i = place_pos[rng.next_usize(place_pos.len())];
            let j = place_pos[rng.next_usize(place_pos.len())];
            q.order.swap(i, j);
            q.gaps = None;
        }
        1 if place_pos.len() > 1 => {
            let i = place_pos[rng.next_usize(place_pos.len())];
            let dest = place_pos[rng.next_usize(place_pos.len())];
            let v = q.order.remove(i);
            q.order.insert(dest.min(q.order.len()), v);
            q.gaps = None;
        }
        2 if !ic_cids.is_empty() => {
            let cid = ic_cids[rng.next_usize(ic_cids.len())];
            let cur = q.rot.get(&cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
            q.rot.insert(cid, u8::from(cur == 0));
            q.rot_mutated = true;
        }
        3 if q.gaps.as_ref().is_some_and(|g| !g.is_empty()) => {
            q.lock_gaps = true;
            let gs = q.gaps.as_mut().unwrap();
            let gi = rng.next_usize(gs.len());
            let gj = rng.next_usize(gs.len());
            gs[gi] = (gs[gi] + 1).min(4);
            if gs[gj] > 0 {
                gs[gj] -= 1;
            }
        }
        4 if !io_comps.is_empty() && !lock_right => {
            let (cid, ct) = io_comps[rng.next_usize(io_comps.len())];
            let def_h = u8::from(ct == CompType::SW);
            let cur = q.half.get(&cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_h);
            q.half.insert(cid, u8::from(cur == 0));
            if rng.next_f64() < 0.65 {
                rebalance_io_halves(&mut q.half, comps, rng);
            }
            if ct == CompType::BTN2 || rng.next_f64() < 0.45 {
                mutate_one_btn_bend(&mut q.rot, &q.half, comps, rng);
            }
            q.rot_mutated = true;
        }
        _ => {
            if rng.next_f64() < 0.45 && ic_cids.len() > 1 && !lock_right {
                for &cid in &ic_cids {
                    let cur = q.rot.get(&cid).copied().unwrap_or(0);
                    q.rot.insert(cid, u8::from(cur == 0));
                }
                q.rot_mutated = true;
            }
            if r_comp_count > 1 {
                let i = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
                let j = rng.next_usize(r_comp_count);
                q.r_order.swap(i, j);
            }
            q.rev = !q.rev;
            q.net_ord = rng.next_usize(24);
        }
    }
    q
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::circuit::BoardLayout;
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_mutator_io_symmetry_and_near_solved_btn_bend_ortho_lowering() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "S1", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "S2", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "S3", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "S4", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(5), "B1", CompType::BTN2),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(6), "B2", CompType::BTN2),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(7), "R1", CompType::R),
        ];
        let parsed = parse_netlist("VCC U1:14; GND U1:7; U1:1 -> R1 -> B1:1; B1:2 -> GND", &comps);
        let mut rng = Mulberry32::new(7);

        // 1. Kiểm tra cân bằng đối xứng 3 Trên – 3 Dưới cho 6 linh kiện IO (4 SW + 2 BTN2) (#3)
        let mut half = FxHashMap::default();
        for id in 1..=6 {
            half.insert(CompId(id), 1); // Ban đầu dồn cả 6 IO xuống nửa dưới
        }
        rebalance_io_halves(&mut half, &comps, &mut rng);
        let top_cnt = (1..=6).filter(|&id| half.get(&CompId(id)) == Some(&0)).count();
        let bot_cnt = (1..=6).filter(|&id| half.get(&CompId(id)) == Some(&1)).count();
        assert_eq!((top_cnt, bot_cnt), (3, 3));

        // 2. Kiểm tra khi phôi cha còn 1..=3 lỗi: hạ nhẹ ortho_weight (2.5..6.0) và đột biến btn_bend (#8)
        let base_opts = CandidateOpts {
            order: (0..comps.len()).collect(),
            half,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let parent_sol = BoardSolution {
            layout: BoardLayout::default(),
            wires: Vec::new(),
            fast_wires: Vec::new(),
            errors: vec![
                "Net NET1: không đi được dây từ cột 5 tới cột 12".into(),
                "Net NET2: không đi được dây từ cột 8 tới cột 15".into(),
                "Net NET3: không đi được dây từ cột 10 tới cột 20".into(),
            ],
            warns: Vec::new(),
            comp_nets: FxHashMap::default(),
            nets: 3,
            len: 20,
            raw_len: 20.0,
            max_c: 20,
            score: 3e8,
            unplaced: 0,
            ortho_count: 1,
            persona: "I3:BridgeLED".into(),
            opts: base_opts.clone(),
            sig: 123,
            layout_hash: 456,
        };

        let mut agent = MetaAgent::default();
        agent.focus = AgentFocus::Surgery;
        let tabu = TabuBasin::new();
        let rl = RlBandit::new(comps.len());

        let mut mutated_bend = false;
        for _ in 0..10 {
            let child = mutate_candidate(
                &base_opts,
                Some(&parent_sol),
                2,
                None,
                2,
                10,
                7,
                1,
                &agent,
                &tabu,
                &rl,
                &comps,
                &parsed,
                &mut rng,
            );
            let ow = child.ortho_weight.expect("ortho_weight must be lowered on 1..=3 errors");
            assert!((2.5..=6.0).contains(&ow), "ortho_weight out of 2.5..=6.0: {ow}");
            if child.rot.contains_key(&CompId(5)) || child.rot.contains_key(&CompId(6)) {
                mutated_bend = true;
            }
        }
        assert!(mutated_bend, "BTN2 bend should be mutated when parent has 1..=3 errors");
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/rl_bandit.rs ===
use crate::model::circuit::CompSpec;
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, Mulberry32};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const NUM_ISLANDS: usize = 4;

/// Chế độ điều hướng trực tiếp từ giao diện (`#steer-mode` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum SteerMode {
    #[default]
    Auto,
    Macro,
    Bridge,
    Surgery,
    LockIo,
}

/// Trọng tâm chiến thuật hiện tại của MetaAgent (`MetaAgent.focus` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum AgentFocus {
    #[default]
    Balanced,
    Macro,
    Bridge,
    Surgery,
    LockIo,
}

impl AgentFocus {
    pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            AgentFocus::Balanced => "balanced",
            AgentFocus::Macro => "macro",
            AgentFocus::Bridge => "bridge",
            AgentFocus::Surgery => "surgery",
            AgentFocus::LockIo => "lock_io",
        }
    }
}

/// Meta-Agent Tự Phản Tỉnh & Điều Hướng Trọng Số Động (`MetaAgent` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct MetaAgent {
    pub mode: SteerMode,
    pub focus: AgentFocus,
    pub dyn_br_pen: Option<f64>,
    pub dyn_aux_share: Option<f64>,
}

impl MetaAgent {
    pub fn reflect(&mut self, stall_cnt: u32, best_bridges: usize, best_err_count: Option<usize>) {
        if self.mode != SteerMode::Auto {
            self.focus = match self.mode {
                SteerMode::Auto => AgentFocus::Balanced,
                SteerMode::Macro => AgentFocus::Macro,
                SteerMode::Bridge => AgentFocus::Bridge,
                SteerMode::Surgery => AgentFocus::Surgery,
                SteerMode::LockIo => AgentFocus::LockIo,
            };
            self.dyn_br_pen = (self.mode == SteerMode::Bridge).then_some(42.0);
            self.dyn_aux_share = (self.mode == SteerMode::Bridge).then_some(26.0);
            return;
        }
        if stall_cnt > 110 {
            self.focus = AgentFocus::Macro;
            self.dyn_br_pen = None;
            self.dyn_aux_share = None;
        } else if best_bridges <= 8 && best_err_count.is_some_and(|e| e > 0) {
            self.focus = AgentFocus::Bridge;
            self.dyn_br_pen = Some(42.0);
            self.dyn_aux_share = Some(26.0);
        } else if best_err_count == Some(0) {
            self.focus = AgentFocus::Surgery;
            self.dyn_br_pen = Some(38.0);
            self.dyn_aux_share = Some(24.0);
        } else {
            self.focus = AgentFocus::Balanced;
            self.dyn_br_pen = None;
            self.dyn_aux_share = None;
        }
    }
}

/// Bộ Học Tăng Cường UCB1 Bandit & Ma trận Q-Learning (`RL` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RlBandit {
    pub q_rot: [[f32; 2]; 256],
    pub q_half: [[f32; 2]; 256],
    pub q_pair: Vec<Vec<f32>>,
    pub arm_pulls: [f32; 6],
    pub arm_reward: [f32; 6],
    pub total_pulls: f32,
    pub i_pulls: [f32; NUM_ISLANDS],
    pub i_reward: [f32; NUM_ISLANDS],
    pub total_i_pulls: f32,
}

impl RlBandit {
    pub fn new(num_comps: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            q_rot: [[1.0; 2]; 256],
            q_half: [[1.0; 2]; 256],
            q_pair: vec![vec![1.0; num_comps]; num_comps],
            arm_pulls: [1.0; 6],
            arm_reward: [1.0; 6],
            total_pulls: 6.0,
            i_pulls: [1.0; NUM_ISLANDS],
            i_reward: [1.0; NUM_ISLANDS],
            total_i_pulls: NUM_ISLANDS as f32,
        }
    }

    pub fn pick_arm(&self, focus: AgentFocus, rng: &mut Mulberry32) -> usize {
        if focus == AgentFocus::Macro && rng.next_f64() < 0.65 {
            return if rng.next_f64() < 0.5 { 0 } else { 2 };
        }
        if focus == AgentFocus::Bridge && rng.next_f64() < 0.55 {
            return 3;
        }
        let log_n = (self.total_pulls as f64).ln();
        let (mut best_a, mut best_ucb) = (0usize, -1e9_f64);
        for a in 0..6 {
            let pulls = self.arm_pulls[a] as f64;
            let ucb = (self.arm_reward[a] as f64) / pulls
                + 1.35 * (log_n / pulls).sqrt()
                + rng.next_f64() * 0.05;
            if ucb > best_ucb {
                best_ucb = ucb;
                best_a = a;
            }
        }
        best_a
    }

    pub fn pick_island(&self, rr_tick: usize, focus: AgentFocus, rng: &mut Mulberry32) -> usize {
        if focus == AgentFocus::Bridge && rng.next_f64() < 0.45 {
            return 2;
        }
        if rr_tick.is_multiple_of(2) {
            return (rr_tick >> 1) % NUM_ISLANDS;
        }
        let log_n = (self.total_i_pulls as f64).ln();
        let (mut best_i, mut best_ucb) = (0usize, -1e9_f64);
        for i in 0..NUM_ISLANDS {
            let pulls = self.i_pulls[i] as f64;
            let ucb = (self.i_reward[i] as f64) / pulls
                + 1.15 * (log_n / pulls).sqrt()
                + rng.next_f64() * 0.08;
            if ucb > best_ucb {
                best_ucb = ucb;
                best_i = i;
            }
        }
        best_i
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn sample_binary(&self, q: [f32; 2], eps: f64, rng: &mut Mulberry32) -> u8 {
        if rng.next_f64() < eps {
            return rng.next_usize(2) as u8;
        }
        let e0 = (q[0] as f64).exp();
        let e1 = (q[1] as f64).exp();
        u8::from(rng.next_f64() < (e1 / (e0 + e1)))
    }

    /// Giảm trọng số Q khi bố cục bị cưỡng bức đưa vào Sổ đen Tabu (`TabuBasin.forceBan`)
    pub fn penalize_force_ban(
        &mut self,
        order: &[usize],
        rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
        comps: &[CompSpec],
    ) {
        for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC) {
            let v = usize::from(rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0);
            self.q_rot[c.cid.0 as usize][v] *= 0.55;
        }
        let pl: Vec<usize> = order
            .iter()
            .copied()
            .filter(|&ci| comps.get(ci).is_some_and(|c| !c.comp_type.is_special()))
            .collect();
        for w in pl.windows(2) {
            self.q_pair[w[0]][w[1]] *= 0.55;
        }
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    pub fn update(
        &mut self,
        unplaced: usize,
        err_cnt: usize,
        score: f64,
        parent_score: Option<f64>,
        isl_improved: bool,
        newly_tabu: bool,
        island: usize,
        arm: Option<usize>,
        order: &[usize],
        rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
        half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
        comps: &[CompSpec],
    ) {
        if newly_tabu {
            for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::IC) {
                let v = usize::from(rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0);
                self.q_rot[c.cid.0 as usize][v] *= 0.72;
            }
            return;
        }
        let imp_bonus = if parent_score.is_some_and(|ps| score < ps) {
            2.4_f32
        } else if isl_improved {
            1.8_f32
        } else {
            0.0_f32
        };
        let base_r = if unplaced > 0 {
            0.0_f32
        } else {
            ((10.0 - (err_cnt as f32)).max(0.0)) * 0.18 + if err_cnt == 0 { 5.0 } else { 0.0 }
        };
        let rew = base_r + imp_bonus;
        let lr = 0.12_f32;

        if imp_bonus > 0.0 {
            for c in comps {
                if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
                    let v = usize::from(rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0);
                    let q = &mut self.q_rot[c.cid.0 as usize][v];
                    *q = (1.0 - lr) * (*q) + lr * rew;
                } else if c.comp_type.is_io() {
                    let def_half = u8::from(c.comp_type == CompType::SW);
                    let v = usize::from(half.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_half) != 0);
                    let q = &mut self.q_half[c.cid.0 as usize][v];
                    *q = (1.0 - lr) * (*q) + lr * rew;
                }
            }
            let pl: Vec<usize> = order
                .iter()
                .copied()
                .filter(|&ci| comps.get(ci).is_some_and(|c| !c.comp_type.is_special()))
                .collect();
            for i in 0..pl.len() {
                for j in (i + 1)..pl.len() {
                    let (u, v) = (pl[i], pl[j]);
                    self.q_pair[u][v] = 0.92 * self.q_pair[u][v] + 0.08 * rew;
                }
            }
        }
        if let Some(a) = arm.filter(|&a| a < 6) {
            self.arm_pulls[a] += 1.0;
            self.arm_reward[a] += imp_bonus + if err_cnt <= 2 { 0.45 } else { 0.04 };
            self.total_pulls += 1.0;
        }
        let isl = island % NUM_ISLANDS;
        self.i_pulls[isl] += 1.0;
        self.i_reward[isl] += imp_bonus
            + if err_cnt == 0 {
                3.5
            } else if err_cnt <= 2 {
                0.65
            } else {
                0.08
            };
        self.total_i_pulls += 1.0;
    }

    /// Sinh thứ tự linh kiện dẫn đường bởi ma trận `q_pair` (`buildGuidedOrder` trong main.html)
    pub fn build_guided_order(&self, comps: &[CompSpec], rng: &mut Mulberry32) -> Vec<usize> {
        let mut place_set: Vec<usize> = (0..comps.len())
            .filter(|&i| !comps[i].comp_type.is_special())
            .collect();
        let mut r_set: Vec<usize> = (0..comps.len())
            .filter(|&i| comps[i].comp_type.is_special())
            .collect();
        rng.shuffle(&mut r_set);
        if place_set.is_empty() {
            return r_set;
        }
        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(comps.len());
        let first_idx = rng.next_usize(place_set.len());
        let mut cur = place_set.remove(first_idx);
        out.push(cur);

        while !place_set.is_empty() {
            let next_pos = if rng.next_f64() < 0.25 {
                rng.next_usize(place_set.len())
            } else {
                let (mut best_pos, mut best_w) = (0usize, -1e9_f64);
                for (pos, &nx) in place_set.iter().enumerate() {
                    let w = (self.q_pair[cur][nx] as f64) + rng.next_f64() * 0.35;
                    if w > best_w {
                        best_w = w;
                        best_pos = pos;
                    }
                }
                best_pos
            };
            cur = place_set.remove(next_pos);
            out.push(cur);
        }
        out.extend(r_set);
        out
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::meta_search::tabu_basin::TabuBasin;
    use crate::meta_search::zobrist::{encode_btn_bend, layout_sig, sol_sig, tabu_key};
    use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, PlacedBtn, PlacedIc};

    #[test]
    fn test_zobrist_tabu_and_rl_bandit() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(1), "U2", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "S1", CompType::SW),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "B1", CompType::BTN2),
        ];
        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 10, c_max: 16, w: 7 });
        layout.btns.push(PlacedBtn {
            cid: CompId(4), id: "B1".into(), comp_type: CompType::BTN2,
            c_min: 1, c_max: 3, row_top: 1, row_bot: 2, flip: 0, bend: -1,
        });
        let s1 = layout_sig(&layout);
        layout.btns[0].bend = 1;
        let s1_bent = layout_sig(&layout);
        assert_ne!(s1, s1_bent); // layout_sig phân biệt rõ trạng thái bend của BTN2

        let s2 = sol_sig(&layout, &[], 0);
        assert_ne!(s1_bent, s2);

        let mut rot = FxHashMap::default();
        rot.insert(CompId(0), 0);
        rot.insert(CompId(1), 1);
        rot.insert(CompId(4), encode_btn_bend(-1));
        let mut half = FxHashMap::default();
        half.insert(CompId(3), 0); // S1 ở nửa trên
        half.insert(CompId(4), 1); // B1 ở nửa dưới
        let t_key = tabu_key(&[0, 1, 3, 4, 2], &rot, &half, &comps);

        let mut half_alt = half.clone();
        half_alt.insert(CompId(3), 1); // Đổi S1 xuống nửa dưới -> tabu_key phải thay đổi
        assert_ne!(t_key, tabu_key(&[0, 1, 3, 4, 2], &rot, &half_alt, &comps));

        let mut tabu = TabuBasin::new();
        for it in 1..=25 {
            let banned = tabu.record(t_key, 1000.0, it);
            if it == 25 {
                assert!(banned);
            }
        }
        assert!(tabu.is_tabu(t_key, 100));
        assert!(!tabu.is_tabu(t_key, 400));

        let mut agent = MetaAgent::default();
        agent.reflect(50, 7, Some(2));
        assert_eq!(agent.focus, AgentFocus::Bridge);
        assert_eq!(agent.dyn_br_pen, Some(42.0));

        let mut rl = RlBandit::new(comps.len());
        let mut rng = Mulberry32::new(42);
        rl.update(0, 0, 500.0, Some(800.0), true, false, 2, Some(1), &[0, 1, 3, 4, 2], &rot, &half, &comps);
        assert!(rl.q_rot[1][1] > 1.0);
        assert!(rl.q_half[3][0] > 1.0); // SW (S1) được cập nhật q_half
        assert!(rl.q_half[4][1] > 1.0); // BTN2 (B1) được cập nhật q_half
        let guided = rl.build_guided_order(&comps, &mut rng);
        assert_eq!(guided.len(), 5);
        assert_eq!(guided[4], 2);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/tabu_basin.rs ===
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct TabuRecord {
    pub visits: u32,
    pub best: f64,
    pub last_imp: u32,
    pub until: u32,
}

/// Sổ Đen Vùng Trũng (`TabuBasin` trong main.html) chống lặp vô tận trên khung IC bị kẹt
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct TabuBasin {
    pub map: FxHashMap<u64, TabuRecord>,
    pub ban_count: u32,
}

impl TabuBasin {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Ghi nhận kết quả thăm vùng trũng `key`. Trả về `true` nếu vừa kích hoạt lệnh cấm Tabu mới.
    pub fn record(&mut self, key: u64, score: f64, cur_it: u32) -> bool {
        if key == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        let rec = self.map.entry(key).or_insert(TabuRecord {
            visits: 0,
            best: score,
            last_imp: cur_it,
            until: 0,
        });
        rec.visits += 1;
        if score < rec.best {
            rec.best = score;
            rec.last_imp = cur_it;
            rec.until = 0;
            return false;
        }
        if rec.visits >= 24 && cur_it.saturating_sub(rec.last_imp) >= 24 && cur_it > rec.until {
            rec.until = cur_it + 360;
            self.ban_count += 1;
            return true;
        }
        false
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn is_tabu(&self, key: u64, cur_it: u32) -> bool {
        key != 0 && self.map.get(&key).is_some_and(|rec| cur_it < rec.until)
    }

    /// Khóa cưỡng bức khung IC hiện tại trong 800 vòng (`TabuBasin.forceBan`)
    pub fn force_ban(&mut self, key: u64, cur_it: u32) {
        if key == 0 {
            return;
        }
        self.map.insert(
            key,
            TabuRecord {
                visits: 99,
                best: 0.0,
                last_imp: 0,
                until: cur_it + 800,
            },
        );
        self.ban_count += 1;
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/meta_search/zobrist.rs ===
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, CompSpec};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType};
use crate::routing::h_route::FastWire;
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

const FNV_OFFSET: u64 = 0xcbf29ce484222325;
const FNV_PRIME: u64 = 0x00000100000001b3;

#[inline(always)]
const fn mix_u64(mut h: u64, val: u64) -> u64 {
    h ^= val;
    h = h.wrapping_mul(FNV_PRIME);
    h ^ (h >> 32)
}

/// Mã hóa góc bẻ chân `BTN2` (`bend: -1, 0, +1`) thành `u8` (`0, 1, 2`)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn encode_btn_bend(bend: i8) -> u8 {
    match bend {
        -1 => 0,
        0 => 1,
        1 => 2,
        _ => 1,
    }
}

/// Giải mã `u8` (`0, 1, 2`) về góc bẻ chân `BTN2` (`bend: -1, 0, +1`)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn decode_btn_bend(code: u8) -> i8 {
    match code {
        0 => -1,
        1 => 0,
        2 => 1,
        _ => 0,
    }
}

/// Băm 64-bit bố cục Đại cục IC/SW/BTN (`layoutSig` trong main.html - bao gồm cả `b.bend` của BTN2)
pub fn layout_sig(layout: &BoardLayout) -> u64 {
    let mut h = FNV_OFFSET;
    for ic in &layout.ics {
        let v = ((ic.cid.0 as u64) << 24)
            | ((ic.rot as u64) << 16)
            | ((ic.c_min as u64) << 8)
            | (ic.c_max as u64);
        h = mix_u64(h, v);
    }
    for sw in &layout.sws {
        let v = ((sw.cid.0 as u64) << 24)
            | ((sw.flip as u64) << 16)
            | (((sw.row as u8) as u64) << 8)
            | (sw.c_min as u64);
        h = mix_u64(h, v);
    }
    for b in &layout.btns {
        let bend_bits = (encode_btn_bend(b.bend) & 0x3) as u64;
        let v = ((b.cid.0 as u64) << 24)
            | (bend_bits << 18)
            | ((b.flip as u64) << 16)
            | (((b.row_top as u8) as u64) << 8)
            | (b.c_min as u64);
        h = mix_u64(h, v);
    }
    h
}

/// Băm 64-bit toàn bộ phương án (`solSig` trong main.html) để lọc trùng O(1)
pub fn sol_sig(layout: &BoardLayout, wires: &[FastWire], err_count: usize) -> u64 {
    let mut h = layout_sig(layout);
    for sp in layout.rs.iter().chain(&layout.leds).chain(&layout.cs) {
        let v = ((sp.cid.0 as u64) << 32)
            | ((sp.c1 as u64) << 24)
            | (((sp.r1 as u8) as u64) << 16)
            | ((sp.c2 as u64) << 8)
            | ((sp.r2 as u8) as u64);
        h = mix_u64(h, v);
    }
    for w in wires {
        let v = ((w.net.0 as u64) << 40)
            | ((w.c1 as u64) << 32)
            | (((w.r1 as u8) as u64) << 24)
            | ((w.c2 as u64) << 16)
            | (((w.r2 as u8) as u64) << 8)
            | ((w.lane as u8) as u64);
        h = mix_u64(h, v);
    }
    mix_u64(h, err_count as u64)
}

/// Khóa định danh vùng trũng Đại cục (`TabuBasin.keyOf` - mở rộng cho cả SW và BTN2 `is_io()` + `btn_bend`)
pub fn tabu_key(
    order: &[usize],
    rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
) -> u64 {
    if order.is_empty() {
        return 0;
    }
    let mut h = FNV_OFFSET;
    for &ci in order {
        if let Some(c) = comps.get(ci) {
            if !c.comp_type.is_special() {
                h = mix_u64(h, (c.cid.0 as u64) + 1);
            }
        }
    }
    for c in comps {
        if c.comp_type == CompType::IC {
            let r = u64::from(rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(0) != 0);
            h = mix_u64(h, ((c.cid.0 as u64) << 8) | r);
        } else if c.comp_type.is_io() {
            let def_half = u8::from(c.comp_type == CompType::SW);
            let hf = u64::from(half.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_half) != 0);
            let bend_bits = if c.comp_type == CompType::BTN2 {
                let def_bend = if hf == 0 { 0u8 } else { 2u8 };
                (rot.get(&c.cid).copied().unwrap_or(def_bend) & 0x3) as u64
            } else {
                0u64
            };
            h = mix_u64(h, ((c.cid.0 as u64) << 8) | (hf << 1) | (bend_bits << 2));
        }
    }
    h
}

/// Khóa định danh bàn giao ca mổ Vi phẫu (`macroSig` của `handoverSeen` trong main.html)
pub fn macro_handover_sig(
    order: &[usize],
    rot: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    half: &FxHashMap<CompId, u8>,
    gaps: &[i32],
    err_count: usize,
    comps: &[CompSpec],
) -> u64 {
    let mut h = tabu_key(order, rot, half, comps);
    for &g in gaps {
        h = mix_u64(h, (g as u64).wrapping_add(100));
    }
    mix_u64(h, err_count as u64)
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/model/bitboard.rs ===
/// Tạo mặt nạ bit u64 cho đoạn cột [c1, c2] (1 <= c <= 63)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn span_mask(c1: u8, c2: u8) -> u64 {
    let lo = if c1 < c2 { c1 } else { c2 };
    let hi = if c1 > c2 { c1 } else { c2 };
    let len = (hi - lo + 1) as u32;
    let ones = if len >= 64 { !0u64 } else { (1u64 << len) - 1 };
    ones << lo
}

/// Kiểm tra cột nguồn hợp lệ (10 nhóm x 5 lỗ từ cột 3..=61, nghỉ tại các cột 8, 14, 20, 26, 32, 38, 44, 50, 56)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn rail_ok(col: u8) -> bool {
    col >= 3 && col <= 61 && (col - 3) % 6 != 5
}

/// Kiểm tra hàng có thuộc 4 thanh nguồn (-2, -1, 10, 11) hay không
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn is_rail_row(row: i8) -> bool {
    row < 0 || row > 9
}

/// Trả về nửa mặt bo: 0 = Nửa trên (hàng 0..=4), 1 = Nửa dưới (hàng 5..=9)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn half_of(row: i8) -> u8 {
    if row <= 4 { 0 } else { 1 }
}

/// Kiểm tra lỗ cắm hợp lệ trên toàn mặt Breadboard (Khớp 100% `BreadboardRenderer.validHole`)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn valid_hole(col: u8, row: i8) -> bool {
    if col < 1 || col > 63 || row < -2 || row > 11 {
        return false;
    }
    if is_rail_row(row) {
        rail_ok(col)
    } else {
        true
    }
}

/// Ánh xạ (row, lane) sang chỉ số mảng `track_masks` (0..19):
/// - lane = 0 (tâm lỗ): row 0..=9 -> index 0..=9
/// - lane = ±1 (rãnh phụ giữa 2 hàng): gap_{r}_{r+1} -> index 10 + r (với r in 0..=3 hoặc 5..=8)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn track_index(row: i8, lane: i8) -> Option<usize> {
    if row < 0 || row > 9 {
        return None;
    }
    match lane {
        0 => Some(row as usize),
        1 => {
            if row == 4 || row == 9 {
                None
            } else {
                Some(10 + (row as usize))
            }
        }
        -1 => {
            if row == 0 || row == 5 {
                None
            } else {
                Some(10 + ((row - 1) as usize))
            }
        }
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// 12 lỗ cắm mặc định bị chiếm bởi 6 dây nhảy nguồn cố định trên Breadboard
pub const DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES: [(u8, i8); 12] = [
    (3, -1), (3, 11), (61, -2), (61, 10),
    (31, -1), (33, -1), (31, 11), (33, 11),
    (31, -2), (33, -2), (31, 10), (33, 10),
];

/// Trạng thái mặt bo siêu tốc trên Stack (~432 bytes) - Thay thế `BreadboardRouter` + Undo Log
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct BoardState {
    /// 14 hàng lỗ cắm: từ row = -2 (index 0) đến row = 11 (index 13)
    pub occupied_holes: [u64; 14],
    /// 10 làn tâm lỗ (0..10) + 9 vị trí rãnh phụ (10..19)
    pub track_masks: [u64; 19],
    /// Mạng điện hiện tại của từng dải 5 lỗ: col_net[half][col] (0 = trống)
    pub col_net: [[u16; 64]; 2],
    /// Cờ đảo thứ tự ưu tiên hàng (tương đương `R.rev` trong JS)
    pub rev: bool,
}

impl BoardState {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new(rev: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            occupied_holes: [0u64; 14],
            track_masks: [0u64; 19],
            col_net: [[0u16; 64]; 2],
            rev,
        }
    }

    /// Khóa 12 lỗ cắm của 6 dây nguồn mặc định (đầu hàm `blockSpace` trong main.html)
    #[inline]
    pub fn occupy_default_power_holes(&mut self) {
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES.len() {
            let (c, r) = DEFAULT_POWER_HOLES[i];
            self.occupy(c, r);
            i += 1;
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_occupied(&self, col: u8, row: i8) -> bool {
        let idx = (row + 2) as usize;
        (self.occupied_holes[idx] & (1u64 << col)) != 0
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn occupy(&mut self, col: u8, row: i8) -> bool {
        let idx = (row + 2) as usize;
        let bit = 1u64 << col;
        if (self.occupied_holes[idx] & bit) != 0 {
            return false;
        }
        self.occupied_holes[idx] |= bit;
        true
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn unoccupy(&mut self, col: u8, row: i8) -> bool {
        let idx = (row + 2) as usize;
        let bit = 1u64 << col;
        if (self.occupied_holes[idx] & bit) == 0 {
            return false;
        }
        self.occupied_holes[idx] &= !bit;
        true
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_segment_free(&self, track_id: usize, c1: u8, c2: u8) -> bool {
        (self.track_masks[track_id] & span_mask(c1, c2)) == 0
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn occupy_segment(&mut self, track_id: usize, c1: u8, c2: u8) {
        self.track_masks[track_id] |= span_mask(c1, c2);
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn unoccupy_segment(&mut self, track_id: usize, c1: u8, c2: u8) {
        self.track_masks[track_id] &= !span_mask(c1, c2);
    }

    /// Tìm làn tâm lỗ (0) hoặc rãnh phụ (±1) còn trống tại hàng `row` cho đoạn `[c1, c2]`
    /// Khớp chính xác 100% thứ tự ưu tiên của `BreadboardRouter.getAvailableTrackForRow`
    #[inline]
    pub fn get_available_track_for_row(&self, row: i8, c1: u8, c2: u8) -> Option<(i8, usize)> {
        let pref_lane = if row == 4 { -1 } else { 1 };
        let lanes = [0, pref_lane, -pref_lane];
        let mask = span_mask(c1, c2);
        for lane in lanes {
            if let Some(tid) = track_index(row, lane) {
                if (self.track_masks[tid] & mask) == 0 {
                    return Some((lane, tid));
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// Đếm số lỗ trống trong 1 dải 5 lỗ tại cột `col` thuộc nửa `half` (0 hoặc 1)
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn free_hole_count(&self, col: u8, half: u8) -> u8 {
        let bit = 1u64 << col;
        let start = if half == 0 { 2 } else { 7 }; // row 0 -> idx 2, row 5 -> idx 7
        let mut cnt = 0;
        let mut r_idx = start;
        while r_idx < start + 5 {
            if (self.occupied_holes[r_idx] & bit) == 0 {
                cnt += 1;
            }
            r_idx += 1;
        }
        cnt
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn has_free_hole(&self, col: u8, half: u8) -> bool {
        self.free_hole_count(col, half) > 0
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/model/circuit.rs ===
use crate::model::bitboard::{track_index, BoardState};
use crate::model::geometry::Footprint;
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Thông số khai báo của 1 linh kiện trong danh sách `components` (main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CompSpec {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub id: String,
    pub comp_type: CompType,
    pub pins: u8,
    pub rot: u8,
    pub custom_pwr: bool,
    pub vcc_pin: Option<u8>,
    pub gnd_pin: Option<u8>,
}

impl CompSpec {
    pub fn new_ic(cid: CompId, id: impl Into<String>, pins: u8, rot: u8) -> Self {
        Self {
            cid,
            id: id.into(),
            comp_type: CompType::IC,
            pins,
            rot,
            custom_pwr: false,
            vcc_pin: Some(pins),
            gnd_pin: Some(pins / 2),
        }
    }

    pub fn new_other(cid: CompId, id: impl Into<String>, comp_type: CompType) -> Self {
        let pins = match comp_type {
            CompType::SW => 3,
            _ => 2,
        };
        Self {
            cid,
            id: id.into(),
            comp_type,
            pins,
            rot: 0,
            custom_pwr: false,
            vcc_pin: None,
            gnd_pin: None,
        }
    }

    /// Khớp 100% hàm `getIcPwrPins(c)` trong main.html
    #[inline]
    pub fn get_ic_pwr_pins(&self) -> (Option<u8>, Option<u8>) {
        if self.comp_type != CompType::IC {
            return (None, None);
        }
        if self.custom_pwr {
            let v = self.vcc_pin.filter(|&p| p >= 1 && p <= self.pins);
            let g = self.gnd_pin.filter(|&p| p >= 1 && p <= self.pins);
            return (v, g);
        }
        (Some(self.pins), Some(self.pins / 2))
    }
}

/// IC đã đặt trên rãnh giữa E-F (Hàng 4 & 5)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PlacedIc {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub id: String,
    pub pins: u8,
    pub rot: u8,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub w: u8,
}

/// Công tắc gạt 3 chân (SW) đã đặt tại hàng 0 hoặc hàng 9
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PlacedSw {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub id: String,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub row: i8,
    pub flip: u8,
}

/// Nút bấm 2 chân (BTN2) đã đặt trên mặt bo (Hỗ trợ 3 TH cắm: bend = 0, -1, +1)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PlacedBtn {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub id: String,
    pub comp_type: CompType,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub row_top: i8,
    pub row_bot: i8,
    pub flip: u8,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bend: i8,
}

impl PlacedBtn {
    /// Hàng lỗ cắm thực tế của 2 chân BTN2: Hàng B (row = 1) ở nửa trên, Hàng I (row = 8) ở nửa dưới
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn pin_row(&self) -> i8 {
        if self.row_top == 7 && self.row_bot == 8 {
            8
        } else {
            self.row_top
        }
    }
}

/// Linh kiện rời 2 chân (R, LED, C, CC) đã đặt trên mặt bo
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PlacedSpecial {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub id: String,
    pub comp_type: CompType,
    pub c1: u8,
    pub r1: i8,
    pub c2: u8,
    pub r2: i8,
    pub locked: bool,
    pub ic_id: Option<CompId>,
}

/// Đoạn dây nối trên Breadboard
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WireSeg {
    pub net: NetId,
    pub net_name: String,
    pub cells: [[i8; 2]; 2],
    pub lane: i8,
    pub trk: Option<usize>,
}

/// Bố cục toàn bộ linh kiện trên mặt bo (Tương đương đối tượng `L` trong buildBoard)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BoardLayout {
    pub ics: Vec<PlacedIc>,
    pub sws: Vec<PlacedSw>,
    pub btns: Vec<PlacedBtn>,
    pub rs: Vec<PlacedSpecial>,
    pub leds: Vec<PlacedSpecial>,
    pub cs: Vec<PlacedSpecial>,
}

impl BoardLayout {
    /// Ánh xạ chân `pin` (1..=pins) của linh kiện `cid` sang lỗ cắm `(col, row)` (`pinHole` trong main.html)
    #[inline]
    pub fn pin_hole(&self, cid: CompId, pin: u8) -> Option<(u8, i8)> {
        if let Some(ic) = self.ics.iter().find(|x| x.cid == cid) {
            let h = ic.pins / 2;
            let (col, row) = if ic.rot == 0 {
                if pin <= h {
                    (ic.c_min + pin - 1, 5)
                } else {
                    (ic.c_min + (ic.pins - pin), 4)
                }
            } else if pin <= h {
                (ic.c_max - pin + 1, 4)
            } else {
                (ic.c_min + pin - h - 1, 5)
            };
            return Some((col, row));
        }
        if let Some(sw) = self.sws.iter().find(|x| x.cid == cid) {
            let col = if sw.flip != 0 {
                sw.c_max - pin + 1
            } else {
                sw.c_min + pin - 1
            };
            return Some((col, sw.row));
        }
        if let Some(b) = self.btns.iter().find(|x| x.cid == cid) {
            let c1 = if b.flip != 0 { b.c_max } else { b.c_min };
            let c2 = if b.flip != 0 { b.c_min } else { b.c_max };
            let r_pin = b.pin_row();
            return Some(if pin == 1 { (c1, r_pin) } else { (c2, r_pin) });
        }
        None
    }

    /// Chặn không gian của IC, SW (1 TH), BTN2 (3 TH: bend = 0, -1, +1), R/LED/C và 6 dây nguồn mặc định
    pub fn block_space(&self, board: &mut BoardState, fps: &mut Vec<Footprint>) {
        board.occupy_default_power_holes();
        for ic in &self.ics {
            fps.push(Footprint::for_ic(ic.c_min, ic.c_max));
            for c in ic.c_min..=ic.c_max {
                board.occupy(c, 4);
                board.occupy(c, 5);
            }
            if let Some(t4) = track_index(4, 0) {
                board.occupy_segment(t4, ic.c_min, ic.c_max);
            }
            if let Some(t5) = track_index(5, 0) {
                board.occupy_segment(t5, ic.c_min, ic.c_max);
            }
        }
        for sw in &self.sws {
            fps.push(Footprint::for_sw(sw.c_min, sw.c_max, sw.row));
            for c in sw.c_min..=sw.c_max {
                board.occupy(c, sw.row);
            }
            for lane in [0, -1, 1] {
                if let Some(tid) = track_index(sw.row, lane) {
                    board.occupy_segment(tid, sw.c_min, sw.c_max);
                }
            }
        }
        for sp in self.rs.iter().chain(self.leds.iter()).chain(self.cs.iter()) {
            board.occupy(sp.c1, sp.r1);
            board.occupy(sp.c2, sp.r2);
        }
        for btn in &self.btns {
            let r_pin = btn.pin_row();
            let r_up = r_pin - 1;
            let r_dn = r_pin + 1;
            let c_mid = u8::midpoint(btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
            fps.push(Footprint::for_btn_bent(btn.c_min, btn.c_max, r_pin, btn.bend));

            // Cả 3 TH của BTN2 đều chiếm cứng 3 ô trên hàng chân cắm (VD: (B,1), (B,2), (B,3)) và 3 làn AB, B, BC
            for c in btn.c_min..=btn.c_max {
                board.occupy(c, r_pin);
            }
            for lane in [-1, 0, 1] {
                if let Some(tid) = track_index(r_pin, lane) {
                    board.occupy_segment(tid, btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
                }
            }

            match btn.bend {
                -1 => {
                    // TH2 (bẻ nhích lên về phía A / r_up): Khóa thêm ô giữa (A,2), khóa làn A (0), nhả hoàn toàn hàng C!
                    if r_up >= 0 {
                        board.occupy(c_mid, r_up);
                        if let Some(tid_up) = track_index(r_up, 0) {
                            board.occupy_segment(tid_up, btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
                        }
                    }
                }
                1 => {
                    // TH3 (bẻ nhích xuống về phía C / r_dn): Khóa thêm ô giữa (C,2), khóa làn C (0), nhả hoàn toàn hàng A!
                    if r_dn <= 9 {
                        board.occupy(c_mid, r_dn);
                        if let Some(tid_dn) = track_index(r_dn, 0) {
                            board.occupy_segment(tid_dn, btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {
                    // TH1 (cắm thẳng, bend = 0): Mở lỗ cắm trên A và C cho dây dọc, nhưng chặn đủ 5 làn A, AB, B, BC, C
                    if r_up >= 0 {
                        if let Some(tid_up) = track_index(r_up, 0) {
                            board.occupy_segment(tid_up, btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
                        }
                    }
                    if r_dn <= 9 {
                        if let Some(tid_dn) = track_index(r_dn, 0) {
                            board.occupy_segment(tid_dn, btn.c_min, btn.c_max);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Một mạng điện sau khi phân tích từ Netlist
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ParsedNet {
    pub id: NetId,
    pub name: String,
    pub pins: Vec<PinRef>,
    pub pin_strs: Vec<String>,
    pub is_star: bool,
}

/// Kết quả trả về của `parse_netlist` (Khớp 100% đối tượng `parsed` trong main.html + `sym_led_groups`)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ParsedNetlist {
    pub errors: Vec<String>,
    pub warns: Vec<String>,
    pub nets: Vec<ParsedNet>,
    pub net_of: FxHashMap<PinRef, NetId>,
    pub cap_ic_hint: FxHashMap<CompId, CompId>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sym_led_groups: Vec<Vec<CompId>>,
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_btn2_three_bend_modes_and_sw_block_space() {
        // TH1: bend = 0 tại (B, 1..=3) -> Khóa lỗ (B,1..3), mở lỗ (A,1..3) & (C,1..3), chặn 5 làn A, AB, B, BC, C
        let mut l0 = BoardLayout::default();
        l0.btns.push(PlacedBtn {
            cid: CompId(1), id: "B1".into(), comp_type: CompType::BTN2,
            c_min: 1, c_max: 3, row_top: 1, row_bot: 2, flip: 0, bend: 0,
        });
        let (mut b0, mut fps0) = (BoardState::new(false), Vec::new());
        l0.block_space(&mut b0, &mut fps0);
        assert!(b0.is_occupied(1, 1) && b0.is_occupied(2, 1) && b0.is_occupied(3, 1));
        assert!(!b0.is_occupied(2, 0) && !b0.is_occupied(2, 2));
        assert!(!b0.is_segment_free(track_index(0, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));
        assert!(!b0.is_segment_free(track_index(2, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));

        // TH2: bend = -1 (nhích lên A) -> Khóa thêm lỗ (A,2), mở lỗ (A,1)/(A,3) cho dây dọc, nhả hoàn toàn làn C!
        let mut l_up = BoardLayout::default();
        l_up.btns.push(PlacedBtn { bend: -1, ..l0.btns[0].clone() });
        let (mut b_up, mut fps_up) = (BoardState::new(false), Vec::new());
        l_up.block_space(&mut b_up, &mut fps_up);
        assert!(b_up.is_occupied(2, 0) && !b_up.is_occupied(1, 0) && !b_up.is_occupied(3, 0));
        assert!(!b_up.is_segment_free(track_index(0, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));
        assert!(b_up.is_segment_free(track_index(2, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));

        // TH3: bend = +1 (nhích xuống C) -> Khóa thêm lỗ (C,2), mở lỗ (C,1)/(C,3) cho dây dọc, nhả hoàn toàn làn A!
        let mut l_dn = BoardLayout::default();
        l_dn.btns.push(PlacedBtn { bend: 1, ..l0.btns[0].clone() });
        let (mut b_dn, mut fps_dn) = (BoardState::new(false), Vec::new());
        l_dn.block_space(&mut b_dn, &mut fps_dn);
        assert!(b_dn.is_occupied(2, 2) && !b_dn.is_occupied(1, 2) && !b_dn.is_occupied(3, 2));
        assert!(b_dn.is_segment_free(track_index(0, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));
        assert!(!b_dn.is_segment_free(track_index(2, 0).unwrap(), 1, 3));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/model/geometry.rs ===
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// Tính khoảng cách Euclid siêu tốc bằng lệnh `f64.sqrt` phần cứng của CPU/WASM
#[inline(always)]
pub fn fast_hypot(dx: f64, dy: f64) -> f64 {
    (dx * dx + dy * dy).sqrt()
}

/// Khung giới hạn vật lý (Bounding Box) của IC, SW, BTN2
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Footprint {
    pub is_sw: bool,
    pub c0: f64,
    pub c1: f64,
    pub r0: f64,
    pub r1: f64,
}

impl Footprint {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn for_ic(c_min: u8, c_max: u8) -> Self {
        Self {
            is_sw: false,
            c0: (c_min as f64) - 0.45,
            c1: (c_max as f64) + 0.45,
            r0: 3.7,
            r1: 5.3,
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn for_sw(c_min: u8, c_max: u8, row: i8) -> Self {
        Self {
            is_sw: true,
            c0: (c_min as f64) - 0.45,
            c1: (c_max as f64) + 0.45,
            r0: (row as f64) - 0.45,
            r1: (row as f64) + 0.45,
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn for_btn(c_min: u8, c_max: u8, row_top: i8, row_bot: i8) -> Self {
        Self {
            is_sw: false,
            c0: (c_min as f64) - 0.45,
            c1: (c_max as f64) + 0.45,
            r0: (row_top as f64) - 0.3,
            r1: (row_bot as f64) + 0.3,
        }
    }

    /// Footprint BTN2 theo 3 trường hợp cắm (#2: TH1 bend=0, TH2 bend=-1 nhích lên, TH3 bend=+1 nhích xuống)
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn for_btn_bent(c_min: u8, c_max: u8, row_pin: i8, bend: i8) -> Self {
        let (r0, r1) = match bend {
            -1 => ((row_pin as f64) - 1.15, (row_pin as f64) + 0.45),
            1 => ((row_pin as f64) - 0.45, (row_pin as f64) + 1.15),
            _ => ((row_pin as f64) - 1.10, (row_pin as f64) + 1.10),
        };
        Self {
            is_sw: false,
            c0: (c_min as f64) - 0.45,
            c1: (c_max as f64) + 0.45,
            r0,
            r1,
        }
    }
}

/// Tọa độ Y trực quan có tính khoảng cách rãnh nguồn AS/JS & rãnh E-F (`visY` trong main.html)
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn vis_y(row: i8) -> f64 {
    let mut y = row as i32;
    if row >= 0 {
        y += 2;
    }
    if row >= 5 {
        y += 2;
    }
    if row >= 10 {
        y += 2;
    }
    y as f64
}

/// Kiểm tra đoạn thẳng đứng tại cột `col` từ `r_a` đến `r_b` không đè lên thân IC/SW/BTN2 (`R.vSegFree`)
#[inline]
pub fn v_seg_free(fps: &[Footprint], col: u8, r_a: i8, r_b: i8) -> bool {
    let lo = (r_a.min(r_b) as f64) + 0.35;
    let hi = (r_a.max(r_b) as f64) - 0.35;
    if hi <= lo {
        return true;
    }
    let c = col as f64;
    let is_pwr_jump = r_a < 0 || r_a > 9 || r_b < 0 || r_b > 9;
    for f in fps {
        if is_pwr_jump && f.is_sw && c > f.c0 && c < f.c1 {
            continue;
        }
        if c > f.c0 && c < f.c1 && lo.max(f.r0) < hi.min(f.r1) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    true
}

/// Kiểm tra đoạn thẳng (x1, y1) -> (x2, y2) có cắt qua thân linh kiện nào không (`R.segHitsFp`)
/// Đề xuất #2: Cho phép dây nguồn từ lỗ sát SW (như hàng I=8 hoặc B=1) nhảy qua SW ở hàng J=9 / A=0 ra thẳng Rail!
#[inline]
pub fn seg_hits_fp(fps: &[Footprint], x1: f64, y1: f64, x2: f64, y2: f64) -> bool {
    if fps.is_empty() {
        return false;
    }
    let min_x = x1.min(x2);
    let max_x = x1.max(x2);
    let min_y = y1.min(y2);
    let max_y = y1.max(y2);
    let dx = x2 - x1;
    let dy = y2 - y1;
    let is_pwr_jump = y1 < 0.0 || y1 > 9.0 || y2 < 0.0 || y2 > 9.0;

    for f in fps {
        if max_x <= f.c0 || min_x >= f.c1 || max_y <= f.r0 || min_y >= f.r1 {
            continue;
        }
        if is_pwr_jump && f.is_sw && x1 > f.c0 && x1 < f.c1 {
            continue;
        }
        let mut t0 = 0.0_f64;
        let mut t1 = 1.0_f64;
        if dx == 0.0 {
            if x1 <= f.c0 || x1 >= f.c1 {
                continue;
            }
        } else {
            let inv_x = 1.0 / dx;
            let mut tx0 = (f.c0 - x1) * inv_x;
            let mut tx1 = (f.c1 - x1) * inv_x;
            if tx0 > tx1 {
                core::mem::swap(&mut tx0, &mut tx1);
            }
            if tx0 > t0 {
                t0 = tx0;
            }
            if tx1 < t1 {
                t1 = tx1;
            }
            if t0 > t1 {
                continue;
            }
        }
        if dy == 0.0 {
            if y1 <= f.r0 || y1 >= f.r1 {
                continue;
            }
        } else {
            let inv_y = 1.0 / dy;
            let mut ty0 = (f.r0 - y1) * inv_y;
            let mut ty1 = (f.r1 - y1) * inv_y;
            if ty0 > ty1 {
                core::mem::swap(&mut ty0, &mut ty1);
            }
            if ty0 > t0 {
                t0 = ty0;
            }
            if ty1 < t1 {
                t1 = ty1;
            }
            if t0 > t1 {
                continue;
            }
        }
        return true;
    }
    false
}

#[inline(always)]
pub fn pt_seg_dist_sq(px: f64, py: f64, x1: f64, y1: f64, x2: f64, y2: f64) -> f64 {
    let dx = x2 - x1;
    let dy = y2 - y1;
    let l2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if l2 == 0.0 {
        return (px - x1) * (px - x1) + (py - y1) * (py - y1);
    }
    let t = (((px - x1) * dx + (py - y1) * dy) / l2).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    let proj_x = px - (x1 + t * dx);
    let proj_y = py - (y1 + t * dy);
    proj_x * proj_x + proj_y * proj_y
}

#[inline(always)]
fn ccw(a: [f64; 2], b: [f64; 2], c: [f64; 2]) -> f64 {
    (c[1] - a[1]) * (b[0] - a[0]) - (b[1] - a[1]) * (c[0] - a[0])
}

#[inline(always)]
pub fn segs_strictly_intersect(p1: [f64; 2], p2: [f64; 2], p3: [f64; 2], p4: [f64; 2]) -> bool {
    let d1 = ccw(p3, p4, p1);
    let d2 = ccw(p3, p4, p2);
    let d3 = ccw(p1, p2, p3);
    let d4 = ccw(p1, p2, p4);
    ((d1 > 0.0 && d2 < 0.0) || (d1 < 0.0 && d2 > 0.0))
        && ((d3 > 0.0 && d4 < 0.0) || (d3 < 0.0 && d4 > 0.0))
}

#[inline]
pub fn seg_dist_sq(p1: [f64; 2], p2: [f64; 2], p3: [f64; 2], p4: [f64; 2]) -> f64 {
    if segs_strictly_intersect(p1, p2, p3, p4) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    pt_seg_dist_sq(p1[0], p1[1], p3[0], p3[1], p4[0], p4[1])
        .min(pt_seg_dist_sq(p2[0], p2[1], p3[0], p3[1], p4[0], p4[1]))
        .min(pt_seg_dist_sq(p3[0], p3[1], p1[0], p1[1], p2[0], p2[1]))
        .min(pt_seg_dist_sq(p4[0], p4[1], p1[0], p1[1], p2[0], p2[1]))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_sw_power_jump_not_blocked_by_own_footprint() {
        let fps = vec![Footprint::for_sw(10, 12, 9)];
        // Dây nguồn từ hàng I (r=8) tại cột 10 nhảy qua hàng J (r=9) xuống thanh nguồn (r=10) không bị chặn!
        assert!(!seg_hits_fp(&fps, 10.0, 8.0, 10.0, 10.0));
        assert!(v_seg_free(&fps, 10, 8, 10));
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// ID số nguyên của Linh kiện (Thay thế chuỗi "U1", "R1", "L1"... trong vòng lặp nóng)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CompId(pub u8);

/// ID số nguyên của Mạng điện (0 = Trống, 1 = VCC, 2 = GND, >=3 = Các mạng tín hiệu/Tag nguồn)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NetId(pub u16);

impl NetId {
    pub const NONE: NetId = NetId(0);
    pub const VCC: NetId = NetId(1);
    pub const GND: NetId = NetId(2);

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_none(self) -> bool {
        self.0 == 0
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_power(self) -> bool {
        self.0 == 1 || self.0 == 2
    }
}

/// Tham chiếu cực kỳ nhỏ gọn tới 1 chân linh kiện (2 bytes trên Stack)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct PinRef {
    pub comp: CompId,
    pub pin: u8, // Chân đánh số từ 1..=pins
}

/// Phân loại linh kiện trên Breadboard (Đã loại bỏ hoàn toàn BTN4)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum CompType {
    IC,   // Vi mạch DIP (8..40 chân chẵn)
    R,    // Điện trở (2 chân)
    LED,  // Đèn LED phân cực (Chân 1: Anode +, Chân 2: Cathode -)
    C,    // Tụ hóa phân cực (Chân 1: +, Chân 2: -)
    CC,   // Tụ gốm không phân cực (2 chân)
    SW,   // Công tắc gạt 3 chân
    BTN2, // Nút bấm 2 chân
}

impl CompType {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_special(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, CompType::R | CompType::LED | CompType::C | CompType::CC)
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn is_io(self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, CompType::BTN2 | CompType::SW)
    }
}

/// Bộ sinh số giả ngẫu nhiên Mulberry32 khớp bit-for-bit với hàm `mulberry32(a)` trong main.html
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Mulberry32 {
    state: u32,
}

impl Mulberry32 {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new(seed: u32) -> Self {
        Self { state: seed }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn next_f64(&mut self) -> f64 {
        self.state = self.state.wrapping_add(0x6D2B79F5);
        let mut t = self.state;
        t = (t ^ (t >> 15)).wrapping_mul(t | 1);
        t = t.wrapping_add((t ^ (t >> 7)).wrapping_mul(t | 61)) ^ t;
        ((t ^ (t >> 14)) as f64) / 4294967296.0
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn next_usize(&mut self, max_exclusive: usize) -> usize {
        if max_exclusive <= 1 {
            return 0;
        }
        (self.next_f64() * (max_exclusive as f64)) as usize
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn shuffle<T>(&mut self, slice: &mut [T]) {
        let len = slice.len();
        for i in (1..len).rev() {
            let j = self.next_usize(i + 1);
            slice.swap(i, j);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_comp_type_and_mulberry32() {
        assert_eq!(NetId::default(), NetId::NONE);
        assert!(CompType::R.is_special());
        assert!(CompType::BTN2.is_io());
        let mut rng = Mulberry32::new(1);
        assert!((rng.next_f64() - 0.6270739405881613).abs() < 1e-15);
        assert!((rng.next_f64() - 0.002735721180215478).abs() < 1e-15);
    }
}
