=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/parser/netlist.rs ===
use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, ParsedNet, ParsedNetlist};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use crate::parser::safety::check_circuit_safety;
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;

/// Kiểm tra chuỗi có định dạng mã linh kiện (Chữ cái in hoa ở đầu + Chữ số ở cuối, VD: "U1", "CC12")
#[inline]
pub fn is_comp_id_format(s: &str) -> bool {
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() && bytes[i].is_ascii_uppercase() {
        i += 1;
    }
    if i == 0 || i == bytes.len() {
        return false;
    }
    while i < bytes.len() && bytes[i].is_ascii_digit() {
        i += 1;
    }
    i == bytes.len()
}

/// Tách token dạng `COMP:PIN` (VD: "U1:14" -> Some(("U1", 14)))
#[inline]
pub fn parse_comp_pin(tok: &str) -> Option<(&str, u8)> {
    let (comp, pin_str) = tok.split_once(':')?;
    if !is_comp_id_format(comp) {
        return None;
    }
    Some((comp, pin_str.parse::<u8>().ok()?))
}

/// Chuẩn hóa ký hiệu chân phân cực `A/+` -> `1`, `K/-` -> `2` (`normPinToken` trong main.html)
fn norm_pin_token(tok: &str) -> String {
    if let Some((comp, pin_part)) = tok.split_once(':') {
        if is_comp_id_format(comp) {
            let mapped = match pin_part {
                "A" | "+" => Some("1"),
                "K" | "-" => Some("2"),
                _ if !pin_part.is_empty() && pin_part.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) => Some(pin_part),
                _ => None,
            };
            if let Some(p) = mapped {
                return format!("{comp}:{p}");
            }
        }
    }
    tok.to_string()
}

/// Nhận diện nhãn tên mạng ở đầu câu lệnh (VD: `*CLK: U1:2 -> U2:3` hoặc `SIG_A = U1:1 U2:2`)
fn extract_net_label<'a>(stmt: &'a str, by_id: &FxHashMap<&str, &CompSpec>) -> (Option<String>, &'a str) {
    let Some(sep_idx) = stmt.find([':', '=']) else { return (None, stmt); };
    let cand_raw = stmt[..sep_idx].trim();
    let rest = stmt[sep_idx + 1..].trim();
    if cand_raw.is_empty() || rest.is_empty() { return (None, stmt); }
    let without_star = cand_raw.strip_prefix('*').unwrap_or(cand_raw);
    let mut chars = without_star.chars();
    let Some(first_ch) = chars.next() else { return (None, stmt); };
    if !(first_ch.is_ascii_alphabetic() || first_ch == '_')
        || !chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
    {
        return (None, stmt);
    }
    let cand_upper = cand_raw.to_ascii_uppercase();
    if by_id.contains_key(cand_upper.as_str()) {
        let first_tok = rest.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("");
        let first_tok = first_tok.trim_end_matches("->").trim_end_matches('→');
        let is_pin_suffix = matches!(first_tok, "A" | "a" | "K" | "k" | "+" | "-")
            || (!first_tok.is_empty() && first_tok.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()));
        if is_pin_suffix { return (None, stmt); }
    }
    (Some(cand_upper), rest)
}

/// Trích xuất chỉ dẫn `#SYM_LED:` (VD: `#SYM_LED: L1=L2, L3=L4` hoặc `#SYM_LED: L1:L2:L3`) (#10)
fn parse_sym_led_directive(
    raw_line: &str,
    by_id: &FxHashMap<&str, &CompSpec>,
    sym_led_groups: &mut Vec<Vec<CompId>>,
) {
    let upper = raw_line.to_ascii_uppercase();
    let Some(pos) = upper.find("SYM_LED:") else { return };
    let prefix = upper[..pos].trim_end();
    if !prefix.ends_with('#') && !prefix.ends_with("//") && !prefix.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let spec = upper[pos + "SYM_LED:".len()..].trim();
    if spec.is_empty() {
        return;
    }
    let chunks: Vec<&str> = if spec.contains([',', ';']) {
        spec.split([',', ';']).collect()
    } else if spec.contains(['=', ':', '-', '/']) {
        spec.split_whitespace().collect()
    } else {
        vec![spec]
    };
    for chunk in chunks {
        let mut grp = Vec::new();
        for tok in chunk.split(['=', ':', '-', '/', ' ', '\t']) {
            let t = tok.trim();
            if let Some(c) = by_id.get(t).filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::LED) {
                if !grp.contains(&c.cid) {
                    grp.push(c.cid);
                }
            }
        }
        if grp.len() >= 2 && !sym_led_groups.contains(&grp) {
            sym_led_groups.push(grp);
        }
    }
}

/// Phân tích cú pháp Netlist chuẩn BSE-Netlist V2 & kiểm tra an toàn mạch (`parseNetlist` trong main.html)
pub fn parse_netlist(text: &str, comps: &[CompSpec]) -> ParsedNetlist {
    let mut errors = Vec::new();
    let mut warns = Vec::new();
    let mut cap_ic_hint: FxHashMap<CompId, CompId> = FxHashMap::default();
    let mut sym_led_groups: Vec<Vec<CompId>> = Vec::new();
    let by_id: FxHashMap<&str, &CompSpec> = comps.iter().map(|c| (c.id.as_str(), c)).collect();

    let mut parent: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
    let mut node_names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let mut node_idx: FxHashMap<String, usize> = FxHashMap::default();

    let mut ensure_node = |name: &str, parent: &mut Vec<usize>, node_names: &mut Vec<String>| -> usize {
        if let Some(&idx) = node_idx.get(name) { return idx; }
        let idx = parent.len();
        parent.push(idx);
        node_names.push(name.to_string());
        node_idx.insert(name.to_string(), idx);
        idx
    };

    fn find(parent: &mut [usize], mut x: usize) -> usize {
        while parent[x] != x { parent[x] = parent[parent[x]]; x = parent[x]; }
        x
    }

    let mut statements: Vec<(String, String)> = Vec::new();
    for (line_idx, raw_line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
        parse_sym_led_directive(raw_line, &by_id, &mut sym_led_groups);
        let clean = raw_line.split('#').next().unwrap_or("").split("//").next().unwrap_or("").trim();
        if clean.is_empty() { continue; }
        for part in clean.split([';', ',']) {
            let s = part.trim();
            if !s.is_empty() { statements.push((s.to_string(), format!("Dòng {}", line_idx + 1))); }
        }
    }

    for (raw_stmt, ln) in statements {
        let (net_label, stmt) = extract_net_label(&raw_stmt, &by_id);
        let has_arrow = stmt.contains("->") || stmt.contains('→');
        let norm_str = stmt.to_uppercase().replace('→', "->").replace("->", " -> ");
        let stripped: Vec<String> = norm_str
            .split_whitespace()
            .filter(|&t| t != "->" && t != "-")
            .map(norm_pin_token)
            .collect();

        if let Some(stmt_ic) = stripped.iter().find_map(|t| {
            let c = by_id.get(t.split(':').next()?)?;
            (c.comp_type == CompType::IC).then_some(c.cid)
        }) {
            for t in &stripped {
                if let Some(c) = by_id.get(t.split(':').next().unwrap_or("")) {
                    if matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::CC | CompType::C) {
                        cap_ic_hint.entry(c.cid).or_insert(stmt_ic);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        let is_rev_flow = has_arrow && stripped.len() > 1
            && ((stripped[0] == "GND" && stripped.last().unwrap() != "GND")
                || (stripped.last().unwrap() == "VCC" && stripped[0] != "VCC"));

        let mut expanded: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        let len = stripped.len();
        for (i, t) in stripped.iter().enumerate() {
            if let Some(c) = by_id.get(t.as_str()) {
                if c.pins == 2 && has_arrow {
                    let rev_pol = is_rev_flow && matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::LED | CompType::C);
                    let (p_in, p_out) = if rev_pol { ("2", "1") } else { ("1", "2") };
                    if i > 0 && i + 1 < len {
                        expanded.push(format!("{t}:{p_in}"));
                        expanded.push(format!("{t}:{p_out}"));
                    } else if i == 0 && len > 1 {
                        expanded.push(format!("{t}:{p_out}"));
                    } else if i + 1 == len && len > 1 {
                        expanded.push(format!("{t}:{p_in}"));
                    } else {
                        expanded.push(t.clone());
                    }
                    continue;
                }
            }
            expanded.push(t.clone());
        }

        let mut segments: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::new();
        let mut cur_seg: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
        for cur in expanded {
            if let Some(prev) = cur_seg.last() {
                if has_arrow {
                    if let (Some((c_p, p_p)), Some((c_c, p_c))) = (parse_comp_pin(prev), parse_comp_pin(&cur)) {
                        if c_p == c_c && p_p != p_c && by_id.get(c_p).is_some_and(|c| c.comp_type != CompType::IC) {
                            segments.push(cur_seg);
                            cur_seg = vec![cur];
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            cur_seg.push(cur);
        }
        if !cur_seg.is_empty() { segments.push(cur_seg); }
        if let Some(lbl) = net_label {
            if let Some(first_seg) = segments.first_mut() { first_seg.insert(0, lbl); }
        }

        for toks in segments {
            if toks.len() < 2 {
                errors.push(format!("{ln}: cụm \"{}\" cần ít nhất 2 nút để nối dây", toks.join(" ")));
                continue;
            }
            let mut ok = true;
            for t in &toks {
                if let Some((c_str, p_num)) = parse_comp_pin(t) {
                    match by_id.get(c_str) {
                        None => { errors.push(format!("{ln}: không có linh kiện {c_str}")); ok = false; }
                        Some(c) if p_num < 1 || p_num > c.pins => {
                            errors.push(format!("{ln}: {t} ngoài dải 1-{}", c.pins));
                            ok = false;
                        }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                } else if by_id.contains_key(t.as_str()) {
                    errors.push(format!("{ln}: {t} thiếu số chân (dạng {t}:1 hoặc dùng mũi tên ->)"));
                    ok = false;
                }
            }
            if !ok { continue; }
            let first_idx = ensure_node(&toks[0], &mut parent, &mut node_names);
            for t in &toks[1..] {
                let tidx = ensure_node(t, &mut parent, &mut node_names);
                let (ra, rb) = (find(&mut parent, first_idx), find(&mut parent, tidx));
                parent[ra] = rb;
            }
        }
    }

    let mut groups: Vec<(usize, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..node_names.len() {
        let r = find(&mut parent, i);
        if let Some(grp) = groups.iter_mut().find(|(root, _)| *root == r) {
            grp.1.push(node_names[i].clone());
        } else {
            groups.push((r, vec![node_names[i].clone()]));
        }
    }

    let (mut nets, mut net_of, mut k, mut next_sig_id) = (Vec::new(), FxHashMap::default(), 0usize, 3u16);
    for (_, g) in groups {
        let (has_v, has_g) = (g.iter().any(|n| n == "VCC"), g.iter().any(|n| n == "GND"));
        if has_v && has_g {
            errors.push("Ngắn mạch trực tiếp: VCC và GND bị nối chung trong cùng một mạng".to_string());
        }
        let bare: Vec<&String> = g.iter().filter(|n| !n.contains(':') && *n != "VCC" && *n != "GND").collect();
        let star_name = bare.iter().find(|n| n.starts_with('*')).copied();
        let clean_named = star_name.or_else(|| bare.first().copied()).map(|s| s.trim_start_matches('*').to_string());
        let name = if has_v { "VCC".into() } else if has_g { "GND".into() } else if let Some(cn) = clean_named.filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) { cn } else { k += 1; format!("NET{k}") };
        let net_id = if name == "VCC" { NetId::VCC } else if name == "GND" { NetId::GND } else { let id = NetId(next_sig_id); next_sig_id += 1; id };
        let is_star = star_name.is_some() || g.iter().any(|n| n.starts_with('*'));
        let (mut pin_strs, mut pins) = (Vec::new(), Vec::new());
        for tok in g {
            if tok.contains(':') {
                if let Some((c_str, p_num)) = parse_comp_pin(&tok) {
                    if let Some(c) = by_id.get(c_str) {
                        let pref = PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: p_num };
                        pins.push(pref);
                        net_of.insert(pref, net_id);
                    }
                }
                pin_strs.push(tok);
            }
        }
        nets.push(ParsedNet { id: net_id, name, pins, pin_strs, is_star });
    }

    check_circuit_safety(comps, &nets, &net_of, &mut errors, &mut warns);
    ParsedNetlist { errors, warns, nets, net_of, cap_ic_hint, sym_led_groups }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_parse_sym_led_directive() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "R2", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "L1", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "L2", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(5), "L3", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(6), "L4", CompType::LED),
        ];
        let txt = "
            #SYM_LED: L1=L2, L3:L4
            VCC U1:14; GND U1:7
            U1:1 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND; U1:2 -> R2 -> L2 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        assert_eq!(parsed.sym_led_groups, vec![vec![CompId(3), CompId(4)], vec![CompId(5), CompId(6)]]);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/parser/safety.rs ===
use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, ParsedNet};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId, PinRef};
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};

#[inline]
fn add_sw_edge(sw_adj: &mut FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<NetId>>, na: Option<NetId>, nb: Option<NetId>) {
    if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (na, nb) {
        if a != b {
            sw_adj.entry(a).or_default().push(b);
            sw_adj.entry(b).or_default().push(a);
        }
    }
}

/// Kiểm tra toàn diện an toàn mạch điện & lý thuyết mạch (Khớp 100% nửa sau `parseNetlist` trong main.html)
pub fn check_circuit_safety(
    comps: &[CompSpec],
    nets: &[ParsedNet],
    net_of: &FxHashMap<PinRef, NetId>,
    errors: &mut Vec<String>,
    warns: &mut Vec<String>,
) {
    let by_cid: FxHashMap<CompId, &CompSpec> = comps.iter().map(|c| (c.cid, c)).collect();
    let net_by_id: FxHashMap<NetId, &ParsedNet> = nets.iter().map(|n| (n.id, n)).collect();
    let net_name = |nid: NetId| -> &str { net_by_id.get(&nid).map_or("", |n| n.name.as_str()) };

    let mut sw_adj: FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<NetId>> = FxHashMap::default();

    for c in comps {
        let p = |pin: u8| net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin }).copied();
        if c.pins == 2 {
            if let (Some(n1), Some(n2)) = (p(1), p(2)) {
                if n1 == n2 {
                    errors.push(format!("{}: hai chân 1 và 2 bị nối tắt trong cùng mạng {}", c.id, net_name(n1)));
                }
            }
        }
        if c.comp_type == CompType::BTN2 {
            let (n1, n2) = (p(1), p(2));
            if matches!((n1, n2), (Some(NetId::VCC), Some(NetId::GND)) | (Some(NetId::GND), Some(NetId::VCC))) {
                errors.push(format!("Ngắn mạch VCC và GND khi nhấn nút {}", c.id));
            }
            add_sw_edge(&mut sw_adj, n1, n2);
        } else if c.comp_type == CompType::SW {
            for (pa, pb, pair_lbl) in [(1, 2, "1-2"), (2, 3, "2-3")] {
                let (na, nb) = (p(pa), p(pb));
                if let (Some(a), Some(b)) = (na, nb) {
                    if a == b {
                        errors.push(format!("{}: chân {pair_lbl} của công tắc bị nối tắt sẵn ở mạng {}", c.id, net_name(a)));
                    } else if matches!((a, b), (NetId::VCC, NetId::GND) | (NetId::GND, NetId::VCC)) {
                        errors.push(format!("Ngắn mạch VCC và GND khi gạt công tắc {} (cặp chân {pair_lbl})", c.id));
                    }
                    add_sw_edge(&mut sw_adj, Some(a), Some(b));
                }
            }
        }
        if matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::LED | CompType::C) {
            let (n1, n2) = (p(1), p(2));
            let label = if c.comp_type == CompType::LED { format!("LED {}", c.id) } else { format!("Tụ phân cực {}", c.id) };
            if n1 == Some(NetId::GND) || n2 == Some(NetId::VCC) {
                warns.push(format!("{label} mắc ngược cực tính (Chân 1 [+] đang nối GND hoặc Chân 2 [-] đang nối VCC)"));
            }
            let sw1 = n1.and_then(|n| sw_adj.get(&n)).map_or(&[][..], |v| v.as_slice());
            let sw2 = n2.and_then(|n| sw_adj.get(&n)).map_or(&[][..], |v| v.as_slice());
            if (sw1.contains(&NetId::GND) && (n2 == Some(NetId::VCC) || sw2.contains(&NetId::VCC)))
                || (sw2.contains(&NetId::VCC) && (n1 == Some(NetId::GND) || sw1.contains(&NetId::GND)))
            {
                warns.push(format!("{label} bị cấp ngược cực tính khi đóng công tắc/nút bấm"));
            }
        }
    }

    // Cảnh báo Floating Bounce nếu BTN2/SW nối vào IC mà thiếu điện trở kéo
    for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| matches!(c.comp_type, CompType::BTN2 | CompType::SW)) {
        for pin in 1..=c.pins {
            let Some(&nid) = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin }) else { continue; };
            if nid.is_power() { continue; }
            let Some(net_obj) = net_by_id.get(&nid) else { continue; };
            let has_ic = net_obj.pins.iter().any(|pr| by_cid.get(&pr.comp).is_some_and(|x| x.comp_type == CompType::IC));
            let has_r = net_obj.pins.iter().any(|pr| by_cid.get(&pr.comp).is_some_and(|x| x.comp_type == CompType::R));
            if has_ic && !has_r {
                warns.push(format!("{}:{pin} (net {}) nối trực tiếp vào IC mà không thấy Điện trở kéo/hạn dòng (nguy cơ thả nổi Floating Bounce)", c.id, net_obj.name));
            }
        }
    }

    // Đệ quy kiểm tra mọi đèn LED phải đi nối tiếp qua ít nhất 1 điện trở hạn dòng
    fn is_safe_series_resistor_net(
        nid: NetId,
        visited_leds: &mut FxHashSet<CompId>,
        visited_nets: &mut FxHashSet<NetId>,
        net_by_id: &FxHashMap<NetId, &ParsedNet>,
        by_cid: &FxHashMap<CompId, &CompSpec>,
        net_of: &FxHashMap<PinRef, NetId>,
        sw_adj: &FxHashMap<NetId, Vec<NetId>>,
    ) -> bool {
        if nid.is_none() || nid.is_power() || !visited_nets.insert(nid) { return false; }
        let Some(net_obj) = net_by_id.get(&nid) else { return false; };
        let sw_edges = sw_adj.get(&nid).map_or(&[][..], |v| v.as_slice());
        if sw_edges.iter().any(|e| e.is_power()) { return false; }

        let (mut has_r, mut has_direct_src) = (false, false);
        for pr in &net_obj.pins {
            let Some(comp) = by_cid.get(&pr.comp) else { continue; };
            match comp.comp_type {
                CompType::IC => has_direct_src = true,
                CompType::R => {
                    let other_p = PinRef { comp: comp.cid, pin: if pr.pin == 1 { 2 } else { 1 } };
                    if net_of.get(&other_p).is_some_and(|&on| on != nid) { has_r = true; }
                }
                CompType::LED if visited_leds.insert(comp.cid) => {
                    let n1 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: comp.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
                    let n2 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: comp.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
                    let other_net = if n1 == Some(nid) { n2 } else { n1 };
                    if let Some(on) = other_net {
                        if is_safe_series_resistor_net(on, visited_leds, &mut visited_nets.clone(), net_by_id, by_cid, net_of, sw_adj) {
                            has_r = true;
                        }
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        if !has_r && !has_direct_src && !sw_edges.is_empty() {
            if sw_edges.iter().all(|&to_n| {
                is_safe_series_resistor_net(to_n, &mut visited_leds.clone(), &mut visited_nets.clone(), net_by_id, by_cid, net_of, sw_adj)
            }) {
                has_r = true;
            }
        }
        has_r && !has_direct_src
    }

    for c in comps.iter().filter(|c| c.comp_type == CompType::LED) {
        let n1 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 1 }).copied();
        let n2 = net_of.get(&PinRef { comp: c.cid, pin: 2 }).copied();
        if n1.is_none() && n2.is_none() { continue; }
        let ok1 = n1.is_some_and(|id| is_safe_series_resistor_net(id, &mut FxHashSet::from_iter([c.cid]), &mut FxHashSet::default(), &net_by_id, &by_cid, net_of, &sw_adj));
        let ok2 = n2.is_some_and(|id| is_safe_series_resistor_net(id, &mut FxHashSet::from_iter([c.cid]), &mut FxHashSet::default(), &net_by_id, &by_cid, net_of, &sw_adj));
        if !ok1 && !ok2 {
            errors.push(format!("LED {} nối trực tiếp vào nguồn/IC/công tắc mà không đi nối tiếp qua điện trở hạn dòng", c.id));
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use crate::model::circuit::CompSpec;
    use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType};
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_parse_and_safety_checks_match_js() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(1), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "L1", CompType::LED),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "CC1", CompType::CC),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "B1", CompType::BTN2),
        ];
        let txt = "
            VCC U1:14; GND U1:7
            U1:1 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND
            VCC -> U99:1
            *CLK: U1:2 -> U1:3
            U1:14 -> CC1 -> GND
            B1:1 -> U1:4; B1:2 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        assert_eq!(parsed.errors.len(), 1); // Bắt đúng lỗi không có linh kiện U99
        assert_eq!(parsed.warns.len(), 1);  // Bắt đúng cảnh báo B1:1 nối thẳng vào U1:4 thiếu trở kéo
        assert_eq!(parsed.cap_ic_hint.get(&CompId(3)), Some(&CompId(0)));
        assert!(parsed.nets.iter().any(|n| n.name == "CLK" && n.is_star));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/routing/bridge_pair.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::{base_net_of, is_pwr_tag};
use crate::model::bitboard::BoardState;
use crate::model::geometry::{v_seg_free, Footprint};
use crate::routing::h_route::{
    half_span_load, is_potential_bridge_col, route_h, try_row_swap_h, FastWire, PassWires,
};

/// Định tuyến cùng nửa mặt bo `h`: Đi thẳng -> Row Swap -> Dogleg 1 bước -> Dogleg 2 bước (`routeSameHalf`)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn route_same_half(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    tag: u16,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    h: u8,
    allow_swap: bool,
    rem_cross: i32,
    col_hist: &[f32; 65],
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
    if c1 == c2 {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    if let Some(w) = route_h(board, fps, tag, c1, c2, h, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights) {
        return Some(vec![w]);
    }
    if allow_swap {
        if let Some(w) = try_row_swap_h(board, pass_wires, tag, c1, c2, h) {
            return Some(vec![w]);
        }
    }

    let is_pwr = is_pwr_tag(tag) && tag >= 0x4000;
    let mut mids = [0u8; 64];
    let mut m_len = 0usize;
    for cm in 1u8..=63 {
        if cm == c1 || cm == c2 {
            continue;
        }
        let cur = board.col_net[h as usize][cm as usize];
        if is_pwr {
            if cur != 0 {
                continue;
            }
        } else if cur != 0 && cur != tag {
            continue;
        }
        if board.free_hole_count(cm, h) < 2 {
            continue;
        }
        mids[m_len] = cm;
        m_len += 1;
    }
    if m_len == 0 {
        return None;
    }

    let mid_slice = &mut mids[..m_len];
    mid_slice.sort_by(|&x, &y| {
        let same_x = if !is_pwr && board.col_net[h as usize][x as usize] == tag { -8.0 } else { 0.0 };
        let same_y = if !is_pwr && board.col_net[h as usize][y as usize] == tag { -8.0 } else { 0.0 };
        let br_x = if is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, x, 0, rem_cross) {
            24.0
        } else if board.col_net[(1 - h) as usize][x as usize] == 0 && rem_cross > 0 {
            8.0
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let br_y = if is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, y, 0, rem_cross) {
            24.0
        } else if board.col_net[(1 - h) as usize][y as usize] == 0 && rem_cross > 0 {
            8.0
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let cx = same_x + br_x + ((x as i32 - c1 as i32).abs() + (x as i32 - c2 as i32).abs()) as f64 + (col_hist[x as usize] as f64) * 4.0;
        let cy = same_y + br_y + ((y as i32 - c1 as i32).abs() + (y as i32 - c2 as i32).abs()) as f64 + (col_hist[y as usize] as f64) * 4.0;
        cx.total_cmp(&cy)
    });

    let max_mids = mid_slice.len().min(30);
    for (idx, &cm) in mid_slice[..max_mids].iter().enumerate() {
        let can_swap_mid = allow_swap && idx < 2;
        let mut forbid_mask = 0u16;
        for att in 0..2 {
            let snap_b = *board;
            let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
            let w1 = route_h(board, fps, tag, c1, cm, h, forbid_mask, rem_cross, row_hist, weights)
                .or_else(|| if can_swap_mid && att == 1 { try_row_swap_h(board, pass_wires, tag, c1, cm, h) } else { None });
            let Some(w1) = w1 else {
                *board = snap_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
                break;
            };
            let w2 = route_h(board, fps, tag, cm, c2, h, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights)
                .or_else(|| if can_swap_mid { try_row_swap_h(board, pass_wires, tag, cm, c2, h) } else { None });
            if let Some(w2) = w2 {
                board.col_net[h as usize][cm as usize] = tag;
                return Some(vec![w1, w2]);
            }
            forbid_mask |= 1u16 << w1.r1;
            *board = snap_b;
            pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
        }
    }

    let top_len = mid_slice.len().min(10);
    for &m1 in &mid_slice[..top_len] {
        let snap1 = *board;
        let Some(w1) = route_h(board, fps, tag, c1, m1, h, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights) else {
            *board = snap1;
            continue;
        };
        for &m2 in &mid_slice[..top_len] {
            if m1 == m2 {
                continue;
            }
            let snap2 = *board;
            if let Some(w2) = route_h(board, fps, tag, m1, m2, h, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights) {
                if let Some(w3) = route_h(board, fps, tag, m2, c2, h, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights) {
                    board.col_net[h as usize][m1 as usize] = tag;
                    board.col_net[h as usize][m2 as usize] = tag;
                    return Some(vec![w1, w2, w3]);
                }
            }
            *board = snap2;
        }
        *board = snap1;
    }
    None
}

/// Nối cặp cột `(c1, h1)` và `(c2, h2)` có san tải 2 nửa bo & cầu rãnh đôi (`connectPair` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn connect_pair(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    tag: u16,
    c1: u8,
    h1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    h2: u8,
    rem_cross: i32,
    col_hist: &[f32; 65],
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
    let w_net = base_net_of(tag);
    if c1 == c2 && h1 == h2 {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    if h1 == h2 {
        if let Some(sh) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, c1, c2, h1, true, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) {
            return Some(sh);
        }
        let oh = 1 - h1;
        let mut b_cols = Vec::with_capacity(32);
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if board.is_occupied(c, 4) || board.is_occupied(c, 5) || !v_seg_free(fps, c, 4, 5) {
                continue;
            }
            let (n0, n1) = (board.col_net[0][c as usize], board.col_net[1][c as usize]);
            if (n0 != 0 && n0 != tag) || (n1 != 0 && n1 != tag) || board.free_hole_count(c, oh) < 2 {
                continue;
            }
            b_cols.push(c);
        }
        let mut near1 = b_cols.clone();
        near1.sort_by(|&x, &y| {
            let cx = (x as i32 - c1 as i32).abs() as f64 + (col_hist[x as usize] as f64) * 2.5;
            let cy = (y as i32 - c1 as i32).abs() as f64 + (col_hist[y as usize] as f64) * 2.5;
            cx.total_cmp(&cy)
        });
        near1.truncate(10);

        let mut near2 = b_cols;
        near2.sort_by(|&x, &y| {
            let cx = (x as i32 - c2 as i32).abs() as f64 + (col_hist[x as usize] as f64) * 2.5;
            let cy = (y as i32 - c2 as i32).abs() as f64 + (col_hist[y as usize] as f64) * 2.5;
            cx.total_cmp(&cy)
        });
        near2.truncate(10);

        for (i1, &b1) in near1.iter().enumerate() {
            if b1 != c1 && board.free_hole_count(b1, h1) < 2 {
                continue;
            }
            let snap1_b = *board;
            let snap1_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
            board.occupy(b1, 4);
            board.occupy(b1, 5);
            board.col_net[0][b1 as usize] = tag;
            board.col_net[1][b1 as usize] = tag;
            let Some(w1) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, c1, b1, h1, i1 < 2, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) else {
                *board = snap1_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap1_w);
                continue;
            };
            for (i2, &b2) in near2.iter().enumerate() {
                if b1 == b2
                    || board.is_occupied(b2, 4)
                    || board.is_occupied(b2, 5)
                    || (b2 != c2 && board.free_hole_count(b2, h1) < 2)
                    || board.free_hole_count(b2, oh) < 2
                {
                    continue;
                }
                let snap2_b = *board;
                let snap2_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
                board.occupy(b2, 4);
                board.occupy(b2, 5);
                board.col_net[0][b2 as usize] = tag;
                board.col_net[1][b2 as usize] = tag;
                if let Some(w_mid) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, b1, b2, oh, i2 < 2, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) {
                    if let Some(w2) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, b2, c2, h1, i2 < 2, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) {
                        let mut out = w1;
                        out.push(FastWire::new_v(w_net, b1, 4, b1, 5));
                        out.extend(w_mid);
                        out.push(FastWire::new_v(w_net, b2, 4, b2, 5));
                        out.extend(w2);
                        return Some(out);
                    }
                }
                *board = snap2_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap2_w);
            }
            *board = snap1_b;
            pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap1_w);
        }
        return None;
    }

    let (c_top, c_bot) = if h1 == 0 { (c1, c2) } else { (c2, c1) };
    let mut cols = Vec::with_capacity(48);
    for c in 1u8..=63 {
        if board.is_occupied(c, 4) || board.is_occupied(c, 5) || !v_seg_free(fps, c, 4, 5) {
            continue;
        }
        let (n0, n1) = (board.col_net[0][c as usize], board.col_net[1][c as usize]);
        if (n0 != 0 && n0 != tag) || (n1 != 0 && n1 != tag) {
            continue;
        }
        cols.push(c);
    }
    cols.sort_by(|&x, &y| {
        let cost_of = |c: u8| -> f64 {
            let own = (if board.col_net[0][c as usize] == tag { -38.0 } else { 0.0 })
                + (if board.col_net[1][c as usize] == tag { -38.0 } else { 0.0 });
            let load_top = half_span_load(board, row_hist, 0, c_top, c);
            let load_bot = half_span_load(board, row_hist, 1, c, c_bot);
            own + ((c as i32 - c_top as i32).abs() + (c as i32 - c_bot as i32).abs()) as f64
                + (col_hist[c as usize] as f64) * 4.2
                + (load_top + load_bot) * 2.4
        };
        cost_of(x).total_cmp(&cost_of(y))
    });

    for (idx, &c) in cols.iter().enumerate() {
        if (c != c_top && board.free_hole_count(c, 0) < 2)
            || (c != c_bot && board.free_hole_count(c, 1) < 2)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let use_swap = idx < 5;
        let snap_b = *board;
        let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
        board.occupy(c, 4);
        board.occupy(c, 5);
        board.col_net[0][c as usize] = tag;
        board.col_net[1][c as usize] = tag;
        if let Some(w_top) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, c_top, c, 0, use_swap, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) {
            if let Some(w_bot) = route_same_half(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, c, c_bot, 1, use_swap, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights) {
                let mut out = w_top;
                out.push(FastWire::new_v(w_net, c, 4, c, 5));
                out.extend(w_bot);
                return Some(out);
            }
        }
        *board = snap_b;
        pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
    }
    None
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_route_same_half_dogleg_and_connect_pair_bridge() {
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::new(true);
        let fps = vec![Footprint::for_ic(10, 16)];
        let col_hist = [0.0f32; 65];
        let row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        // Chặn lệch các hàng ở cột 5 và cột 20 để ép nhảy dogleg 2 đoạn qua cột trung gian
        for r in [0, 1, 2, 4] { board.occupy(5, r); }   // Cột 5 chỉ trống hàng 3
        for r in [0, 1, 3, 4] { board.occupy(20, r); }  // Cột 20 chỉ trống hàng 2
        let dogleg = route_same_half(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, 3, 5, 20, 0, true, 0, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights,
        ).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(dogleg.len(), 2);

        // Nối vượt rãnh E-F từ cột 12 (nửa trên, bị IC 10..16 chắn) sang cột 14 (nửa dưới)
        let cross = connect_pair(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, 4, 12, 0, 14, 1, 1, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights,
        ).unwrap();
        assert!(cross.iter().any(|w| w.r1 == 4 && w.r2 == 5 && (w.c1 < 10 || w.c1 > 16)));
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/routing/h_route.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::{CostWeights, SpecialSpan};
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::base_net_of;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, is_rail_row, span_mask, track_index, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, WireSeg};
use crate::model::geometry::{seg_dist_sq, v_seg_free, vis_y, Footprint};
use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType, NetId};

/// Giá trị đánh dấu đoạn dây không chiếm rãnh ngang (dây cầu rãnh dọc hoặc dây nhảy nguồn)
pub const NO_TRACK: u8 = u8::MAX;

/// Đoạn dây 8-byte trên Stack (`Copy`, không cấp phát `String`) dùng trong vòng lặp định tuyến
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct FastWire {
    pub net: NetId,
    pub c1: u8,
    pub r1: i8,
    pub c2: u8,
    pub r2: i8,
    pub lane: i8,
    pub trk: u8,
}

impl FastWire {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new_h(net: NetId, c1: u8, c2: u8, row: i8, lane: i8, trk: usize) -> Self {
        Self { net, c1, r1: row, c2, r2: row, lane, trk: trk as u8 }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new_v(net: NetId, c1: u8, r1: i8, c2: u8, r2: i8) -> Self {
        Self { net, c1, r1, c2, r2, lane: 0, trk: NO_TRACK }
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn to_wire_seg(self, net_name: String) -> WireSeg {
        WireSeg {
            net: self.net,
            net_name,
            cells: [[self.c1 as i8, self.r1], [self.c2 as i8, self.r2]],
            lane: self.lane,
            trk: (self.trk != NO_TRACK).then_some(self.trk as usize),
        }
    }
}

/// Thông tin chân linh kiện rời (R, LED, C) phục vụ trượt/đổi chỗ nội cột tối đa 1 hàng (#5)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct MovableSpecPin {
    pub cid: CompId,
    pub comp_type: CompType,
    pub c1: u8,
    pub r1: i8,
    pub c2: u8,
    pub r2: i8,
    pub orig_r1: i8,
    pub orig_r2: i8,
    pub locked: bool,
}

/// Quản lý danh sách dây trong Pass hiện tại kèm bộ đệm hoàn tác `try_row_swap_h` & `try_slide_spec_pin_1row`
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct PassWires {
    pub wires: Vec<FastWire>,
    pub swap_enabled: bool,
    pub spec_pins: Vec<MovableSpecPin>,
    swap_log: [(u16, FastWire); 64],
    swap_len: usize,
    pin_swap_log: [(u16, i8, i8); 64],
    pin_swap_len: usize,
}

impl PassWires {
    pub fn new(swap_enabled: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            wires: Vec::with_capacity(96),
            swap_enabled,
            spec_pins: Vec::new(),
            swap_log: [(0, FastWire::default()); 64],
            swap_len: 0,
            pin_swap_log: [(0, 0, 0); 64],
            pin_swap_len: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_layout(swap_enabled: bool, layout: &BoardLayout) -> Self {
        let mut pw = Self::new(swap_enabled);
        pw.load_layout(layout);
        pw
    }

    pub fn load_layout(&mut self, layout: &BoardLayout) {
        self.spec_pins.clear();
        for s in layout.cs.iter().chain(&layout.leds).chain(&layout.rs) {
            self.spec_pins.push(MovableSpecPin {
                cid: s.cid,
                comp_type: s.comp_type,
                c1: s.c1,
                r1: s.r1,
                c2: s.c2,
                r2: s.r2,
                orig_r1: s.r1,
                orig_r2: s.r2,
                locked: s.locked,
            });
        }
        self.pin_swap_len = 0;
    }

    pub fn apply_pin_slides(&self, layout: &mut BoardLayout) {
        for s in layout.cs.iter_mut().chain(&mut layout.leds).chain(&mut layout.rs) {
            if let Some(mp) = self.spec_pins.iter().find(|p| p.cid == s.cid) {
                s.r1 = mp.r1;
                s.r2 = mp.r2;
            }
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn snapshot_swaps(&self) -> usize {
        ((self.pin_swap_len & 0xFFFF) << 16) | (self.swap_len & 0xFFFF)
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn record_swap(&mut self, idx: usize, new_wire: FastWire) {
        if self.swap_len < self.swap_log.len() {
            self.swap_log[self.swap_len] = (idx as u16, self.wires[idx]);
            self.swap_len += 1;
        }
        self.wires[idx] = new_wire;
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn record_pin_slide(&mut self, idx: usize, new_r1: i8, new_r2: i8) {
        let cur = self.spec_pins[idx];
        if self.pin_swap_len < self.pin_swap_log.len() {
            self.pin_swap_log[self.pin_swap_len] = (idx as u16, cur.r1, cur.r2);
            self.pin_swap_len += 1;
        }
        self.spec_pins[idx].r1 = new_r1;
        self.spec_pins[idx].r2 = new_r2;
    }

    #[inline]
    pub fn restore_swaps(&mut self, snap: usize) {
        let target_swap = snap & 0xFFFF;
        let target_pin = (snap >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
        while self.swap_len > target_swap {
            self.swap_len -= 1;
            let (idx, old_w) = self.swap_log[self.swap_len];
            self.wires[idx as usize] = old_w;
        }
        while self.pin_swap_len > target_pin {
            self.pin_swap_len -= 1;
            let (idx, old_r1, old_r2) = self.pin_swap_log[self.pin_swap_len];
            if let Some(sp) = self.spec_pins.get_mut(idx as usize) {
                sp.r1 = old_r1;
                sp.r2 = old_r2;
            }
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn commit_swaps(&mut self) {
        self.swap_len = 0;
        self.pin_swap_len = 0;
    }
}

/// Kiểm tra quy tắc cấm dây tới ngang (`lane = 0`) tại các lỗ bị hạn chế ngang của `BTN2` (#2, #6):
/// - Cấm `lane = 0` nếu hàng `row` nằm trong vùng bẻ chân của `BTN2` trên đoạn `[c1, c2]`
/// - Cho phép `lane = ±1` (dây đi ở làn khoảng cách và cắm dọc từ làn khoảng cách vào lỗ)
#[inline]
pub fn is_btn_lane_allowed(fps: &[Footprint], row: i8, lane: i8, c1: u8, c2: u8) -> bool {
    if lane != 0 {
        return true;
    }
    let (c_min, c_max) = (c1.min(c2) as f64, c1.max(c2) as f64);
    let rf = row as f64;
    for f in fps {
        if !f.is_sw && (f.r1 < 3.5 || f.r0 > 5.5) && rf > f.r0 && rf < f.r1 && c_max >= f.c0 && c_min <= f.c1 {
            return false;
        }
    }
    true
}

#[inline]
pub fn get_available_track_with_btn_rule(
    board: &BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    row: i8,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    pref_gap_dir: i8,
) -> Option<(i8, usize)> {
    let pref_lane = if row == 4 { -1 } else { 1 };
    let lanes = if pref_gap_dir != 0 {
        [pref_gap_dir, -pref_gap_dir, 0]
    } else {
        [0, pref_lane, -pref_lane]
    };
    let mask = span_mask(c1, c2);
    for lane in lanes {
        if !is_btn_lane_allowed(fps, row, lane, c1, c2) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(tid) = track_index(row, lane) {
            if (board.track_masks[tid] & mask) == 0 {
                return Some((lane, tid));
            }
        }
    }
    None
}

/// Kiểm tra cột `col` có tiềm năng làm cầu rãnh E-F (`isPotentialBridgeCol` trong main.html)
#[inline]
pub fn is_potential_bridge_col(
    board: &BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    col: u8,
    for_tag: u16,
    rem_cross: i32,
) -> bool {
    if rem_cross <= 0 {
        return false;
    }
    if board.is_occupied(col, 4) || board.is_occupied(col, 5) || !v_seg_free(fps, col, 4, 5) {
        return false;
    }
    let cu = col as usize;
    let (n0, n1) = (board.col_net[0][cu], board.col_net[1][cu]);
    let ok0 = n0 == 0 || (for_tag != 0 && n0 == for_tag);
    let ok1 = n1 == 0 || (for_tag != 0 && n1 == for_tag);
    ok0 && ok1
}

/// Độ nghẽn trung bình trên đoạn cột `[c1, c2]` (`spanCongestion` trong main.html)
#[inline]
pub fn span_congestion(col_hist: &[f32; 65], c1: u8, c2: u8) -> f64 {
    let (lo, hi) = (c1.min(c2), c1.max(c2));
    let mut s = 0.0_f64;
    for c in lo..=hi {
        s += col_hist[c as usize] as f64;
    }
    s / ((hi - lo + 1).max(1) as f64)
}

/// Đo áp lực làn ngang trên đoạn `[c_a, c_b]` của nửa `h` (`halfSpanLoad` trong main.html)
#[inline]
pub fn half_span_load(
    board: &BoardState,
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    h: u8,
    c_a: u8,
    c_b: u8,
) -> f64 {
    if c_a == c_b {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let (c_min, c_max) = (c_a.min(c_b), c_a.max(c_b));
    let rows = if h == 0 { [1i8, 2, 3, 0] } else { [8i8, 7, 6, 9] };
    let mut blocked_rows = 0usize;
    let mut hist_sum = 0.0_f64;
    for r in rows {
        hist_sum += row_hist[r as usize] as f64;
        if board.get_available_track_for_row(r, c_min, c_max).is_none() {
            blocked_rows += 1;
        }
    }
    let sat_pen = match blocked_rows {
        4.. => 18.0,
        3 => 8.5,
        _ => (blocked_rows as f64) * 1.8,
    };
    sat_pen + hist_sum * 0.45
}

/// Đi 1 đoạn dây ngang trực tiếp từ `c1` sang `c2` trên nửa mặt bo `h` (`routeH` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn route_h(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    tag: u16,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    h: u8,
    forbid_mask: u16,
    rem_cross: i32,
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<FastWire> {
    let w_net = base_net_of(tag);
    let (c_min, c_max) = (c1.min(c2), c1.max(c2));
    let all_rows = match (board.rev, h) {
        (true, 0) => [1i8, 2, 3, 0, 4],
        (true, _) => [8i8, 7, 6, 9, 5],
        (false, 0) => [3i8, 2, 1, 0, 4],
        (false, _) => [6i8, 7, 8, 9, 5],
    };

    let mut best_r: i8 = -1;
    let mut best_track: Option<(i8, usize)> = None;
    let mut best_cost = 1e9_f64;

    for (idx, &r) in all_rows.iter().enumerate() {
        if (forbid_mask & (1u16 << r)) != 0 || board.is_occupied(c1, r) || board.is_occupied(c2, r)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(track) = get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r, c_min, c_max, 0) else {
            continue;
        };
        let is_groove = r == 4 || r == 5;
        let bridge_destroy_pen = if rem_cross > 0
            && is_groove
            && (is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, c1, tag, rem_cross)
                || is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, c2, tag, rem_cross))
        {
            weights.route_bridge_destroy_pen
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let groove_base = if rem_cross > 0 {
            weights.route_groove_pen_cross
        } else {
            weights.route_groove_pen_done
        };
        let edge_pen = if r == 0 || r == 9 {
            weights.route_outer_row_pen
        } else if is_groove {
            groove_base + bridge_destroy_pen
        } else {
            (idx as f64) * 2.5
        };
        let lane_pen = if track.0 == 0 { 0.0 } else { weights.route_gap_lane_pen };
        let cost = edge_pen + lane_pen + (row_hist[r as usize] as f64).min(5.0);
        if cost < best_cost {
            best_cost = cost;
            best_r = r;
            best_track = Some(track);
        }
    }

    let (lane, tid) = best_track?;
    board.occupy(c1, best_r);
    board.occupy(c2, best_r);
    board.occupy_segment(tid, c_min, c_max);
    Some(FastWire::new_h(w_net, c1, c2, best_r, lane, tid))
}

/// Trượt chân linh kiện rời (R, LED, C không khóa) tại lỗ `(col, r_old)` sang hàng liền kề `r_new`
/// Quy tắc Cứng #5: Độ lệch tối đa 1 hàng liền kề (`|r_new - r_old| <= 1` và `|r_new - orig_r| <= 1`)!
pub fn try_slide_spec_pin_1row(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    col: u8,
    r_old: i8,
    h: u8,
    forbid_row: Option<i8>,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<i8> {
    let idx = pass_wires.spec_pins.iter().position(|s| {
        !s.locked && ((s.c1 == col && s.r1 == r_old) || (s.c2 == col && s.r2 == r_old))
    })?;
    let sp_item = pass_wires.spec_pins[idx];
    let is_pin1 = sp_item.c1 == col && sp_item.r1 == r_old;
    let (other_c, other_r) = if is_pin1 { (sp_item.c2, sp_item.r2) } else { (sp_item.c1, sp_item.r1) };
    let orig_r = if is_pin1 { sp_item.orig_r1 } else { sp_item.orig_r2 };
    let span_lim = SpecialSpan::for_type(sp_item.comp_type);

    let mut cands = [r_old - 1, r_old + 1];
    if (cands[1] == other_r) || (cands[0] == 4 || cands[0] == 5) {
        cands.swap(0, 1);
    }

    for r_new in cands {
        if is_rail_row(r_new)
            || half_of(r_new) != h
            || (r_new - r_old).abs() > 1
            || (r_new - orig_r).abs() > 1
            || forbid_row == Some(r_new)
            || board.is_occupied(col, r_new)
        {
            continue;
        }
        let (nc1, nr1, nc2, nr2) = if is_pin1 {
            (col, r_new, other_c, other_r)
        } else {
            (other_c, other_r, col, r_new)
        };
        let one_rail = is_rail_row(nr1) != is_rail_row(nr2);
        let dy = if one_rail {
            (vis_y(nr1) - vis_y(nr2)).abs()
        } else {
            (nr1 - nr2).abs() as f64
        };
        let d = ((nc1 as f64) - (nc2 as f64)).hypot(dy);
        if d < span_lim.min || d > span_lim.max {
            continue;
        }
        let (p1, p2) = ([nc1 as f64, vis_y(nr1)], [nc2 as f64, vis_y(nr2)]);
        let hits = pass_wires.spec_pins.iter().enumerate().any(|(j, o)| {
            j != idx
                && !o.locked
                && seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, [o.c1 as f64, vis_y(o.r1)], [o.c2 as f64, vis_y(o.r2)])
                    < weights.spec_body_clearance_sq
        });
        if !hits {
            board.unoccupy(col, r_old);
            board.occupy(col, r_new);
            pass_wires.record_pin_slide(idx, nr1, nr2);
            return Some(r_new);
        }
    }
    None
}

/// Di chuyển 1 dây chắn `victim`:
/// - Ưu tiên #1 (`allow_same_row = true`): Lách thân dây sang làn khoảng cách (`lane = ±1`) ngay trên cùng hàng `r_from` (tốn 0 lỗ cắm mới!) (#6)
/// - Ưu tiên #2: Dời sang hàng khác `r_alt` (hỗ trợ đổi chỗ 1 hàng với chân linh kiện rời theo Quy tắc Cứng #5)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn move_one_victim(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    v_idx: usize,
    r_from: i8,
    all_rows: &[i8; 5],
    allow_same_row: bool,
    forbid_hole: Option<(u8, i8)>,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> bool {
    let victim = pass_wires.wires[v_idx];
    if victim.trk == NO_TRACK {
        return false;
    }
    let (vc1, vc2) = (victim.c1, victim.c2);
    let (v_min, v_max) = (vc1.min(vc2), vc1.max(vc2));

    if allow_same_row {
        let gap_lanes = if r_from == 4 { [-1i8, 1, 0] } else { [1i8, -1, 0] };
        for alt_lane in gap_lanes {
            if alt_lane == victim.lane || !is_btn_lane_allowed(fps, r_from, alt_lane, v_min, v_max) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(alt_tid) = track_index(r_from, alt_lane) else {
                continue;
            };
            if alt_tid != (victim.trk as usize) && board.is_segment_free(alt_tid, v_min, v_max) {
                board.unoccupy_segment(victim.trk as usize, v_min, v_max);
                board.occupy_segment(alt_tid, v_min, v_max);
                pass_wires.record_swap(
                    v_idx,
                    FastWire::new_h(victim.net, vc1, vc2, r_from, alt_lane, alt_tid),
                );
                return true;
            }
        }
    }

    let h = half_of(r_from);
    for &r_alt in all_rows {
        if r_alt == r_from {
            continue;
        }
        let Some((alt_lane, alt_tid)) =
            get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r_alt, v_min, v_max, 0)
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let snap_b = *board;
        let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
        board.unoccupy_segment(victim.trk as usize, v_min, v_max);
        board.unoccupy(vc1, r_from);
        board.unoccupy(vc2, r_from);

        let f1 = if !board.is_occupied(vc1, r_alt) {
            true
        } else if (r_alt - r_from).abs() <= 1 {
            let f_row = forbid_hole.and_then(|(fc, fr)| (fc == vc1).then_some(fr));
            try_slide_spec_pin_1row(board, pass_wires, vc1, r_alt, h, f_row, weights).is_some()
        } else {
            false
        };
        let f2 = f1
            && if !board.is_occupied(vc2, r_alt) {
                true
            } else if (r_alt - r_from).abs() <= 1 {
                let f_row = forbid_hole.and_then(|(fc, fr)| (fc == vc2).then_some(fr));
                try_slide_spec_pin_1row(board, pass_wires, vc2, r_alt, h, f_row, weights).is_some()
            } else {
                false
            };

        if f2 {
            board.occupy(vc1, r_alt);
            board.occupy(vc2, r_alt);
            board.occupy_segment(alt_tid, v_min, v_max);
            pass_wires.record_swap(
                v_idx,
                FastWire::new_h(victim.net, vc1, vc2, r_alt, alt_lane, alt_tid),
            );
            return true;
        }
        *board = snap_b;
        pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
    }
    false
}

fn free_endpoint_hole(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    col: u8,
    r: i8,
    h: u8,
    all_rows: &[i8; 5],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<(Option<usize>, i8)> {
    if !board.is_occupied(col, r) {
        return Some((None, 0));
    }
    if let Some(r_new) = try_slide_spec_pin_1row(board, pass_wires, col, r, h, Some(r), weights) {
        return Some((None, (r_new - r).signum()));
    }
    if let Some(hv_idx) = pass_wires.wires.iter().position(|w| {
        w.trk != NO_TRACK && w.r1 == r && w.r2 == r && (w.c1 == col || w.c2 == col)
    }) {
        let old_trk = pass_wires.wires[hv_idx].trk as usize;
        if move_one_victim(board, pass_wires, fps, hv_idx, r, all_rows, false, Some((col, r)), weights)
            && !board.is_occupied(col, r)
        {
            return Some((Some(old_trk), 0));
        }
        return None;
    }
    for r_adj in [r - 1, r + 1] {
        if is_rail_row(r_adj) || half_of(r_adj) != h {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(w_idx) = pass_wires.wires.iter().position(|w| {
            w.trk != NO_TRACK && w.r1 == r_adj && w.r2 == r_adj && (w.c1 == col || w.c2 == col)
        }) else {
            continue;
        };
        let snap_b = *board;
        let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
        if move_one_victim(board, pass_wires, fps, w_idx, r_adj, all_rows, false, Some((col, r)), weights) {
            if let Some(r_new) = try_slide_spec_pin_1row(board, pass_wires, col, r, h, Some(r), weights) {
                return Some((None, (r_new - r).signum()));
            }
        }
        *board = snap_b;
        pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
    }
    None
}

/// Thử dời dây ngang cũ sang làn phụ / hàng khác hoặc trượt chân linh kiện rời 1 hàng để nhường chỗ cho `[c1, c2]`
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn try_row_swap_h_ext(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    tag: u16,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    h: u8,
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<FastWire> {
    if !pass_wires.swap_enabled || (pass_wires.wires.is_empty() && pass_wires.spec_pins.is_empty()) {
        return None;
    }
    let w_net = base_net_of(tag);
    let (c_min, c_max) = (c1.min(c2), c1.max(c2));
    let all_rows = if h == 0 { [1i8, 2, 3, 0, 4] } else { [8i8, 7, 6, 9, 5] };

    for &r in &all_rows {
        let (occ1, occ2) = (board.is_occupied(c1, r), board.is_occupied(c2, r));
        if !occ1 && !occ2 {
            let mut trk_order = [NO_TRACK; 3];
            let mut trk_cnt = 0usize;
            for w in &pass_wires.wires {
                if w.trk != NO_TRACK && w.r1 == r && w.r2 == r {
                    let (v_min, v_max) = (w.c1.min(w.c2), w.c1.max(w.c2));
                    if c_min.max(v_min) <= c_max.min(v_max) && !trk_order[..trk_cnt].contains(&w.trk)
                    {
                        if trk_cnt < 3 {
                            trk_order[trk_cnt] = w.trk;
                            trk_cnt += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            for &tk in &trk_order[..trk_cnt] {
                let mut v_group = [0usize; 4];
                let mut v_len = 0usize;
                for (idx, w) in pass_wires.wires.iter().enumerate() {
                    if w.trk == tk && w.r1 == r && w.r2 == r {
                        let (v_min, v_max) = (w.c1.min(w.c2), w.c1.max(w.c2));
                        if c_min.max(v_min) <= c_max.min(v_max) {
                            if v_len < 4 {
                                v_group[v_len] = idx;
                            }
                            v_len += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if v_len == 0 || v_len > 3 {
                    continue;
                }
                let snap_b = *board;
                let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
                let all_moved = v_group[..v_len].iter().all(|&vi| {
                    move_one_victim(board, pass_wires, fps, vi, r, &all_rows, true, None, weights)
                });
                if all_moved {
                    if let Some((lane, tid)) =
                        get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r, c_min, c_max, 0)
                    {
                        board.occupy(c1, r);
                        board.occupy(c2, r);
                        board.occupy_segment(tid, c_min, c_max);
                        return Some(FastWire::new_h(w_net, c1, c2, r, lane, tid));
                    }
                }
                *board = snap_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
            }
        } else {
            let snap_b = *board;
            let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
            let Some((old_t1, dir1)) =
                free_endpoint_hole(board, pass_wires, fps, c1, r, h, &all_rows, weights)
            else {
                *board = snap_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
                continue;
            };
            let Some((old_t2, dir2)) =
                free_endpoint_hole(board, pass_wires, fps, c2, r, h, &all_rows, weights)
            else {
                *board = snap_b;
                pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
                continue;
            };
            let pref_dir = if dir1 != 0 { dir1 } else { dir2 };
            if !board.is_occupied(c1, r) && !board.is_occupied(c2, r) {
                let mut new_track =
                    get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r, c_min, c_max, pref_dir);
                if new_track.is_none() {
                    let target_trk = old_t1.or(old_t2).map(|t| t as u8);
                    let mut blockers = [0usize; 3];
                    let mut b_len = 0usize;
                    for (idx, w) in pass_wires.wires.iter().enumerate() {
                        if w.trk != NO_TRACK
                            && w.r1 == r
                            && w.r2 == r
                            && target_trk.is_none_or(|tk| w.trk == tk)
                        {
                            let (v_min, v_max) = (w.c1.min(w.c2), w.c1.max(w.c2));
                            if c_min.max(v_min) <= c_max.min(v_max) {
                                if b_len < 3 {
                                    blockers[b_len] = idx;
                                }
                                b_len += 1;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if b_len > 0
                        && b_len <= 2
                        && blockers[..b_len].iter().all(|&bi| {
                            move_one_victim(board, pass_wires, fps, bi, r, &all_rows, true, None, weights)
                        })
                    {
                        new_track =
                            get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r, c_min, c_max, pref_dir);
                    }
                }
                if let Some((lane, tid)) = new_track {
                    board.occupy(c1, r);
                    board.occupy(c2, r);
                    board.occupy_segment(tid, c_min, c_max);
                    return Some(FastWire::new_h(w_net, c1, c2, r, lane, tid));
                }
            }
            *board = snap_b;
            pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
        }
    }
    None
}

/// Thử dời dây ngang cũ sang hàng khác để nhường chỗ cho đoạn `[c1, c2]` (`tryRowSwapH`)
pub fn try_row_swap_h(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    tag: u16,
    c1: u8,
    c2: u8,
    h: u8,
) -> Option<FastWire> {
    try_row_swap_h_ext(board, pass_wires, &[], tag, c1, c2, h, &CostWeights::DEFAULT)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::model::circuit::PlacedSpecial;

    #[test]
    fn test_route_h_and_try_row_swap_h() {
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let fps = Vec::new();
        let row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::new(true);

        let w1 = route_h(&mut board, &fps, 3, 5, 15, 0, 0, 0, &row_hist, &weights).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(w1.r1, 3);
        assert_eq!(w1.lane, 0);
        pass_wires.wires.push(w1);

        for r in [0, 1, 2, 4] {
            board.occupy(5, r);
        }
        assert!(route_h(&mut board, &fps, 4, 5, 12, 0, 0, 0, &row_hist, &weights).is_none());

        board.unoccupy(5, 1);
        board.occupy(12, 1);
        let w2 = try_row_swap_h(&mut board, &mut pass_wires, 4, 5, 12, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(w2.r1, 3);
        assert_eq!(pass_wires.wires[0].r1, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_same_row_gap_lane_shift_zero_new_holes_and_btn_rule() {
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::new(true);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        // 1. Dây victim w1 (10..15) đang nằm ở hàng 3, lane = 0
        let tid_r3_l0 = track_index(3, 0).unwrap();
        board.occupy(10, 3);
        board.occupy(15, 3);
        board.occupy_segment(tid_r3_l0, 10, 15);
        pass_wires.wires.push(FastWire::new_h(NetId(3), 10, 15, 3, 0, tid_r3_l0));

        // Khóa sạch các hàng 0, 1, 2, 4 tại cột 10, 15, 2, 12 -> victim KHÔNG THỂ dời sang hàng khác!
        for c in [2, 10, 12, 15] {
            for r in [0, 1, 2, 4] {
                board.occupy(c, r);
            }
        }
        // Chặn làn phụ (+1 và -1) của hàng 3 trên đoạn [2..4] để dây mới (2..12) bắt buộc cần lane = 0 của hàng 3
        board.occupy_segment(track_index(3, 1).unwrap(), 2, 4);
        board.occupy_segment(track_index(3, -1).unwrap(), 2, 4);

        // try_row_swap_h tự động lách thân w1 (10..15) sang làn khoảng cách (lane = +1) ngay trên hàng 3 (tốn 0 lỗ mới!)
        let w_new = try_row_swap_h(&mut board, &mut pass_wires, 4, 2, 12, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((w_new.r1, w_new.lane), (3, 0));
        assert_eq!((pass_wires.wires[0].r1, pass_wires.wires[0].lane), (3, 1));

        // 2. Kiểm tra quy tắc cấm lane = 0 tại lỗ bẻ chân BTN2 (TH3: bend = +1 tại B, 1..=3 -> cấm lane = 0 trên hàng C=2)
        let fps = vec![Footprint::for_btn_bent(1, 3, 1, 1)];
        assert!(!is_btn_lane_allowed(&fps, 2, 0, 3, 8));
        assert!(is_btn_lane_allowed(&fps, 2, 1, 3, 8));
        assert!(is_btn_lane_allowed(&fps, 0, 0, 3, 8));
        let _ = weights;
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_try_slide_spec_pin_1row_hard_limit() {
        let weights = CostWeights::default();
        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.rs.push(PlacedSpecial {
            cid: CompId(1),
            id: "R1".into(),
            comp_type: CompType::R,
            c1: 5,
            r1: 2,
            c2: 10,
            r2: 2,
            locked: false,
            ic_id: None,
        });
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut fps = Vec::new();
        layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::with_layout(true, &layout);

        // Cột 5 chỉ trống ở hàng 1 (|1 - 2| == 1), cột 12 chỉ trống ở hàng 2 -> R1 trượt 1 hàng từ (5,2) sang (5,1) để nhường (5,2) cho dây!
        for r in [0, 3, 4] {
            board.occupy(5, r);
        }
        for r in [0, 1, 3, 4] {
            board.occupy(12, r);
        }
        let w = try_row_swap_h_ext(&mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, 3, 5, 12, 0, &weights).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(w.r1, 2);
        pass_wires.apply_pin_slides(&mut layout);
        assert_eq!(layout.rs[0].r1, 1);

        // Quy tắc Cứng #5: Không bao giờ cho phép trượt tiếp từ hàng 1 sang hàng 0 vì |0 - orig_r1(2)| = 2 > 1!
        board.unoccupy(5, 0);
        assert!(try_slide_spec_pin_1row(&mut board, &mut pass_wires, 5, 1, 0, None, &weights).is_none());
    }
}

pub mod bridge_pair;
