use crate::config::personas::PersonaConfig;
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::{is_pwr_tied_tag, pwr_direct_tag, DiscreteContext};
use crate::macro_place::cost::pick_best_cross_pair;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, rail_ok, BoardState};
use crate::model::circuit::{BoardLayout, ParsedNetlist};
use crate::model::geometry::{v_seg_free, Footprint};
use crate::model::ids::{Mulberry32, NetId};
use crate::routing::h_route::{is_potential_bridge_col, FastWire, MovableSpecPin, PassWires};
use crate::routing::rail::{rail_connect, PwrSegTracker};
use crate::routing::steiner::{connect_steiner, strip_idx, StripUf};
use rustc_hash::{FxHashMap, FxHashSet};

/// Một nhiệm vụ nối dây đơn lẻ trong danh sách `items` của `runRoutingEngine`
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct RouteItem {
    pub net: NetId,
    pub tag: u16,
    pub rail: bool,
    pub cross: bool,
    pub a: (u8, i8),
    pub b: (u8, i8),
    pub star_hub: Option<(u8, u8)>,
    pub pri: f64,
}

/// Kết quả định tuyến của 1 Pass (`bestPass` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default)]
pub struct RoutingPassResult {
    pub wires: Vec<FastWire>,
    pub w_len: f64,
    pub failed: Vec<usize>,
    pub failed_items: Vec<RouteItem>,
    pub spec_pins: Vec<MovableSpecPin>,
    pub errs: Vec<String>,
    pub fail_span: i32,
    pub item_spans: Vec<Option<(u8, u8)>>,
}

impl RoutingPassResult {
    /// Đồng bộ tọa độ các chân linh kiện rời (R, LED, C) đã trượt 1 hàng trong Pass tốt nhất về `BoardLayout` (#5)
    pub fn apply_spec_pins(&self, layout: &mut BoardLayout) {
        if self.spec_pins.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        for s in layout.cs.iter_mut().chain(&mut layout.leds).chain(&mut layout.rs) {
            if let Some(mp) = self.spec_pins.iter().find(|p| p.cid == s.cid) {
                s.r1 = mp.r1;
                s.r2 = mp.r2;
            }
        }
    }
}

fn push_steiner_net_items(
    net_id: NetId,
    reps: &[(u8, i8)],
    boost_nets: &FxHashSet<NetId>,
    p_cfg: &PersonaConfig,
    layout: &BoardLayout,
    board: &BoardState,
    weights: &CostWeights,
    items: &mut Vec<RouteItem>,
) {
    let pre_boost = if boost_nets.contains(&net_id) { -420.0 } else { 0.0 };
    let mut top_r: Vec<(u8, i8)> = reps.iter().copied().filter(|h| h.1 <= 4).collect();
    let mut bot_r: Vec<(u8, i8)> = reps.iter().copied().filter(|h| h.1 >= 5).collect();
    top_r.sort_by_key(|h| h.0);
    bot_r.sort_by_key(|h| h.0);

    let fanout_bonus = if p_cfg.short_first && reps.len() >= 3 { 16.0 } else { 0.0 };
    for i in 1..top_r.len() {
        let d = (top_r[i].0 as i32 - top_r[i - 1].0 as i32).abs() as f64;
        let h_pri = if p_cfg.short_first { -95.0 + d * 0.75 - fanout_bonus } else { -90.0 - d };
        items.push(RouteItem {
            net: net_id,
            tag: net_id.0,
            rail: false,
            cross: false,
            a: top_r[i - 1],
            b: top_r[i],
            star_hub: None,
            pri: h_pri + pre_boost,
        });
    }
    for i in 1..bot_r.len() {
        let d = (bot_r[i].0 as i32 - bot_r[i - 1].0 as i32).abs() as f64;
        let h_pri = if p_cfg.short_first { -95.0 + d * 0.75 - fanout_bonus } else { -90.0 - d };
        items.push(RouteItem {
            net: net_id,
            tag: net_id.0,
            rail: false,
            cross: false,
            a: bot_r[i - 1],
            b: bot_r[i],
            star_hub: None,
            pri: h_pri + pre_boost,
        });
    }
    if !top_r.is_empty() && !bot_r.is_empty() {
        let top_arr: Vec<[i8; 2]> = top_r.iter().map(|&(c, r)| [c as i8, r]).collect();
        let bot_arr: Vec<[i8; 2]> = bot_r.iter().map(|&(c, r)| [c as i8, r]).collect();
        let (best_t, best_b, min_d) =
            pick_best_cross_pair(&top_arr, &bot_arr, &layout.ics, board, net_id.0, weights);
        items.push(RouteItem {
            net: net_id,
            tag: net_id.0,
            rail: false,
            cross: true,
            a: (best_t[0] as u8, best_t[1]),
            b: (best_b[0] as u8, best_b[1]),
            star_hub: None,
            pri: p_cfg.cross_pri - (min_d as f64) * 0.35 + pre_boost,
        });
    }
}

fn try_setup_star_hub(
    net_id: NetId,
    reps: &[(u8, i8)],
    board: &mut BoardState,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    items: &mut Vec<RouteItem>,
    extra_bridges: &mut Vec<((u8, u8), (u8, u8))>,
    star_bridge_wires: &mut Vec<FastWire>,
) -> bool {
    let mut top_p: Vec<(u8, i8)> = reps.iter().copied().filter(|h| h.1 <= 4).collect();
    let mut bot_p: Vec<(u8, i8)> = reps.iter().copied().filter(|h| h.1 >= 5).collect();
    top_p.sort_by_key(|h| h.0);
    bot_p.sort_by_key(|h| h.0);

    let mut used_hub_cols = [0u64; 2];
    let pick_hub_col = |board: &BoardState,
                        used: &[u64; 2],
                        h: u8,
                        target_c: f64,
                        need_free: u8,
                        need_bridge: bool,
                        need_bot_free: u8|
     -> Option<u8> {
        let (mut best_c, mut best_d) = (None, 1e9_f64);
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if (used[h as usize] & (1u64 << c)) != 0
                || board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize] != 0
                || board.free_hole_count(c, h) < need_free
            {
                continue;
            }
            if need_bridge
                && (board.col_net[(1 - h) as usize][c as usize] != 0
                    || board.free_hole_count(c, 1 - h) < need_bot_free
                    || board.is_occupied(c, 4)
                    || board.is_occupied(c, 5)
                    || !v_seg_free(fps, c, 4, 5))
            {
                continue;
            }
            let br_pen = if !need_bridge && is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, c, 0, 1) {
                14.0
            } else {
                0.0
            };
            let d = (c as f64 - target_c).abs() + br_pen;
            if d < best_d {
                best_d = d;
                best_c = Some(c);
            }
        }
        best_c
    };

    let build_half_hubs = |pts: &[(u8, i8)],
                           h: u8,
                           need_bridge_first: bool,
                           first_bot_free: u8,
                           used: &mut [u64; 2]|
     -> Option<Vec<(u8, u8, Vec<(u8, i8)>)>> {
        if pts.is_empty() {
            return Some(Vec::new());
        }
        let chunks: Vec<&[(u8, i8)]> = pts.chunks(3).collect();
        let mut hubs = Vec::with_capacity(chunks.len());
        for (idx, &ch) in chunks.iter().enumerate() {
            let avg: f64 = ch.iter().map(|p| p.0 as f64).sum::<f64>() / (ch.len() as f64);
            let need_free = 4.min(
                ch.len()
                    + usize::from(idx == 0 && need_bridge_first)
                    + usize::from(chunks.len() > 1),
            ) as u8;
            let hc = pick_hub_col(
                board,
                used,
                h,
                avg,
                need_free,
                idx == 0 && need_bridge_first,
                first_bot_free,
            )?;
            used[h as usize] |= 1u64 << hc;
            hubs.push((hc, h, ch.to_vec()));
        }
        Some(hubs)
    };

    let need_br = !top_p.is_empty() && !bot_p.is_empty();
    let first_bot_len = if need_br { bot_p.len().min(3) } else { 0 };
    let rest_bot_p = if need_br { &bot_p[first_bot_len..] } else { &bot_p[..] };
    let need_bot_free = if need_br {
        4.min(1 + first_bot_len + usize::from(!rest_bot_p.is_empty())) as u8
    } else {
        2
    };

    let Some(top_hubs) = build_half_hubs(&top_p, 0, need_br, need_bot_free, &mut used_hub_cols)
    else {
        return false;
    };
    let bot_hubs = if need_br && !top_hubs.is_empty() {
        let bc = top_hubs[0].0;
        used_hub_cols[1] |= 1u64 << bc;
        let Some(mut extra) = build_half_hubs(rest_bot_p, 1, false, 2, &mut used_hub_cols) else {
            return false;
        };
        let mut v = vec![(bc, 1u8, bot_p[..first_bot_len].to_vec())];
        v.append(&mut extra);
        v
    } else {
        let Some(v) = build_half_hubs(&bot_p, 1, false, 2, &mut used_hub_cols) else {
            return false;
        };
        v
    };

    if need_br && !top_hubs.is_empty() {
        let bc = top_hubs[0].0;
        board.col_net[0][bc as usize] = net_id.0;
        board.col_net[1][bc as usize] = net_id.0;
        board.occupy(bc, 4);
        board.occupy(bc, 5);
        extra_bridges.push(((bc, 0), (bc, 1)));
        star_bridge_wires.push(FastWire::new_v(net_id, bc, 4, bc, 5));
    }
    for (hc, hh, ch) in top_hubs.iter().chain(bot_hubs.iter()) {
        board.col_net[*hh as usize][*hc as usize] = net_id.0;
        let hub_row = if *hh != 0 { 7 } else { 2 };
        for &r_pt in ch {
            items.push(RouteItem {
                net: net_id,
                tag: net_id.0,
                rail: false,
                cross: false,
                a: (*hc, hub_row),
                b: r_pt,
                star_hub: Some((*hc, *hh)),
                pri: -150.0 - (r_pt.0 as f64 - *hc as f64).abs(),
            });
        }
    }
    for i in 1..top_hubs.len() {
        items.push(RouteItem {
            net: net_id,
            tag: net_id.0,
            rail: false,
            cross: false,
            a: (top_hubs[i - 1].0, 2),
            b: (top_hubs[i].0, 2),
            star_hub: Some((top_hubs[i - 1].0, 0)),
            pri: -160.0,
        });
    }
    for i in 1..bot_hubs.len() {
        items.push(RouteItem {
            net: net_id,
            tag: net_id.0,
            rail: false,
            cross: false,
            a: (bot_hubs[i - 1].0, 7),
            b: (bot_hubs[i].0, 7),
            star_hub: Some((bot_hubs[i - 1].0, 1)),
            pri: -160.0,
        });
    }
    true
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn execute_single_pass(
    order: &[usize],
    items: &[RouteItem],
    flip_rev: bool,
    cand_lim: usize,
    s0: &BoardState,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &BoardLayout,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
    extra_bridges: &[((u8, u8), (u8, u8))],
    star_bridge_wires: &[FastWire],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    p_cfg: &PersonaConfig,
    col_hist: &mut [f32; 65],
    row_hist: &mut [f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> RoutingPassResult {
    *board = *s0;
    if flip_rev {
        board.rev = !s0.rev;
    }
    for c in 1..=63 {
        col_hist[c] *= 0.92;
    }
    for r in 0..10 {
        row_hist[r] *= 0.92;
    }

    let mut uf = StripUf::new();
    for &(a, b) in ctx.btn_bridges.iter().chain(extra_bridges.iter()) {
        uf.union(strip_idx(a.0, a.1), strip_idx(b.0, b.1));
    }

    let mut pass_err = Vec::new();
    let mut pass_wires = PassWires::with_layout(p_cfg.swap, layout);
    pass_wires.wires.extend_from_slice(star_bridge_wires);
    let mut pwr_segs = PwrSegTracker::new();
    let mut failed = Vec::new();
    let mut failed_items = Vec::new();
    let mut item_spans = vec![None; items.len()];
    let mut fail_span = 0i32;
    let mut rem_cross = order.iter().filter(|&&idx| items[idx].cross).count() as i32;

    for &idx in order {
        let it = items[idx];
        let before = pass_err.len();
        let res = if it.rail {
            rail_connect(
                board, &mut pass_wires, &mut pwr_segs, fps, &mut uf,
                it.net, it.tag, &[it.a], rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights, &mut pass_err,
            )
        } else {
            match connect_steiner(
                board, &mut pass_wires, fps, &mut uf, it.tag, it.a, it.b,
                cand_lim, it.star_hub, rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights,
            ) {
                Some(w) => w,
                None => {
                    let net_name = parsed
                        .nets
                        .iter()
                        .find(|n| n.id == it.net)
                        .map_or("?", |n| n.name.as_str());
                    pass_err.push(format!(
                        "Net {net_name}: không đi được dây từ cột {} tới cột {}",
                        it.a.0, it.b.0
                    ));
                    Vec::new()
                }
            }
        };
        if it.cross {
            rem_cross -= 1;
        }
        if pass_err.len() > before {
            failed.push(idx);
            failed_items.push(it);
            let (ca, cb) = (it.a.0, if it.rail { it.a.0 } else { it.b.0 });
            let lo = ca.min(cb).saturating_sub(3).max(1);
            let hi = (ca.max(cb) + 3).min(63);
            let span = if !it.rail {
                (ca as i32 - cb as i32).abs()
                    + if half_of(it.a.1) != half_of(it.b.1) { 12 } else { 4 }
            } else {
                10
            };
            fail_span += span;
            for c in lo..=hi {
                col_hist[c as usize] += if c == ca || c == cb { 3.5 } else { 1.8 };
            }
            if (0..10).contains(&it.a.1) {
                row_hist[it.a.1 as usize] += 1.0;
            }
            if !it.rail && (0..10).contains(&it.b.1) {
                row_hist[it.b.1 as usize] += 1.0;
            }
        } else {
            if !res.is_empty() {
                let (mut min_c, mut max_c) = (63u8, 1u8);
                for seg in &res {
                    min_c = min_c.min(seg.c1).min(seg.c2);
                    max_c = max_c.max(seg.c1).max(seg.c2);
                }
                item_spans[idx] = Some((min_c, max_c));
            }
            pass_wires.wires.extend(res);
            pass_wires.commit_swaps();
        }
    }

    let w_len: f64 = pass_wires
        .wires
        .iter()
        .map(|w| (w.c2 as f64 - w.c1 as f64).hypot(w.r2 as f64 - w.r1 as f64))
        .sum();

    RoutingPassResult {
        wires: pass_wires.wires,
        w_len,
        failed,
        failed_items,
        spec_pins: pass_wires.spec_pins,
        errs: pass_err,
        fail_span,
        item_spans,
    }
}

/// Động cơ Đi dây Đa lượt có Phản hồi Lịch sử Nghẽn (`runRoutingEngine` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn run_routing_engine(
    pass_budget: usize,
    use_star_hub: bool,
    net_ord: usize,
    boost_nets: &FxHashSet<NetId>,
    p_cfg: &PersonaConfig,
    rng: &mut Option<Mulberry32>,
    board: &mut BoardState,
    layout: &BoardLayout,
    ctx: &DiscreteContext,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    parsed: &ParsedNetlist,
    col_hist: &mut [f32; 65],
    row_hist: &mut [f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> RoutingPassResult {
    let base_board = *board;
    let mut items = Vec::with_capacity(64);
    let mut items_no_star = Vec::with_capacity(64);
    let mut star_bridge_wires = Vec::new();
    let mut extra_bridges = Vec::new();
    let mut has_star_hub_active = false;

    for net in &parsed.nets {
        let Some(h_list) = ctx.holes.get(&net.id) else { continue };
        if net.id.is_power() {
            let mut seen_strip = [0u64; 2];
            for h in h_list {
                let half = half_of(h.row);
                let bit = 1u64 << h.col;
                if (seen_strip[half as usize] & bit) != 0 {
                    continue;
                }
                seen_strip[half as usize] |= bit;
                let strip_tag = board.col_net[half as usize][h.col as usize];
                let is_aux_ic_pwr = is_pwr_tied_tag(h.tag) || is_pwr_tied_tag(strip_tag);
                let t = if is_aux_ic_pwr {
                    if is_pwr_tied_tag(strip_tag) { strip_tag } else { h.tag }
                } else {
                    pwr_direct_tag(net.id, h.col, half)
                };
                let blocked =
                    board.is_occupied(h.col, if h.row <= 4 { 0 } else { 9 }) || !rail_ok(h.col);
                // Hạng mục #6: Chân nguồn phụ trên thân IC (như U2:4, U2:10) nếu bị chặn lỗ thẳng
                // thì hạ ưu tiên xuống sau dây vượt rãnh để không cướp cầu rãnh E-F quanh U2!
                let pri = if !blocked {
                    -155.0
                } else if is_aux_ic_pwr {
                    p_cfg.cross_pri + 18.0
                } else {
                    -185.0
                };
                let pwr_item = RouteItem {
                    net: net.id,
                    tag: t,
                    rail: true,
                    cross: false,
                    a: (h.col, h.row),
                    b: (h.col, h.row),
                    star_hub: None,
                    pri,
                };
                items.push(pwr_item);
                items_no_star.push(pwr_item);
            }
            continue;
        }

        let mut seen_strip = [0u64; 2];
        let mut reps = Vec::with_capacity(h_list.len());
        for h in h_list {
            let half = half_of(h.row);
            let bit = 1u64 << h.col;
            if (seen_strip[half as usize] & bit) == 0 {
                seen_strip[half as usize] |= bit;
                reps.push((h.col, h.row));
            }
        }
        if reps.len() < 2 {
            continue;
        }

        push_steiner_net_items(
            net.id, &reps, boost_nets, p_cfg, layout, &base_board, weights, &mut items_no_star,
        );

        if use_star_hub
            && net.is_star
            && try_setup_star_hub(
                net.id,
                &reps,
                board,
                fps,
                &mut items,
                &mut extra_bridges,
                &mut star_bridge_wires,
            )
        {
            has_star_hub_active = true;
            continue;
        }

        push_steiner_net_items(
            net.id, &reps, boost_nets, p_cfg, layout, board, weights, &mut items,
        );
    }

    let mut s0 = *board;
    let sort_by = |items_ref: &[RouteItem], rng_opt: &mut Option<Mulberry32>, jit: f64| -> Vec<usize> {
        let mut keyed: Vec<(f64, usize)> = items_ref
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, it)| {
                let noise = rng_opt.as_mut().map_or(0.0, |r| r.next_f64() * jit);
                (it.pri + noise, i)
            })
            .collect();
        keyed.sort_by(|x, y| x.0.total_cmp(&y.0));
        keyed.into_iter().map(|x| x.1).collect()
    };

    let mut order = sort_by(&items, rng, (net_ord as f64) * 4.0);
    let mut best_pass: Option<RoutingPassResult> = None;
    let mut no_imp = 0usize;
    let mut polish_left = 2i32;
    let half_budget = (pass_budget / 2).max(2);

    for ps in 0..pass_budget.max(1) {
        // Hạng mục #9: Nếu ở nửa sau Pass (ps >= pass_budget / 2) vẫn còn lỗi vượt rãnh/thiếu cột trống,
        // cho phép mạng *CLK/*RESET_n hạ cấp sang nối chuỗi Steiner trực tiếp để trả lại 2–4 cột cầu rãnh!
        if has_star_hub_active
            && ps >= half_budget
            && best_pass.as_ref().is_some_and(|bp| !bp.errs.is_empty())
        {
            has_star_hub_active = false;
            let mut net_min_pri: FxHashMap<NetId, f64> = FxHashMap::default();
            for it in &items {
                let e = net_min_pri.entry(it.net).or_insert(it.pri);
                if it.pri < *e {
                    *e = it.pri;
                }
            }
            for it in &mut items_no_star {
                if let Some(&mp) = net_min_pri.get(&it.net) {
                    if mp < -200.0 {
                        it.pri = mp;
                    }
                }
            }
            items = items_no_star.clone();
            extra_bridges.clear();
            star_bridge_wires.clear();
            s0 = base_board;
            order = sort_by(&items, rng, 6.0 + (ps as f64) * 2.5);
            no_imp = 0;
        }

        let is_near_solved = best_pass
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|bp| !bp.errs.is_empty() && bp.errs.len() <= 2);
        let flip = ps > 0
            && (ps % 3 == 2
                || (best_pass.as_ref().is_some_and(|bp| bp.errs.is_empty()) && ps % 2 == 1));
        let cand_lim = if is_near_solved {
            if p_cfg.id == 4 { 56 } else { 48 }
        } else if ps == 0 {
            20
        } else {
            26
        };

        let res = execute_single_pass(
            &order, &items, flip, cand_lim, &s0, board, layout, fps, ctx,
            &extra_bridges, &star_bridge_wires, parsed, p_cfg, col_hist, row_hist, weights,
        );

        let is_better = match &best_pass {
            None => true,
            Some(bp) => {
                res.errs.len() < bp.errs.len()
                    || (res.errs.len() == bp.errs.len()
                        && !res.errs.is_empty()
                        && res.fail_span < bp.fail_span)
                    || (res.errs.is_empty()
                        && bp.errs.is_empty()
                        && (res.w_len + (res.wires.len() as f64) * 3.5)
                            < (bp.w_len + (bp.wires.len() as f64) * 3.5))
            }
        };
        if is_better {
            best_pass = Some(res.clone());
            no_imp = 0;
        } else {
            no_imp += 1;
        }

        let bp = best_pass.as_ref().unwrap();
        if bp.errs.is_empty() {
            if polish_left <= 0 {
                break;
            }
            polish_left -= 1;
            order = sort_by(&items, rng, 5.0 + (ps as f64) * 2.0);
            continue;
        }
        // Hạng mục #9: Nới phễu cắt tỉa sớm Pass 0–6 cho mạch 6 IC
        let errs = bp.errs.len();
        if (ps == 0 && errs > 14)
            || (ps >= 3 && errs > 7)
            || (ps >= 6 && errs > 4)
            || no_imp >= (if is_near_solved { 14 } else { 7 })
        {
            break;
        }

        let mut fail_cols_mask = 0u64;
        let mut fail_ranges = Vec::with_capacity(res.failed.len());
        let mut tied_tags_boost: FxHashMap<u16, f64> = FxHashMap::default();

        for &f_idx in &res.failed {
            let it = &mut items[f_idx];
            let (c1, c2) = (it.a.0, if it.rail { it.a.0 } else { it.b.0 });
            fail_cols_mask |= (1u64 << c1) | (1u64 << c2);
            fail_ranges.push((c1.min(c2).saturating_sub(1), c1.max(c2).saturating_add(1)));
            it.pri = it.pri.min(-195.0) - weights.route_failed_boost_step * ((ps + 1) as f64);
            if it.rail && is_pwr_tied_tag(it.tag) {
                tied_tags_boost.insert(it.tag, it.pri);
            }
        }

        for (idx, it) in items.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if !it.rail {
                if let Some(&boost_p) = tied_tags_boost.get(&it.tag) {
                    let d = (it.b.0 as i32 - it.a.0 as i32).abs() as f64;
                    it.pri = it.pri.min(boost_p - 30.0 - d);
                    continue;
                }
            }
            if res.failed.contains(&idx) {
                continue;
            }
            if (fail_cols_mask & (1u64 << it.a.0)) != 0
                || (!it.rail && (fail_cols_mask & (1u64 << it.b.0)) != 0)
            {
                it.pri += 42.0;
                continue;
            }
            if let Some((s_min, s_max)) = res.item_spans[idx] {
                if fail_ranges.iter().any(|&(f1, f2)| s_min.max(f1) <= s_max.min(f2)) {
                    it.pri += 24.0;
                }
            }
        }
        order = sort_by(&items, rng, 7.0 + (ps as f64) * 3.5);
    }

    board.rev = base_board.rev;
    best_pass.unwrap_or_default()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::config::personas::get_persona;
    use crate::discrete_place::{place_special_components, setup_macro_and_decoupling, IcSnapshot, SpecialOptions};
    use crate::macro_place::topology::build_topology_cache;
    use crate::model::circuit::{CompSpec, PlacedIc};
    use crate::model::ids::{CompId, CompType};
    use crate::parser::parse_netlist;

    #[test]
    fn test_run_routing_engine_routes_full_circuit_zero_errors() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(1), "U2", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(2), "CC1", CompType::CC),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(3), "R1", CompType::R),
            CompSpec::new_other(CompId(4), "L1", CompType::LED),
        ];
        let txt = "
            VCC U1:14 U2:14; GND U1:7 U2:7
            VCC -> CC1 -> GND
            *CLK: U1:1 -> U2:2 -> U2:12
            U1:3 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 10, c_max: 16, w: 7 });
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(1), id: "U2".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 20, c_max: 26, w: 7 });

        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut fps = Vec::new();
        layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);

        let mut errors = Vec::new();
        let mut unplaced = 0usize;
        let mut ctx = setup_macro_and_decoupling(&mut board, &mut layout, &parsed, &topo, &comps, &weights, &mut errors);
        let snap_ic = IcSnapshot::capture(&board, &ctx, unplaced, errors.len());

        let mut col_hist = [0.0f32; 65];
        let mut row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let spec_opts = SpecialOptions { persona: 2, sr: 1, ..Default::default() };

        place_special_components(
            false, 0, &spec_opts, &snap_ic, &mut board, &mut layout, &mut ctx,
            &fps, &comps, &comps, &parsed, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights, &mut unplaced, &mut errors,
        );

        let mut rng = Some(Mulberry32::new(1));
        let res = run_routing_engine(
            10, true, 0, &FxHashSet::default(), &get_persona(2), &mut rng,
            &mut board, &layout, &ctx, &fps, &parsed, &mut col_hist, &mut row_hist, &weights,
        );
        assert!(res.errs.is_empty(), "Routing errors: {:?}", res.errs);
        assert!(!res.wires.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_star_hub_downgrade_and_aux_ic_pwr_priority() {
        let comps = vec![
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(0), "U1", 14, 0),
            CompSpec::new_ic(CompId(1), "U2", 14, 0),
        ];
        // U2:4 nối GND (chân nguồn phụ trên thân IC tại cột 21, rơi vào cột nghỉ hoặc bị chặn hàng 9)
        // *CLK có 4 chân nửa trên + 1 chân nửa dưới (Star-Hub sẽ chiếm 2 cột trống làm Hub),
        // khi chỉ còn 2 cột cầu rãnh trống trên toàn bo thì nửa sau Pass sẽ tự động hạ cấp *CLK sang Steiner để nhường cầu rãnh cho SIG1!
        let txt = "
            VCC U1:14 U2:14; GND U1:7 U2:7 U2:4
            *CLK: U1:14 -> U1:13 -> U1:12 -> U1:11 -> U2:1
            SIG1: U1:10 -> U2:2
        ";
        let parsed = parse_netlist(txt, &comps);
        let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        let mut layout = BoardLayout::default();
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(0), id: "U1".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 10, c_max: 16, w: 7 });
        layout.ics.push(PlacedIc { cid: CompId(1), id: "U2".into(), pins: 14, rot: 0, c_min: 18, c_max: 24, w: 7 });

        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut fps = Vec::new();
        layout.block_space(&mut board, &mut fps);
        let mut errors = Vec::new();
        let ctx = setup_macro_and_decoupling(&mut board, &mut layout, &parsed, &topo, &comps, &weights, &mut errors);

        // Khóa các cột trống khác trên bo, chỉ chừa đúng 2 cột trống (cột 9 và cột 17) làm cầu rãnh E-F
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if c < 9 || c > 24 {
                board.col_net[0][c as usize] = 99;
                board.col_net[1][c as usize] = 99;
            }
        }
        // Chặn lỗ thẳng hàng 9 tại cột 21 (chân phụ U2:4) để kiểm tra phân tầng ưu tiên #6
        board.occupy(21, 9);

        let mut col_hist = [0.0f32; 65];
        let mut row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let mut rng = Some(Mulberry32::new(7));
        let res = run_routing_engine(
            8, true, 0, &FxHashSet::default(), &get_persona(2), &mut rng,
            &mut board, &layout, &ctx, &fps, &parsed, &mut col_hist, &mut row_hist, &weights,
        );
        assert!(res.errs.is_empty(), "Star-Hub downgrade should rescue routing to 0 errors: {:?}", res.errs);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/routing/rail.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, rail_ok, BoardState};
use crate::model::geometry::{seg_dist_sq, seg_hits_fp, vis_y, Footprint};
use crate::model::ids::NetId;
use crate::routing::bridge_pair::route_same_half;
use crate::routing::h_route::{is_potential_bridge_col, FastWire, PassWires};
use crate::routing::steiner::{strip_idx, StripUf};

/// Bộ theo dõi các đoạn dây nguồn đã đặt trên Stack (Thay thế `R.pwrSegs` trong main.html)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct PwrSegTracker {
    segs: [(u8, i8, u8, i8); 64],
    pub len: usize,
}

impl Default for PwrSegTracker {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl PwrSegTracker {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new() -> Self {
        Self { segs: [(0, 0, 0, 0); 64], len: 0 }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn push(&mut self, c1: u8, r1: i8, c2: u8, r2: i8) {
        if self.len < self.segs.len() {
            self.segs[self.len] = (c1, r1, c2, r2);
            self.len += 1;
        }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[(u8, i8, u8, i8)] {
        &self.segs[..self.len]
    }
}

#[inline]
fn can_place_pwr_wire(
    board: &BoardState,
    pwr_segs: &PwrSegTracker,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    cd: u8,
    r_strip: i8,
    c: u8,
    up: bool,
    rr: i8,
) -> bool {
    if board.is_occupied(cd, r_strip) || board.is_occupied(c, rr) {
        return false;
    }
    if seg_hits_fp(fps, cd as f64, r_strip as f64, c as f64, rr as f64) {
        return false;
    }
    if cd == c {
        if rr == -2 && board.is_occupied(c, -1) {
            return false;
        }
        if rr == 11 && board.is_occupied(c, 10) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    let p1 = [cd as f64, vis_y(r_strip)];
    let p2 = [c as f64, vis_y(rr)];
    let def_y1 = vis_y(if up { -1 } else { 10 });
    let def_y2 = vis_y(if up { -2 } else { 11 });
    if seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, [31.0, def_y1], [33.0, def_y1]) < 0.20
        || seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, [31.0, def_y2], [33.0, def_y2]) < 0.20
    {
        return false;
    }
    for &(s0, s1, s2, s3) in pwr_segs.as_slice() {
        if seg_dist_sq(p1, p2, [s0 as f64, vis_y(s1)], [s2 as f64, vis_y(s3)]) < 0.12 {
            return false;
        }
    }
    true
}

/// Định tuyến Hình Sao trực tiếp từ dải cột chân nguồn lên thanh nguồn VCC/GND (`railConnect`)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn rail_connect(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    pwr_segs: &mut PwrSegTracker,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    uf: &mut StripUf,
    net: NetId,
    tag: u16,
    group: &[(u8, i8)],
    rem_cross: i32,
    col_hist: &[f32; 65],
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
    pass_err: &mut Vec<String>,
) -> Vec<FastWire> {
    let rail_root = uf.alloc_virtual_root();
    let mut out_wires = Vec::new();

    let mut comp_groups: Vec<(u8, Vec<(u8, i8)>)> = Vec::new();
    for &pt in group {
        let root = uf.find(strip_idx(pt.0, half_of(pt.1)));
        if let Some(g) = comp_groups.iter_mut().find(|(r, _)| *r == root) {
            g.1.push(pt);
        } else {
            comp_groups.push((root, vec![pt]));
        }
    }

    let mut rcols = [0u8; 50];
    let mut rcols_len = 0usize;
    for c in 3u8..=61 {
        if rail_ok(c) {
            rcols[rcols_len] = c;
            rcols_len += 1;
        }
    }
    let rcols = &rcols[..rcols_len];

    for (root, pts) in comp_groups {
        if uf.find(root) == uf.find(rail_root) {
            continue;
        }
        let up = pts[0].1 <= 4;
        let h = if up { 0u8 } else { 1u8 };
        let is_vcc = net == NetId::VCC;
        let rr = if up {
            if is_vcc { -1i8 } else { -2i8 }
        } else if is_vcc {
            11i8
        } else {
            10i8
        };
        let strip_rows = if up { [0i8, 1, 2, 3] } else { [9i8, 8, 7, 6] };
        let mut own_cols = Vec::with_capacity(pts.len());
        let mut own_mask = 0u64;
        for &(c, _) in &pts {
            own_cols.push(c);
            own_mask |= 1u64 << c;
        }

        let mut try_cand_list =
            |board: &mut BoardState, col_list: &[u8], max_delta_c: i32| -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
                let mut pairs = Vec::with_capacity(col_list.len() * 12);
                for &cd in col_list {
                    let is_own = (own_mask & (1u64 << cd)) != 0;
                    if !is_own && board.col_net[h as usize][cd as usize] != 0 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let step_dist = if is_own {
                        0
                    } else {
                        own_cols
                            .iter()
                            .map(|&oc| (cd as i32 - oc as i32).abs())
                            .min()
                            .unwrap_or(0)
                    };
                    for (r_idx, &r_strip) in strip_rows.iter().enumerate() {
                        if !is_own && r_idx > 1 {
                            continue;
                        }
                        if board.is_occupied(cd, r_strip) {
                            continue;
                        }
                        for &c in rcols {
                            let dc = (c as i32 - cd as i32).abs();
                            if dc > max_delta_c {
                                continue;
                            }
                            let pwr_len = (dc as f64).hypot((vis_y(rr) - vis_y(r_strip)).abs());
                            let br_pen = if is_own {
                                0.0
                            } else if is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, cd, 0, rem_cross) {
                                24.0
                            } else {
                                4.0
                            };
                            let cost = pwr_len
                                + (r_idx as f64) * 1.2
                                + (step_dist as f64) * 2.5
                                + br_pen;
                            pairs.push((cost, cd, r_strip, c));
                        }
                    }
                }
                pairs.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.total_cmp(&b.0));

                let mut failed_cd_mask = 0u64;
                for &(_, cd, r_strip, c) in &pairs {
                    if (failed_cd_mask & (1u64 << cd)) != 0
                        || !can_place_pwr_wire(board, pwr_segs, fps, cd, r_strip, c, up, rr)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                    let snap_b = *board;
                    let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
                    board.occupy(cd, r_strip);
                    let is_own = (own_mask & (1u64 << cd)) != 0;
                    let mut pre = Vec::new();
                    if !is_own {
                        let src_c = *own_cols
                            .iter()
                            .min_by_key(|&&oc| (oc as i32 - cd as i32).abs())
                            .unwrap();
                        let Some(routed) = route_same_half(
                            board, pass_wires, fps, tag, src_c, cd, h, true,
                            rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights,
                        ) else {
                            failed_cd_mask |= 1u64 << cd;
                            *board = snap_b;
                            pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
                            continue;
                        };
                        if !can_place_pwr_wire(board, pwr_segs, fps, cd, r_strip, c, up, rr) {
                            *board = snap_b;
                            pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
                            continue;
                        }
                        board.col_net[h as usize][cd as usize] = tag;
                        pre = routed;
                    }
                    board.occupy(c, rr);
                    if cd == c {
                        if rr == -2 {
                            board.occupy(c, -1);
                        }
                        if rr == 11 {
                            board.occupy(c, 10);
                        }
                    }
                    pwr_segs.push(cd, r_strip, c, rr);
                    uf.union(root, rail_root);
                    if !is_own {
                        uf.union(strip_idx(cd, h), rail_root);
                    }
                    pre.push(FastWire::new_v(net, cd, r_strip, c, rr));
                    return Some(pre);
                }
                None
            };

        let mut res = try_cand_list(board, &own_cols, 3);
        if res.is_none() {
            let mut empty_cols = Vec::with_capacity(32);
            for c in 1u8..=63 {
                if (own_mask & (1u64 << c)) == 0 && board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize] == 0 {
                    empty_cols.push(c);
                }
            }
            empty_cols.sort_by_key(|&c| {
                let d = own_cols.iter().map(|&oc| (c as i32 - oc as i32).abs()).min().unwrap_or(0);
                let br = if is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, c, 0, rem_cross) { 18 } else { 0 };
                d + br
            });
            let slice_len = empty_cols.len().min(12);
            res = try_cand_list(board, &empty_cols[..slice_len], 2);
        }
        match res {
            Some(w) => out_wires.extend(w),
            None => {
                let net_str = if net == NetId::VCC { "VCC" } else { "GND" };
                pass_err.push(format!(
                    "{net_str}: không kéo được dây nguồn từ cột {} mà không bị cắt đè",
                    own_cols[0]
                ));
            }
        }
    }
    out_wires
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::routing::steiner::connect_steiner;

    #[test]
    fn test_connect_steiner_and_rail_connect() {
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        board.occupy_default_power_holes();
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::new(true);
        let mut pwr_segs = PwrSegTracker::new();
        let mut uf = StripUf::new();
        let fps = Vec::new();
        let col_hist = [0.0f32; 65];
        let row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        // 1. Kiểm tra connect_steiner cho 3 chân cùng mạng tag=3 trên 2 nửa bo
        board.col_net[0][10] = 3;
        board.col_net[0][18] = 3;
        board.col_net[1][15] = 3;
        let w1 = connect_steiner(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, &mut uf, 3, (10, 4), (18, 4),
            18, None, 1, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights,
        ).unwrap();
        assert!(!w1.is_empty());
        let w2 = connect_steiner(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, &mut uf, 3, (10, 4), (15, 5),
            18, None, 1, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights,
        ).unwrap();
        assert!(!w2.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(uf.find(strip_idx(18, 0)), uf.find(strip_idx(15, 1)));

        // 2. Kiểm tra rail_connect nhảy thẳng từ cột 10 (nửa trên) lên thanh VCC (hàng -1)
        board.col_net[0][10] = 1;
        let mut pass_err = Vec::new();
        let pw = rail_connect(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &mut pwr_segs, &fps, &mut uf,
            NetId::VCC, 1, &[(10, 4)], 0, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights, &mut pass_err,
        );
        assert!(pass_err.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(pw.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pw[0].r2, -1);
    }
}

=== FILE: crates/bse-core/src/routing/steiner.rs ===
use crate::config::weights::CostWeights;
use crate::discrete_place::decoupling::{base_net_of, is_pwr_tag};
use crate::model::bitboard::{half_of, BoardState};
use crate::model::geometry::{v_seg_free, Footprint};
use crate::routing::bridge_pair::connect_pair;
use crate::routing::h_route::{
    get_available_track_with_btn_rule, is_potential_bridge_col, route_h, span_congestion,
    try_row_swap_h_ext, FastWire, PassWires,
};

/// Ánh xạ dải 5 lỗ `(col, half)` sang chỉ số phẳng `0..128` trên Stack
#[inline(always)]
pub const fn strip_idx(col: u8, half: u8) -> u8 {
    ((half & 1) << 6) | (col & 63)
}

/// Cấu trúc Union-Find siêu tốc trên Stack (258 bytes, `Copy`, không cấp phát Heap)
/// - `0..128`: Các dải 5 lỗ `(col, half)` trên mặt Breadboard
/// - `128..255`: Các nút gốc ảo cho từng nhánh nguồn `RAIL_${tag}`
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct StripUf {
    parent: [u8; 256],
    next_virtual: u8,
}

impl Default for StripUf {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl StripUf {
    #[inline(always)]
    pub const fn new() -> Self {
        let mut parent = [0u8; 256];
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < 256 {
            parent[i] = i as u8;
            i += 1;
        }
        Self { parent, next_virtual: 128 }
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn alloc_virtual_root(&mut self) -> u8 {
        let r = self.next_virtual;
        self.next_virtual = self.next_virtual.saturating_add(1);
        r
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn find(&mut self, mut x: u8) -> u8 {
        while self.parent[x as usize] != x {
            let p = self.parent[x as usize];
            self.parent[x as usize] = self.parent[p as usize];
            x = p;
        }
        x
    }

    #[inline(always)]
    pub fn union(&mut self, x: u8, y: u8) {
        let rx = self.find(x);
        let ry = self.find(y);
        self.parent[rx as usize] = ry;
    }
}

/// Dò đường mê cung BFS 2D trên 126 dải đồng (63 cột x 2 nửa bo) làm phao cứu sinh cuối cùng cho 1–2 dây kẹt (#9)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn bfs_escape_route(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    uf: &mut StripUf,
    tag: u16,
    a: (u8, i8),
    b: (u8, i8),
    rem_cross: i32,
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
    let ka = strip_idx(a.0, half_of(a.1));
    let kb = strip_idx(b.0, half_of(b.1));
    let (ra, rb) = (uf.find(ka), uf.find(kb));
    if ra == rb {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    let (ha, hb) = (half_of(a.1), half_of(b.1));
    let is_cross_branch = ha != hb;
    let is_pwr = is_pwr_tag(tag) && tag >= 0x4000;
    let w_net = base_net_of(tag);

    let mut in_start = [false; 128];
    let mut in_target = [false; 128];
    let (mut has_start, mut has_target) = (false, false);

    for h in [0u8, 1u8] {
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize] != tag || !board.has_free_hole(c, h) {
                continue;
            }
            let k = strip_idx(c, h);
            let rk = uf.find(k);
            if rk == ra || (is_cross_branch && h == ha) {
                in_start[k as usize] = true;
                has_start = true;
            } else if rk == rb || (is_cross_branch && h == hb) {
                in_target[k as usize] = true;
                has_target = true;
            }
        }
    }
    if !has_start || !has_target {
        return None;
    }

    let mut forbidden_mid = [0u64; 2];
    for _attempt in 0..4 {
        let mut dist = [u8::MAX; 128];
        let mut prev = [0u8; 128];
        let mut in_row = [-1i8; 128];
        let mut queue = [0u8; 128];
        let (mut q_head, mut q_tail) = (0usize, 0usize);

        for k in 1u8..128 {
            if in_start[k as usize] {
                dist[k as usize] = 0;
                queue[q_tail] = k;
                q_tail += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut found_end = None;
        while q_head < q_tail {
            let u = queue[q_head];
            q_head += 1;
            if in_target[u as usize] {
                found_end = Some(u);
                break;
            }
            let d = dist[u as usize];
            if d >= 4 {
                continue;
            }
            let uc = u & 63;
            let uh = (u >> 6) & 1;
            let u_in_r = in_row[u as usize];

            // 1. Nhảy dọc qua cầu rãnh E-F tại cùng cột uc
            let vh = 1 - uh;
            let v_br = strip_idx(uc, vh);
            let (r_u, r_v) = if uh == 0 { (4i8, 5i8) } else { (5i8, 4i8) };
            if dist[v_br as usize] == u8::MAX
                && u_in_r != r_u
                && !board.is_occupied(uc, 4)
                && !board.is_occupied(uc, 5)
                && v_seg_free(fps, uc, 4, 5)
            {
                let cur_v = board.col_net[vh as usize][uc as usize];
                let v_is_tgt = in_target[v_br as usize];
                let v_ok = if v_is_tgt {
                    true
                } else if (forbidden_mid[vh as usize] & (1u64 << uc)) != 0 {
                    false
                } else if is_pwr {
                    cur_v == 0 && board.free_hole_count(uc, vh) >= 2
                } else {
                    (cur_v == 0 || cur_v == tag) && board.free_hole_count(uc, vh) >= 2
                };
                if v_ok && q_tail < 128 {
                    dist[v_br as usize] = d + 1;
                    prev[v_br as usize] = u;
                    in_row[v_br as usize] = r_v;
                    queue[q_tail] = v_br;
                    q_tail += 1;
                }
            }

            // 2. Nhảy ngang trên cùng nửa bo uh (ưu tiên khoảng cách gần & né cột cầu rãnh khi rem_cross > 0)
            let rows = if uh == 0 { [3i8, 2, 1, 0, 4] } else { [6i8, 7, 8, 9, 5] };
            let mut cands = [(0u16, 0u8, 0i8); 64];
            let mut c_len = 0usize;
            for vc in 1u8..=63 {
                if vc == uc {
                    continue;
                }
                let v = strip_idx(vc, uh);
                if dist[v as usize] != u8::MAX {
                    continue;
                }
                let v_is_tgt = in_target[v as usize];
                let cur_v = board.col_net[uh as usize][vc as usize];
                if !v_is_tgt {
                    if (forbidden_mid[uh as usize] & (1u64 << vc)) != 0 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if is_pwr {
                        if cur_v != 0 || board.free_hole_count(vc, uh) < 2 {
                            continue;
                        }
                    } else if (cur_v != 0 && cur_v != tag) || board.free_hole_count(vc, uh) < 2 {
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                let (c_min, c_max) = (uc.min(vc), uc.max(vc));
                let Some(&r_hit) = rows.iter().find(|&&r| {
                    r != u_in_r
                        && !board.is_occupied(uc, r)
                        && !board.is_occupied(vc, r)
                        && get_available_track_with_btn_rule(board, fps, r, c_min, c_max, 0).is_some()
                }) else {
                    continue;
                };
                let br_pen = if !v_is_tgt && is_potential_bridge_col(board, fps, vc, tag, rem_cross) {
                    20u16
                } else {
                    0u16
                };
                let pri = (uc.abs_diff(vc) as u16) + br_pen;
                cands[c_len] = (pri, v, r_hit);
                c_len += 1;
            }
            cands[..c_len].sort_unstable_by_key(|x| x.0);
            for &(_, v, r_hit) in &cands[..c_len] {
                if dist[v as usize] == u8::MAX && q_tail < 128 {
                    dist[v as usize] = d + 1;
                    prev[v as usize] = u;
                    in_row[v as usize] = r_hit;
                    queue[q_tail] = v;
                    q_tail += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        let Some(end_k) = found_end else {
            break;
        };
        let mut path = [0u8; 8];
        let mut p_len = 0usize;
        let mut cur = end_k;
        while cur != 0 && p_len < 8 {
            path[p_len] = cur;
            p_len += 1;
            if in_start[cur as usize] {
                break;
            }
            cur = prev[cur as usize];
        }
        path[..p_len].reverse();
        if p_len < 2 {
            break;
        }

        let snap_b = *board;
        let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
        let mut out_wires = Vec::with_capacity(p_len);
        let mut ok = true;
        let mut fail_mid = path[1];

        for i in 0..(p_len - 1) {
            let (u, v) = (path[i], path[i + 1]);
            let (uc, uh) = (u & 63, (u >> 6) & 1);
            let (vc, vh) = (v & 63, (v >> 6) & 1);
            if uc == vc && uh != vh {
                if board.is_occupied(uc, 4) || board.is_occupied(uc, 5) || !v_seg_free(fps, uc, 4, 5) {
                    ok = false;
                    fail_mid = v;
                    break;
                }
                board.occupy(uc, 4);
                board.occupy(uc, 5);
                board.col_net[0][uc as usize] = tag;
                board.col_net[1][uc as usize] = tag;
                out_wires.push(FastWire::new_v(w_net, uc, 4, uc, 5));
            } else if uh == vh && uc != vc {
                let w_opt = route_h(board, fps, tag, uc, vc, uh, 0, rem_cross, row_hist, weights)
                    .or_else(|| try_row_swap_h_ext(board, pass_wires, fps, tag, uc, vc, uh, weights));
                if let Some(w) = w_opt {
                    board.col_net[uh as usize][uc as usize] = tag;
                    board.col_net[uh as usize][vc as usize] = tag;
                    out_wires.push(w);
                } else {
                    ok = false;
                    fail_mid = if i + 1 < p_len - 1 { v } else { u };
                    break;
                }
            } else {
                ok = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if ok {
            let first_k = path[0];
            for &k in &path[1..p_len] {
                uf.union(first_k, k);
            }
            return Some(out_wires);
        }
        *board = snap_b;
        pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
        let (fc, fh) = (fail_mid & 63, (fail_mid >> 6) & 1);
        forbidden_mid[fh as usize] |= 1u64 << fc;
    }
    None
}

/// Nối hai cụm chân thuộc cùng một mạng theo cây Steiner (`connectSteiner` trong main.html)
#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn connect_steiner(
    board: &mut BoardState,
    pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
    fps: &[Footprint],
    uf: &mut StripUf,
    tag: u16,
    a: (u8, i8),
    b: (u8, i8),
    max_cand: usize,
    star_hub: Option<(u8, u8)>,
    rem_cross: i32,
    col_hist: &[f32; 65],
    row_hist: &[f32; 10],
    weights: &CostWeights,
) -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
    let ka = strip_idx(a.0, half_of(a.1));
    let kb = strip_idx(b.0, half_of(b.1));
    if uf.find(ka) == uf.find(kb) {
        return Some(Vec::new());
    }
    if let Some((hc, hh)) = star_hub {
        let hub_k = strip_idx(hc, hh);
        if let Some(res) = connect_pair(
            board, pass_wires, fps, tag, hc, hh, b.0, half_of(b.1),
            rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights,
        ) {
            uf.union(hub_k, kb);
            return Some(res);
        }
        return bfs_escape_route(
            board, pass_wires, fps, uf, tag, (hc, if hh == 0 { 2 } else { 7 }), b,
            rem_cross, row_hist, weights,
        );
    }

    let (ha, hb) = (half_of(a.1), half_of(b.1));
    let is_cross_branch = ha != hb;
    if is_cross_branch {
        let mut top_roots = [0u128; 2];
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if board.col_net[0][c as usize] == tag {
                let r = uf.find(strip_idx(c, 0)) as usize;
                top_roots[r >> 7] |= 1u128 << (r & 127);
            }
        }
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if board.col_net[1][c as usize] == tag {
                let r = uf.find(strip_idx(c, 1)) as usize;
                if (top_roots[r >> 7] & (1u128 << (r & 127))) != 0 {
                    return Some(Vec::new());
                }
            }
        }
    }

    let try_connect_roots = |board: &mut BoardState,
                             pass_wires: &mut PassWires,
                             uf: &mut StripUf,
                             root_a: u8,
                             root_b: Option<u8>,
                             lim: usize,
                             exclude_root: Option<u8>|
     -> Option<Vec<FastWire>> {
        let mut comp_a = Vec::with_capacity(16);
        let mut comp_b = Vec::with_capacity(16);
        for h in [0u8, 1u8] {
            for c in 1u8..=63 {
                if board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize] != tag || !board.has_free_hole(c, h) {
                    continue;
                }
                let k = strip_idx(c, h);
                let rk = uf.find(k);
                if rk == root_a {
                    comp_a.push((c, h, k));
                } else if root_b.map_or(rk != root_a && Some(rk) != exclude_root, |rb| rk == rb) {
                    comp_b.push((c, h, k));
                } else if is_cross_branch && root_b.is_some() {
                    // Hạng mục #9: Mở khóa thu thập toàn bộ chân cùng mạng còn lỗ trống trên từng nửa bo khi is_cross_branch == true
                    if h == ha {
                        comp_a.push((c, h, k));
                    } else if h == hb {
                        comp_b.push((c, h, k));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        if comp_a.is_empty() || comp_b.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let mut pairs = Vec::with_capacity(comp_a.len() * comp_b.len());
        for &u in &comp_a {
            for &v in &comp_b {
                let same_col_bonus = if u.0 == v.0
                    && u.1 != v.1
                    && !board.is_occupied(u.0, 4)
                    && !board.is_occupied(u.0, 5)
                {
                    -45.0
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                let main_cluster_bonus = (if uf.find(u.2) == root_a { -2.0 } else { 0.0 })
                    + (if root_b.is_some_and(|rb| uf.find(v.2) == rb) { -2.0 } else { 0.0 });
                let dist = (u.0 as i32 - v.0 as i32).abs() as f64
                    + (if u.1 != v.1 { 42.0 } else { 0.0 })
                    + same_col_bonus
                    + main_cluster_bonus
                    + span_congestion(col_hist, u.0, v.0) * 2.2
                    - (if u.2 == ka { 0.5 } else { 0.0 })
                    - (if v.2 == kb { 0.5 } else { 0.0 });
                pairs.push((dist, u, v));
            }
        }
        pairs.sort_by(|x, y| x.0.total_cmp(&y.0));

        for &(_, u, v) in pairs.iter().take(lim) {
            let mut before_mask = [0u64; 2];
            for h in [0usize, 1usize] {
                for c in 1u8..=63 {
                    if board.col_net[h][c as usize] == tag {
                        before_mask[h] |= 1u64 << c;
                    }
                }
            }
            if let Some(res) = connect_pair(
                board, pass_wires, fps, tag, u.0, u.1, v.0, v.1,
                rem_cross, col_hist, row_hist, weights,
            ) {
                uf.union(u.2, v.2);
                for h in [0u8, 1u8] {
                    for c in 1u8..=63 {
                        if board.col_net[h as usize][c as usize] == tag {
                            let k = strip_idx(c, h);
                            if (before_mask[h as usize] & (1u64 << c)) == 0 || k == u.2 || k == v.2
                            {
                                uf.union(u.2, k);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                return Some(res);
            }
        }
        None
    };

    let (ra, rb) = (uf.find(ka), uf.find(kb));
    if let Some(res) = try_connect_roots(board, pass_wires, uf, ra, Some(rb), max_cand, None) {
        return Some(res);
    }

    for (start_k, end_k) in [(ka, kb), (kb, ka)] {
        let snap_b = *board;
        let snap_w = pass_wires.snapshot_swaps();
        let snap_uf = *uf;
        let r_start = uf.find(start_k);
        let r_end = uf.find(end_k);
        if let Some(mut step1) =
            try_connect_roots(board, pass_wires, uf, r_start, None, max_cand.min(18), Some(r_end))
        {
            if uf.find(ka) == uf.find(kb) {
                return Some(step1);
            }
            let (ra2, rb2) = (uf.find(ka), uf.find(kb));
            if let Some(step2) =
                try_connect_roots(board, pass_wires, uf, ra2, Some(rb2), max_cand, None)
            {
                if uf.find(ka) == uf.find(kb) {
                    step1.extend(step2);
                    return Some(step1);
                }
            }
        }
        *board = snap_b;
        pass_wires.restore_swaps(snap_w);
        *uf = snap_uf;
    }

    // Phao cứu sinh cuối cùng (#9): Dò đường mê cung đa nhịp trên 126 dải đồng
    bfs_escape_route(board, pass_wires, fps, uf, tag, a, b, rem_cross, row_hist, weights)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_bfs_escape_route_solves_blocked_maze_and_unlocks_cross_branch_pins() {
        let mut board = BoardState::new(false);
        let mut pass_wires = PassWires::new(true);
        let mut uf = StripUf::new();
        let fps = vec![Footprint::for_ic(8, 26)];
        let row_hist = [0.0f32; 10];
        let weights = CostWeights::default();

        // Mạng tag=5 có (10, 4) (đã hết lỗ trống ở cột 10 nửa trên) và (12, 4) (chỉ còn đúng 1 lỗ trống ở hàng 2)
        // Nhờ nâng cấp #9, (12, 0) được mở khóa làm điểm xuất phát vượt rãnh sang (25, 1) dù chỉ còn 1 lỗ trống!
        board.col_net[0][10] = 5;
        board.col_net[0][12] = 5;
        board.col_net[1][25] = 5;
        for r in 0..=4 {
            board.occupy(10, r);
            if r != 2 {
                board.occupy(12, r);
            }
        }
        // Khóa rãnh E-F dưới chân IC (8..=26) để cột 6 là cột cầu rãnh E-F duy nhất trên toàn mặt bo
        for c in 8u8..=26 {
            board.occupy(c, 4);
            board.occupy(c, 5);
        }
        for c in 1u8..=63 {
            if c != 6 && c != 12 && c != 20 && c != 25 {
                board.col_net[0][c as usize] = 99;
                board.col_net[1][c as usize] = 99;
            }
        }
        // Trên nửa dưới: cột 6 chỉ trống hàng 6, cột 25 chỉ trống hàng 7 -> ép đi qua trung gian cột 20:
        // Đường đi: (12,0) --hàng 2--> (6,0) --cầu rãnh E-F (6,4..5)--> (6,1) --hàng 6--> (20,1) --hàng 7--> (25,1)
        for r in [7i8, 8, 9] {
            board.occupy(6, r);
        }
        for r in [6i8, 8, 9] {
            board.occupy(25, r);
        }

        let col_hist = [0.0f32; 65];
        let wires = connect_steiner(
            &mut board, &mut pass_wires, &fps, &mut uf, 5, (10, 4), (25, 5),
            20, None, 1, &col_hist, &row_hist, &weights,
        )
        .expect("Should find multi-hop path via bridge col 6");

        assert!(wires.len() >= 3);
        assert!(wires.iter().any(|w| w.c1 == 6 && w.c2 == 6 && w.r1 == 4 && w.r2 == 5));
        assert_eq!(uf.find(strip_idx(12, 0)), uf.find(strip_idx(25, 1)));
    }
}

use bse_core::config::weights::CostWeights;
use bse_core::macro_place::topology::build_topology_cache;
use bse_core::meta_search::{
    build_board, BoardSolution, CandidateOpts, KnowledgeBank, MetaSolver, SteerMode,
};
use bse_core::model::circuit::{
    BoardLayout, CompSpec, PlacedBtn, PlacedIc, PlacedSpecial, PlacedSw, WireSeg,
};
use bse_core::model::ids::{CompId, CompType};
use bse_core::parser::parse_netlist;
use rustc_hash::FxHashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Deserializer, Serialize};
use wasm_bindgen::prelude::*;

fn de_opt_u8<'de, D>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Option<u8>, D::Error>
where
    D: Deserializer<'de>,
{
    #[derive(Deserialize)]
    #[serde(untagged)]
    enum RawPin {
        Num(u8),
        Str(String),
        Null,
    }
    match Option::<RawPin>::deserialize(deserializer)? {
        Some(RawPin::Num(n)) => Ok(Some(n)),
        Some(RawPin::Str(s)) => Ok(s.trim().parse::<u8>().ok()),
        Some(RawPin::Null) | None => Ok(None),
    }
}

const fn default_pins() -> u8 {
    2
}

#[inline]
pub fn comp_type_to_str(t: CompType) -> &'static str {
    match t {
        CompType::IC => "IC",
        CompType::R => "R",
        CompType::LED => "LED",
        CompType::C => "C",
        CompType::CC => "CC",
        CompType::SW => "SW",
        CompType::BTN2 => "BTN2",
    }
}

#[inline]
pub fn parse_comp_type(s: &str) -> CompType {
    match s.trim().to_ascii_uppercase().as_str() {
        "IC" => CompType::IC,
        "R" => CompType::R,
        "LED" => CompType::LED,
        "C" => CompType::C,
        "CC" => CompType::CC,
        "SW" => CompType::SW,
        "BTN2" | "BTN" => CompType::BTN2,
        _ => CompType::R,
    }
}

/// Dữ liệu linh kiện đầu vào từ mảng `components` của `main.html`
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsCompInput {
    pub id: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type", alias = "comp_type")]
    pub comp_type: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_pins")]
    pub pins: u8,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub rot: u8,
    #[serde(default, alias = "pwr_mode")]
    pub pwr_mode: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default, alias = "custom_pwr")]
    pub custom_pwr: Option<bool>,
    #[serde(default, alias = "vcc_pin", deserialize_with = "de_opt_u8")]
    pub vcc_pin: Option<u8>,
    #[serde(default, alias = "gnd_pin", deserialize_with = "de_opt_u8")]
    pub gnd_pin: Option<u8>,
}

impl JsCompInput {
    pub fn into_comp_spec(self, cid: CompId) -> CompSpec {
        let ct = parse_comp_type(&self.comp_type);
        let pins = match ct {
            CompType::IC => self.pins.max(8),
            CompType::SW => 3,
            _ => 2,
        };
        let is_custom = self.custom_pwr.unwrap_or_else(|| {
            self.pwr_mode
                .as_deref()
                .is_some_and(|m| m.eq_ignore_ascii_case("custom"))
        });
        let (vcc_pin, gnd_pin) = if ct == CompType::IC {
            if is_custom {
                (self.vcc_pin, self.gnd_pin)
            } else {
                (Some(pins), Some(pins / 2))
            }
        } else {
            (None, None)
        };
        CompSpec {
            cid,
            id: self.id.trim().to_ascii_uppercase(),
            comp_type: ct,
            pins,
            rot: u8::from(self.rot != 0),
            custom_pwr: is_custom,
            vcc_pin,
            gnd_pin,
        }
    }
}

pub fn parse_js_components(comps_json: &str) -> Vec<CompSpec> {
    if let Ok(js_list) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<JsCompInput>>(comps_json) {
        return js_list
            .into_iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, item)| item.into_comp_spec(CompId(i as u8)))
            .collect();
    }
    serde_json::from_str::<Vec<CompSpec>>(comps_json).unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Ghép cấu hình `LED Đối Xứng` từ giao diện vào chuỗi Netlist dưới dạng chỉ dẫn `#SYM_LED:` (#10)
pub fn merge_sym_led_directive(netlist_text: &str, sym_led_spec: &str) -> String {
    let spec = sym_led_spec.trim();
    if spec.is_empty() {
        return netlist_text.to_string();
    }
    let clean_spec = spec
        .strip_prefix("#SYM_LED:")
        .or_else(|| spec.strip_prefix("SYM_LED:"))
        .unwrap_or(spec)
        .trim();
    if clean_spec.is_empty() {
        return netlist_text.to_string();
    }
    format!("#SYM_LED: {clean_spec}\n{netlist_text}")
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsPlacedIc {
    pub id: String,
    pub pins: u8,
    pub rot: u8,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub w: u8,
}

impl From<&PlacedIc> for JsPlacedIc {
    fn from(ic: &PlacedIc) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: ic.id.clone(),
            pins: ic.pins,
            rot: ic.rot,
            c_min: ic.c_min,
            c_max: ic.c_max,
            w: ic.w,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsPlacedSw {
    pub id: String,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub row: i8,
    pub flip: u8,
}

impl From<&PlacedSw> for JsPlacedSw {
    fn from(sw: &PlacedSw) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: sw.id.clone(),
            c_min: sw.c_min,
            c_max: sw.c_max,
            row: sw.row,
            flip: sw.flip,
        }
    }
}

/// Nút bấm 2 chân xuất ra JSON cho `BreadboardRenderer` (Bao gồm trường `bend`: -1, 0, +1) (#2)
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsPlacedBtn {
    pub id: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub comp_type: String,
    pub c_min: u8,
    pub c_max: u8,
    pub row_top: i8,
    pub row_bot: i8,
    pub flip: u8,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub bend: i8,
}

impl From<&PlacedBtn> for JsPlacedBtn {
    fn from(b: &PlacedBtn) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: b.id.clone(),
            comp_type: comp_type_to_str(b.comp_type).to_string(),
            c_min: b.c_min,
            c_max: b.c_max,
            row_top: b.row_top,
            row_bot: b.row_bot,
            flip: b.flip,
            bend: b.bend,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsPlacedSpecial {
    pub id: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub comp_type: String,
    pub c1: u8,
    pub r1: i8,
    pub c2: u8,
    pub r2: i8,
    pub locked: bool,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub ic_id: Option<String>,
}

impl JsPlacedSpecial {
    pub fn from_core(sp: &PlacedSpecial, comps: &[CompSpec]) -> Self {
        let ic_id = sp.ic_id.and_then(|cid| {
            comps
                .iter()
                .find(|c| c.cid == cid)
                .map(|c| c.id.clone())
        });
        Self {
            id: sp.id.clone(),
            comp_type: comp_type_to_str(sp.comp_type).to_string(),
            c1: sp.c1,
            r1: sp.r1,
            c2: sp.c2,
            r2: sp.r2,
            locked: sp.locked,
            ic_id,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct JsBoardLayout {
    pub ics: Vec<JsPlacedIc>,
    pub sws: Vec<JsPlacedSw>,
    pub btns: Vec<JsPlacedBtn>,
    pub rs: Vec<JsPlacedSpecial>,
    pub leds: Vec<JsPlacedSpecial>,
    pub cs: Vec<JsPlacedSpecial>,
}

impl JsBoardLayout {
    pub fn from_core(layout: &BoardLayout, comps: &[CompSpec]) -> Self {
        Self {
            ics: layout.ics.iter().map(JsPlacedIc::from).collect(),
            sws: layout.sws.iter().map(JsPlacedSw::from).collect(),
            btns: layout.btns.iter().map(JsPlacedBtn::from).collect(),
            rs: layout
                .rs
                .iter()
                .map(|x| JsPlacedSpecial::from_core(x, comps))
                .collect(),
            leds: layout
                .leds
                .iter()
                .map(|x| JsPlacedSpecial::from_core(x, comps))
                .collect(),
            cs: layout
                .cs
                .iter()
                .map(|x| JsPlacedSpecial::from_core(x, comps))
                .collect(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct JsWireSeg {
    pub net: String,
    pub cells: [[i8; 2]; 2],
    pub lane: i8,
}

impl From<&WireSeg> for JsWireSeg {
    fn from(w: &WireSeg) -> Self {
        Self {
            net: w.net_name.clone(),
            cells: w.cells,
            lane: w.lane,
        }
    }
}

/// Phương án hoàn chỉnh xuất ra JSON cho `main.html`
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct JsBoardSolution {
    #[serde(rename = "L")]
    pub layout: JsBoardLayout,
    pub wires: Vec<JsWireSeg>,
    pub errors: Vec<String>,
    pub warns: Vec<String>,
    pub comp_nets: FxHashMap<String, Vec<String>>,
    pub nets: usize,
    pub len: i32,
    pub max_c: u8,
    pub score: f64,
    pub unplaced: usize,
    pub ortho_count: usize,
    pub persona: String,
    pub sig: u64,
    pub layout_hash: u64,
    pub opts: CandidateOpts,
}

impl JsBoardSolution {
    pub fn from_core(sol: &BoardSolution, comps: &[CompSpec]) -> Self {
        Self {
            layout: JsBoardLayout::from_core(&sol.layout, comps),
            wires: sol.wires.iter().map(JsWireSeg::from).collect(),
            errors: sol.errors.clone(),
            warns: sol.warns.clone(),
            comp_nets: sol.comp_nets.clone(),
            nets: sol.nets,
            len: sol.len,
            max_c: sol.max_c,
            score: sol.score,
            unplaced: sol.unplaced,
            ortho_count: sol.ortho_count,
            persona: sol.persona.clone(),
            sig: sol.sig,
            layout_hash: sol.layout_hash,
            opts: sol.opts.clone(),
        }
    }
}

/// Bộ giải WASM điều phối Đa Quần Đảo & Vi Phẫu kết nối trực tiếp với giao diện `main.html`
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub struct WasmSolver {
    solver: MetaSolver,
    raw_netlist: String,
}

#[wasm_bindgen]
impl WasmSolver {
    #[wasm_bindgen(constructor)]
    pub fn new(
        comps_json: &str,
        netlist_text: &str,
        use_star_hub: bool,
        seed: u32,
        kb_json: Option<String>,
    ) -> WasmSolver {
        let comps = parse_js_components(comps_json);
        let kb = kb_json
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(|s| serde_json::from_str::<KnowledgeBank>(s).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let solver = MetaSolver::new(comps, netlist_text, use_star_hub, seed, kb);
        WasmSolver {
            solver,
            raw_netlist: netlist_text.to_string(),
        }
    }

    /// Khởi tạo bộ giải kèm cấu hình `LED Đối Xứng` từ giao diện (`sym_led_spec`, VD: "L1=L2, L3=L4")
    pub fn new_with_sym_led(
        comps_json: &str,
        netlist_text: &str,
        sym_led_spec: &str,
        use_star_hub: bool,
        seed: u32,
        kb_json: Option<String>,
    ) -> WasmSolver {
        let merged = merge_sym_led_directive(netlist_text, sym_led_spec);
        let mut ws = Self::new(comps_json, &merged, use_star_hub, seed, kb_json);
        ws.raw_netlist = netlist_text.to_string();
        ws
    }

    /// Cập nhật nóng cấu hình `LED Đối Xứng` từ ô Checkbox + Input trên giao diện (#10)
    pub fn set_sym_led(&mut self, sym_led_spec: &str) {
        let merged = merge_sym_led_directive(&self.raw_netlist, sym_led_spec);
        let updated = parse_netlist(&merged, &self.solver.comps);
        self.solver.parsed.sym_led_groups = updated.sym_led_groups;
    }

    pub fn step_batch(&mut self, batch_size: usize, elapsed_ms: f64) -> String {
        let res = self.solver.step_batch(batch_size, elapsed_ms.max(0.0) as u64);
        serde_json::to_string(&res).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
    }

    pub fn next_candidate_json(&mut self) -> String {
        let cand = self.solver.next_candidate();
        serde_json::to_string(&cand).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
    }

    pub fn ingest_result_json(&mut self, sol_json: &str, elapsed_ms: f64) -> bool {
        let Ok(sol) = serde_json::from_str::<BoardSolution>(sol_json) else {
            return false;
        };
        self.solver.ingest_result(sol, elapsed_ms.max(0.0) as u64)
    }

    pub fn get_top_solutions(&self, k: usize) -> String {
        let top = self.solver.pick_top(k);
        let js_top: Vec<JsBoardSolution> = top
            .iter()
            .map(|s| JsBoardSolution::from_core(s, &self.solver.comps))
            .collect();
        serde_json::to_string(&js_top).unwrap_or_else(|_| "[]".to_string())
    }

    pub fn pick_top_json(&self, k: usize) -> String {
        self.get_top_solutions(k)
    }

    pub fn get_log_report(&self) -> String {
        self.solver.logger.build_text_report()
    }

    pub fn get_log_json(&self) -> String {
        serde_json::to_string(&self.solver.logger).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
    }

    pub fn get_kb_json(&mut self) -> String {
        self.solver.kb.harvest_pool(&self.solver.pool);
        serde_json::to_string(&self.solver.kb).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
    }

    pub fn set_steer_mode(&mut self, mode_str: &str) {
        let mode = match mode_str.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "macro" => SteerMode::Macro,
            "bridge" => SteerMode::Bridge,
            "surgery" => SteerMode::Surgery,
            "lock_io" | "lockio" => SteerMode::LockIo,
            _ => SteerMode::Auto,
        };
        self.solver.set_steer_mode(mode);
    }

    pub fn trigger_tabu_jump(&mut self, tab_idx: usize) {
        self.solver.trigger_tabu_jump(tab_idx);
    }

    pub fn trigger_deep_surgery(&mut self, tab_idx: usize) {
        self.solver.trigger_deep_surgery(tab_idx);
    }
}

/// Hàm WASM độc lập đánh giá 1 cá thể `CandidateOpts` và trả về `BoardSolution` dạng JSON (dùng cho Worker)
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn wasm_eval_candidate(
    comps_json: &str,
    netlist_text: &str,
    cand_opts_json: &str,
) -> String {
    let comps = parse_js_components(comps_json);
    let parsed = parse_netlist(netlist_text, &comps);
    let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
    let weights = CostWeights::default();
    let mut opts = serde_json::from_str::<CandidateOpts>(cand_opts_json).unwrap_or_default();
    let sol = build_board(&mut opts, &comps, &parsed, &topo, &weights);
    serde_json::to_string(&sol).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
}

/// Hàm WASM độc lập dựng 1 phương án hoàn chỉnh và xuất trực tiếp `JsBoardSolution` cho giao diện
#[wasm_bindgen]
pub fn wasm_build_board_js(
    comps_json: &str,
    netlist_text: &str,
    sym_led_spec: &str,
    use_star_hub: bool,
) -> String {
    let comps = parse_js_components(comps_json);
    let merged = merge_sym_led_directive(netlist_text, sym_led_spec);
    let parsed = parse_netlist(&merged, &comps);
    let topo = build_topology_cache(&parsed, &comps);
    let weights = CostWeights::default();
    let mut opts = CandidateOpts {
        island: 2,
        persona: 2,
        sr: Some(1),
        use_star_hub,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    let sol = build_board(&mut opts, &comps, &parsed, &topo, &weights);
    let js_sol = JsBoardSolution::from_core(&sol, &comps);
    serde_json::to_string(&js_sol).unwrap_or_else(|_| "{}".to_string())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_js_placed_btn_bend_and_sym_led_wasm_integration() {
        let comps_json = r#"[
            {"id":"U1","type":"IC","pins":14,"rot":0,"pwrMode":"default","vccPin":14,"gndPin":7},
            {"id":"B1","type":"BTN2","pins":2},
            {"id":"R1","type":"R","pins":2},
            {"id":"R2","type":"R","pins":2},
            {"id":"L1","type":"LED","pins":2},
            {"id":"L2","type":"LED","pins":2}
        ]"#;
        let netlist = "
            VCC U1:14; GND U1:7
            U1:12 -> R1 -> L1 -> GND
            U1:3 -> R2 -> L2 -> GND
            B1:1 -> R1:1; B1:2 -> GND
        ";

        let mut solver = WasmSolver::new_with_sym_led(comps_json, netlist, "L1=L2", true, 42, None);
        assert_eq!(
            solver.solver.parsed.sym_led_groups,
            vec![vec![CompId(4), CompId(5)]]
        );

        // Kiểm tra cập nhật nóng cấu hình LED Đối Xứng từ giao diện
        solver.set_sym_led("L1:L2");
        assert_eq!(
            solver.solver.parsed.sym_led_groups,
            vec![vec![CompId(4), CompId(5)]]
        );

        let step_json = solver.step_batch(2, 15.0);
        assert!(step_json.contains("\"iterations\":2"));

        let top_json = solver.get_top_solutions(1);
        assert!(top_json.contains("\"bend\":"));
        let top_sols: Vec<JsBoardSolution> = serde_json::from_str(&top_json).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(top_sols.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(top_sols[0].layout.btns.len(), 1);
        assert!((-1..=1).contains(&top_sols[0].layout.btns[0].bend));
    }
}
